MMPM consists of two modules: behavior-aware Pedestrian Interaction Module (PIM) that jointly captures pedestrian-vehicle and pedestrian-environment interactions by introducing gaze, head and hand gesture, and a CVAE-based Mode-aware Trajectory Predictor (MTP) module to model the future trajectory distributions on two modes, crossing and non-crossing the road, separately. A query-based decoder further enforces mode consistency during decoding. Experiments on PIE and JAAD datasets show that our method surpasses state-of-the-art baselines. Our proposed MTP is model-agnostic, which can be integrated into existing frameworks such as BiTrap-NP and SGNet-ED to further improve future trajectory prediction performance. We additionally introduce a data-driven validation protocol that matches predictions to spatio-temporally consistent ground-truth trajectories, demonstrating improved frame-wise displacement errors over previous work.

</details>


### [72] [DreamReg: Belief-Driven World Model for 2D-3D Ultrasound Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18825)
*Luoyao Kang,Yuelin Zhang,Jiwei Shan,Haifan Gong,Qingpeng Ding,Shing Shin Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamReg提出了一种基于信念驱动的世界模型框架，将术中2D超声切片与术前3D体积的实时配准问题建模为刚体变换的信念更新，通过隐信念状态总结历史观测与探头运动信息，并在训练中模拟临床扫描行为，推理时通过内部想象（世界模型推演候选探头运动并预测观测结果）实现连续精准配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D-3D超声配准方法多为单次或短时域方法，难以随时间累积证据，也无法捕捉医生根据实时反馈调整探头运动的动态行为，导致在部分观测、散斑噪声和动作依赖的超声采集场景下配准鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出DreamReg框架：1）构建隐信念状态，整合历史观测与位姿信息；2）使用学习到的动力学模型在新切片到达时连续更新配准变换；3）训练时暴露于模拟临床扫描行为的探头运动轨迹；4）推理时通过内部想象——展开世界模型模拟候选探头运动及其预测观测——来优化刚体变换。

Result: 在CAMUS和u-RegPro数据集上，DreamReg相比现有最先进方法在实时引导中展现出更强的鲁棒性和具有竞争力的配准精度。

Conclusion: 基于信念驱动的世界模型能够有效利用多帧时序信息，通过内部想象机制实现积累证据和动态调整，为解决2D-3D超声配准的实时性与鲁棒性矛盾提供了新范式。

Abstract: Ultrasound (US) is widely used for surgical navigation, yet real-time registration between intraoperative 2D slices and preoperative 3D volumes remains challenging due to partial observability, speckle noise, and the action-dependent US acquisition. Existing methods are one-shot or short-horizon, making it hard for them to gather evidence over time or capture how surgeons adjust probe motion based on on-screen feedback. We propose DreamReg, a belief-driven world-model framework that formulates 2D-3D registration as belief updating over rigid transformations. DreamReg maintains a latent belief state that summarizes past observations and poses information, and continuously refines the transformation through learned dynamics as new slices arrive. During training, DreamReg is exposed to probe-motion trajectories that mimic clinical scanning behavior and learns to update its belief by conditioning pose refinement on the current US observation. During inference, DreamReg refines registration via internal imagination: it rolls out the learned world model to simulate candidate probe motions and their predicted observations, and integrates these imagined outcomes to converge to an accurate rigid transformation. Experiments on CAMUS and u-RegPro datasets demonstrate improved robustness and competitive registration accuracy for real-time guidance compared with state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [73] [Rethinking Air-Ground Collaboration: A Progressive Cross-Task Benchmark and Socialized Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18841)
*Zhoupeng Guo,Yunqi Zhu,Zhihe Fan,Xinjie Yao,Ruipu Zhao,Boan Tao,Yiming Sun,Zhen Wang,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种空地渐进式协作感知框架（SCP），通过双层级路由模块（DLR）实现从全局定位到目标关联再到身份解析的渐进式跨任务协作，在包含74.5万帧的AGPC基准上取得3.73%的协同进化增益和7.86%的下游性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空地协同感知研究通常将协作建模为单任务跨视角融合，忽略了定位、目标关联和细粒度解析之间的功能依赖关系；同时空地视角的异构性（几何、尺度、遮挡差异）导致统一特征共享易受负迁移影响。

Method: 构建了时空对齐的AGPC基准（74.5万+原始视频帧），提出SCP粗到细框架：核心模块DLR通过输入侧多尺度专家选择与输出侧任务条件调制解耦，实现选择性跨视角和跨任务交互，抑制有害干扰。

Result: SCP实现了3.73%的协同进化增益和7.86%的平均下游性能提升，验证了任务条件化协作优于统一融合。

Conclusion: 任务条件化协作比统一融合更适用于异构空地感知场景，代码已开源。

Abstract: Air-ground collaborative perception is crucial for robust visual understanding in real-world dynamic environments. However, existing studies typically formulate collaboration as single-task cross-view fusion, overlooking the functional dependencies among localization, target association, and fine-grained parsing. In addition, the heterogeneous nature of aerial and ground views introduces substantial geometric, scale, and occlusion discrepancies, making uniform feature sharing vulnerable to negative transfer. To tackle these issues, we model air-ground perception as a progressive cross-task collaboration task and construct the Air-Ground Progressive Collaboration (AGPC) benchmark, a spatio-temporally aligned benchmark comprising more than 745K raw video frames. Built upon this benchmark, we propose Socialized Co-Perception (SCP), a coarse-to-fine framework that organizes collaboration progressively from aerial global localization to ground target association and identity-aware parsing. Its core module, the Dual-Layer Router (DLR), decouples input-side multi-scale expert selection from output-side task-conditioned modulation, enabling selective cross-view and cross-task interaction while suppressing harmful interference. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of SCP. It achieves a 3.73\% coevolutionary gain and a 7.86\% improvement in average downstream performance. These results show that task-conditioned collaboration is more effective than uniform fusion for heterogeneous air-ground perception. The code is available at https://github.com/g1136639260-spec/AGSCP.

</details>


### [74] [From Bounding Boxes to Visual Reasoning: An On-Policy Data Annotation Tool for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18846)
*Like Zhang,Runliang Niu,Shiqi Wang,Xiyu Hu,Qianli Xing,Pan Wang,Qingzu He,Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScreenAnnotator是一个开源标注工具，通过统一原子架构、策略内标注循环（含贝叶斯验证器）和模板驱动多任务数据合成，解决了视觉语言模型在结构化视觉推理训练中数据标注的三大瓶颈（表达受限、标注-训练脱耦、数据复用性差），在流程图和GUI截图标注上实现了近100%和77%的接受率，微调VLM后准确率提升35.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据标注工具无法满足VLMs对空间坐标、开放词汇描述、结构属性和拓扑关系统一表示的需求，存在表达能力有限、标注与训练严重脱耦、数据复用性差三大系统瓶颈，亟需一种新的基础设施来弥合这一差距。

Method: 1) 定义统一标注原子架构，将空间、语义和结构基元绑定为单一单元；2) 实现嵌入贝叶斯标注验证器(BAV)的策略内标注循环；3) 设计模板驱动的多任务数据合成过程，动态将静态原子转换为多样化多维推理任务，消除冗余重新标注。

Result: 策略内循环驱动流程图标注接受率接近100%，GUI截图标注接受率达77%，同时随着标注数据积累，单张图像标注时间稳步减少。在流程图场景中，微调VLM后平均准确率达76.1%，绝对值提升35.1个百分点。

Conclusion: ScreenAnnotator有效解决了VLM训练数据标注的基础设施瓶颈，通过创新的原子化表示和策略内验证机制，显著提升了标注效率、质量和数据复用性，为复杂结构化视觉推理任务提供了实用的开源工具。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are rapidly advancing toward sophisticated grounded structured visual reasoning. Training models for such advanced capabilities demands a new genre of data that seamlessly unifies spatial coordinates, open-vocabulary descriptions, structured attributes, and topological relationships into a singular representation. However, existing data annotation tools fundamentally fail to meet these intricate demands, suffering from three systematic bottlenecks: limited expressiveness, severe annotation-training decoupling, and poor data reusability. To bridge this infrastructure gap, we introduce an open-source annotation tool, ScreenAnnotator. First, we define a unified annotation atom schema that binds spatial, semantic, and structural primitives into a single unit. Second, we implement an on-policy annotation loop embedded with a Bayesian Annotation Verifier (BAV). Finally, we design a template-driven multi-task data synthesis process dynamically transforms static atoms into diverse multi-dimensional reasoning tasks, eliminating redundant re-annotation. The on-policy loop drives the annotation accept rate to nearly 100% on flowcharts and 77% on GUI screenshots, while steadily reducing per-image annotation time as labeled data accumulate. In the flowchart scenario, fine-tuning a VLM yields 76.1% average accuracy, which is a 35.1% point absolute gain. Our code is available at: https://github.com/WnQinm/Annotator.

</details>


### [75] [Quantification of Uncertainty with Adversarial Models in Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18860)
*Hana Jebril,Thomas Pinetz,Günter Klambauer,Hrvoje Bogunović*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QUAM-SM，一种基于对抗搜索的后验框架，用于量化医学图像分割中像素级的不确定性，能有效区分认知不确定性和偶然不确定性，在边界敏感性和可靠性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准深度学习模型在校准上存在缺陷，在病理边界等关键区域产生过度自信的预测，掩盖了模型脆弱性，无法满足临床治疗规划和手术干预对高可靠性不确定性量化的需求。

Method: 提出QUAM-SM，一种后验框架，通过定向对抗搜索识别"对抗脆弱"像素，主动寻找扰动以暴露预测不稳定性，并进一步将认知不确定性（epistemic uncertainty）与偶然不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty）解耦。

Result: 在两个包含多位专家标注的公开数据集上，QUAM-SM在可靠性和边界敏感性方面均优于标准不确定性估计方法和近期工作。

Conclusion: QUAM-SM通过对抗搜索实现了可靠的像素级不确定性量化，能够区分真正病理变化与伪影，具有在临床纵向监测和治疗规划中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Reliable pixel-level uncertainty quantification holds the potential to transform clinical workflows by enabling high-fidelity longitudinal monitoring and distinguishing true pathological changes from artifacts. Ideally, these models provide the stability required for critical treatment planning and surgical intervention. However, standard deep learning models often suffer from miscalibration, yielding overconfident predictions that mask underlying vulnerabilities at subtle pathological boundaries. To address this, we propose QUAM-SM, a post-hoc framework using targeted adversarial search to identify "adversarially fragile" pixels. By actively seeking perturbations that expose predictive instability, our method highlights regions where decisions are most vulnerable to being flipped. Importantly, the framework disentangles epistemic uncertainty from aleatoric uncertainty. Experiments on two public datasets with multiple expert annotations demonstrate that QUAM-SM outperforms both standard and recent uncertainty estimation approaches in terms of reliability and boundary sensitivity. Code is available at https://github.com/HanaJebril/quam_sm

</details>


### [76] [URDF Synthesis from RGB-D Sequences via Differentiable Joint Inference and Energy-Consistent Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18861)
*Xinze Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KinemaForge 是一个约束驱动的管道，能从短 RGB-D 序列中联合推断关节物体的部件级形状、关节拓扑和关节参数，并通过基于可微刚体动力学的能量一致性验证器确保仿真稳定性，在多项指标上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两大空白：部件级几何重建与运动学参数估计相互解耦，且重建模型常违反能量守恒等动态不变量，导致在物理仿真器中回放 URDF 时出现漂移。

Method: 提出三组件管道：运动学约束图（软边编码关节-部件关系）、可微螺旋轴求解器（通过 Featherstone 算法反向传播到关节参数）、能量残差损失（惩罚非物理的自由响应）。

Result: 在 PartNet-Mobility 五个类别和内部 RGB-D 基准上，关节轴误差从 4.52° 降至 2.83°（-37.4% vs PARIS，-46.6% vs Ditto），长时仿真漂移降低 64%，闭环操控成功率提升 14.6 个百分点。

Conclusion: KinemaForge 通过联合推理与物理约束验证，有效弥合了几何重建与物理仿真之间的鸿沟，生成的 URDF 更稳定、更适用于闭环操控任务。

Abstract: Reconstructing simulation-ready digital twins of articulated objects from sensor observations remains constrained by two persistent gaps: (i) part-level geometric reconstruction is decoupled from kinematic-parameter estimation, and (ii) the recovered models often violate basic dynamic invariants such as energy conservation, leading to drift when the URDF is replayed in physics simulators. We present KinemaForge, a constraint-driven pipeline that jointly infers part-level shape, joint topology, and joint parameters from short RGB-D sequences and validates the result against an energy-consistent verifier built on differentiable rigid-body dynamics. The pipeline introduces three components: a kinematic constraint graph that encodes joint-part incidences as soft edges; a differentiable screw-axis solver that backpropagates from rendered observations through Featherstone's articulated-body algorithm to joint parameters; and an energy residual loss that penalises non-physical free responses of the reconstructed model. Across five PartNet-Mobility categories and an internal RGB-D benchmark, KinemaForge reduces the average joint-axis error from 4.52 degrees to 2.83 degrees (-37.4%) over the strongest geometric baseline (PARIS) and from 5.30 degrees to 2.83 degrees (-46.6%) over the interaction-based Ditto baseline, lowers long-horizon simulation drift by 64% (vs. PARIS) over 50 s rollouts, and yields URDFs whose closed-loop manipulation success rate improves by 14.6 percentage points over Ditto in our preliminary evaluation. Code and reconstruction data will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [77] [Learning to Distort: Weakly-Supervised Image Quality Transfer for Prostate DWI Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18869)
*YuCheng Tang,Wen Yan,Alexander Ng,Natasha Thorley,Pawel Rajwa,Yipei Wang,Aqua Asif,Clare Allen,Louise Dickinson,Francesco Giganti,David Atkinson,Shonit Punwani,Daniel Alexander,Shaheer Ullah Saeed,Veeru Kasivisvanathan,Yipeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种弱监督图像质量迁移框架，利用图像级质量标签（扭曲vs.未扭曲）和原型流匹配算法，从未扭曲图像生成真实感扭曲图像，进而训练前向扭曲校正模型，有效解决了前列腺DWI中几何畸变校正缺乏配对数据的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次激发平面回波前列腺弥散加权成像常因几何畸变影响诊断可靠性，而自动化校正方法面临缺乏成对扭曲/未扭曲临床扫描数据的挑战。

Method: 提出弱监督图像质量迁移框架，利用图像级质量标签建立潜在质量原型，通过原型流匹配算法正则化生成轨迹朝向扭曲原型，模拟真实感伪影；再训练前向IQT模型用于畸变校正。

Result: 生成图像成功模拟了真实伪影的诊断干扰，在校正任务中优于CycleGAN、UNIT-DDPM、OT-FM等现有非配对方法，在PI-RADS和Gleason评分分类的下游任务中表现更优。

Conclusion: 弱监督模拟真实畸变比直接非配对校正更可靠，所提方法能有效提升畸变校正模型的性能。

Abstract: Single-shot echo-planar prostate diffusion-weighted imaging (DWI) is frequently complicated by geometric distortions, which impact the ability to derive reliable diagnoses from such images. Developing automated correction methods is challenged by the absence of paired distorted and undistorted clinical scans. In this paper, we first propose a novel weakly-supervised image quality transfer (IQT) framework from undistorted to distorted images that utilizes image quality assessment (IQA) signals to supervise the transfer process. Unlike traditional methods that require expensive, voxel-wise paired data or resort to developing unpaired algorithms, our approach utilizes image-level quality labels (here, distorted vs. undistorted) to establish latent quality prototypes within a pre-trained feature space. Recognizing that simulating realistic distortions is more reliable than direct unpaired correction, we describe a weakly-supervised prototype flow matching algorithm to explicitly regularize generative trajectories towards distorted prototypes, producing realistic susceptibility artifacts that mimic clinical degradations. By synthesizing these realistic pairs, we enable a second IQT model to be trained in the forward direction for distortion correction. Experimental results demonstrate that our generated images successfully mimic the diagnostic interference of real-world artifacts, which leads to more capable distortion correction IQT models. In addition to qualitative comparisons, we also conduct exhaustive quantitative evaluations that compare our approach with existing unpaired approaches (e.g., CycleGAN, UNIT-DDPM, and OT-FM) - as either forward or reverse alternatives - by assessing clinical downstream task performance in PI-RADS and Gleason score classification, using both in-distribution and external data sets.

</details>


### [78] [Bridging Single Distortion Artifacts and Mmultifactorial Clinical Quality: Few-shot Biparametric MRI Quality Assessment via Distortion-trained Prototypical Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18872)
*Yuheng Tang,Alexander Ng,Wen Yan,Natasha Thorley,Pawel Rajwa,Yipei Wang,Aqua Asif,Clare Allen,Louise Dickinson,Francesco Giganti,Shonit Punwani,Daniel Alexander,Veeru Kasivisvanathan,Yipeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于少样本双参数原型网络的自动图像质量评估方法，用于解决前列腺多参数MRI中DWI图像因直肠气体等导致的几何失真问题。该方法利用双分支3D ResNet融合T2加权与DWI特征，引入FiLM和GRL对齐不同b值下的特征分布并抑制采集偏差，仅需5个代表性样本即可高效预测PI-QUAL评分，显著超越现有少样本基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床前列腺多参数MRI中DWI常因直肠气体产生几何失真，PI-QUAL评分作为新兴临床标准存在主观性强、耗时长、类别不平衡（低质量样本稀少且多样）等问题，亟需自动化的图像质量评估方法解决临床数据双稀缺（标注样本少、低质量样本类别不平衡）的挑战。

Method: 提出少样本双参数原型网络：1) 双分支3D ResNet融合T2加权与DWI特征，提供解剖上下文以区分真实形态与失真；2) 引入特征级线性调制(FiLM)和梯度反转层(GRL)，对齐不同b值下的特征分布并抑制采集相关偏差；3) 在客观易获取的失真标签上元训练模型，仅需5个代表性样本即可适应预测多因素临床质量评分PI-QUAL。

Result: 在两个数据集上的实验表明，所提方法显著优于少样本学习基线方法，为该具有挑战性的IQA任务提供了数据高效的解决方案。

Conclusion: 该方法为临床工作流中前列腺MRI质量控制的标准化提供了一种实用可行、数据高效的自动化方案。

Abstract: Clinical prostate multi-parametric MRI relies heavily on high-quality diffusion-weighted imaging (DWI), yet reading DWI is frequently compromised by geometric distortion, often caused by rectal air. Assessing quality via the PI-QUAL scoring system is an emerging clinical standard, but it is subjective, time-consuming and suffers from a class imbalance where low-quality cases are diverse and relatively scarce. Using the PRIME clinical trial as an example, there are $6\%$ images with PI-QUAL scores lower than 4, $87\%$ of DWI issues are due to distortion. Many of the other clinical quality issues are under-represented. To address this common dual-scarcity of annotated clinical data, we propose a few-shot biparametric prototypical network for automated image quality assessment (IQA). Our framework utilizes a dual-branch 3D ResNet to fuse T2-weighted and DWI features, providing anatomical context to distinguish true morphology from distortion. To handle real-world heterogeneity, we introduce feature-wise linear modulation (FiLM) and a gradient reversal layer (GRL) to align feature distributions conditioned on varying b-values while suppressing acquisition-related biases. We demonstrate that a model meta-trained solely on comparatively objective, readily obtainable distortion labels can effectively adapt to predicting complex, multi-factorial clinical quality scores such as PI-QUAL using only five representative samples. Experimental results on two datasets show that our method significantly outperforms few-shot learning baselines for this challenging IQA task, offering a practically feasible and data-efficient solution for standardizing prostate MRI quality control in clinical workflows.

</details>


### [79] [Test-Time Adaptation in Optical Coherence Tomography Using Trajectory-Aligned Time-Independent Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18876)
*Veit Hucke,Thomas Pinetz,Gregor Reiter,Ursula Schmidt-Erfurth,Hrvoje Bogunović*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于流匹配的测试时自适应方法，通过直方图匹配和移除时间条件，将低质量OCT图像转换为高质量代理图像，在AMD生物标志物分割中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCT在眼科中至关重要，但低成本设备产生的图像质量不一致，阻碍了自动化分析。现有方法在测试数据和训练数据之间存在域差距，导致去噪过程中像素分布不匹配。

Method: 1) 基于流匹配的测试时自适应，从噪声输入生成高质量代理图像；2) 通过将测试图像的直方图匹配到合成参考轨迹，使输入与期望分布对齐；3) 移除网络的时间条件以处理真实噪声分布的轻微偏差。

Result: 在年龄相关性黄斑变性（AMD）两个阶段的生物标志物分割任务中取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了低质量OCT图像的域差距问题，提升了自动化分析的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Optical coherence tomography (OCT) is essential in ophthalmology, but inconsistent image quality especially in low-cost devices hinders automated analysis. To address this, we introduce a flow-matching-based test-time adaptation method that generates high-quality surrogate images from noisy inputs. Typically, domain gaps between test and training data cause pixel distribution mismatches during the denoising process. We overcome this by matching the test image's histogram to synthetic reference trajectories, successfully aligning the input with expected distributions. Additionally, we remove the network's time conditioning to account for slight deviations in real-world noise distributions. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance in segmenting critical biomarkers for two stages of Age-related Macular Degeneration (AMD). Code is available: https://github.com/Veit21/tta-flow.

</details>


### [80] [Performance Gap Analysis between Latin and Arabic Scripts HTR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18884)
*Sana Al-azzawi,Elisa Barney,Marcus Liwicki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对阿拉伯语和拉丁语手写文本识别（HTR）进行了系统比较，使用统一CRNN模型在9个数据集上实验，发现阿拉伯语识别性能始终低于拉丁语5-7个CER点，原因包括：标注质量差、视觉变异性高导致数据覆盖效率低、字符分布更重尾、以及视觉相似字符混淆率更高（约30% vs 15%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明阿拉伯语手写识别性能低于拉丁语，但缺乏控制变量的系统对比研究，其根本原因尚未被充分理解。

Method: 使用统一的CRNN模型在9个数据集（涵盖阿拉伯语、英语、德语、乌尔都语、波斯语）上进行行级HTR实验，设置不同训练规模（K∈{100,500,1000,2000,...,Kfull}），并进行标注质量清理、视觉变异性分析、字符频率分布对比和错误类型分析。

Result: 阿拉伯语与拉丁语HTR性能差距持续存在：低资源设置下差距较大，全量数据下仍有5-7 CER点的差距。标注清理可缩小差距但无法消除。固定训练样本量下阿拉伯语因视觉变异性高导致覆盖效率低。阿拉伯语字符分布更重尾。阿拉伯语数据集中约30%的替换错误源于视觉相似字符混淆，而拉丁语仅约15%。

Conclusion: 阿拉伯语HTR性能差距是多种因素共同作用的结果，包括数据标注质量、视觉变异性、字符分布特性以及字符混淆模式等，弥合这一差距需要在这些方面进行针对性改进。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that handwritten text recognition (HTR) systems perform worse on Arabic-script datasets than on Latin-script data. However, the reasons for this gap are still not well understood due to the lack of controlled comparisons. In this work, we present a comprehensive study of Arabic and Latin scripts HTR using a unified CRNN model for line-level HTR across nine datasets (including KHATT (Arabic), Muharaf (Arabic), NUST-UHWR (Urdu), PHTD (Persian), IAM (English), READ-2016 (German), and others) and di ferent training sizes (K in {100, 500, 1000, 2000, ..., Kfull}). Our results show the performance gap remains: it is large in low-resource settings, decreases with more data, but remains even at full scale, with a consistent difference of 5-7 CER points. We show that annotation quality matters, as many datasets contain labeling errors. Cleaning reduces error rates and narrows the gap, but does not eliminate it. In addition, we find that a fixed number of training samples provides less effective coverage in Arabic due to higher visual variability, requiring more data to learn similar representations. We compare recognition across datasets in terms of the number of text lines and the number of characters, showing an equivalence trade-off. We compare character frequency distributions across scripts and show that Arabic is significantly more heavy-tailed than Latin. Our error analysis reveals that around 30 percent of substitution errors in Arabic datasets (e.g., KHATT) are caused by confusion between visually similar characters, compared to about 15 percent in Latin-script datasets such as IAM.

</details>


### [81] [LARE: Low-Attention Region Encoding for Text-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18885)
*Abdulmalik Alquwayfili,Faisal Almeshal,Jumanah Almajnouni,Leena Alotaibi,Faisal Alhajari,Mohammed Alkhrashi,Alreem Almuhrij,Abdullah Aldwyish,Raied Aljadaany,Huda Alamri,Muhammad Kamran J. Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对拥挤场景下图像检索中传统编码器忽视低注意力区域的问题，本文提出LARE框架，采用双编码策略并行编码低注意力区域与完整图像，并构建Dense-Set数据集进行评测，实验证明该方法通过保留非主导视觉线索提升了检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拥挤场景中的图像检索因传统视觉编码器存在显著性偏差，倾向于关注主导物体而忽略对细粒度检索至关重要的低注意力区域，现有模型在密集场景下表现不佳。

Method: 提出LARE框架，采用双编码策略：并行编码图像的完整特征与低注意力区域特征，从而生成更丰富多样的图像嵌入；同时构建Dense-Set子集（基于COCO和Flickr30K），对图像进行重新标注以丰富低注意力区域的描述，用于更严格的评测。

Result: 实验结果表明，LARE框架通过在共享潜在空间中保留细微的非主导视觉线索，有效提升了拥挤场景下的图像检索性能。

Conclusion: 显式建模被忽视的低注意力区域并采用双编码策略，能够缓解显著性偏差，提升拥挤场景中图像检索的细粒度表现，Dense-Set数据集也为该方向提供了更严格的评测基准。

Abstract: Image retrieval in crowded scenes is particularly challenging due to the salience bias of conventional visual encoders, which tend to focus on dominant objects while neglecting low-attention regions that are often crucial for fine-grained retrieval. We propose LARE (Low-Attention Region Encoding), a framework that explicitly models these overlooked regions. LARE adopts a dual-encoding strategy that encodes low-attention regions of an image and the full image in parallel, leading to more diverse and informative image embeddings. To evaluate image retrieval performance in challenging crowded scenes, we introduce Dense-Set, a challenging subset derived from COCO and Flickr30K. In this subset, images are re-captioned to provide richer descriptions of low-attention or previously overlooked regions. This dataset highlights the limitations of existing retrieval models and enables a more rigorous evaluation under densely crowded scene conditions. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework improves retrieval performance by preserving subtle, non-dominant visual cues within the shared latent space.

</details>


### [82] [DINO-Med3D: Bridging Dimension and Domain Gaps in Volumetric Segmentation via Progressive Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18886)
*Haoyu Hu,Xiyao Ma,Shiqi Liu,Linsen Zhang,Xiaoliang Xie,Xiaohu Zhou,Zeng-Guang Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DINO-Med3D是一个两阶段渐进框架，通过多切片嵌入模块、3D适配器和并行细节恢复流，将DINOv3编码器从自然图像领域迁移至3D医学分割任务，在五个公开数据集上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DINOv3在自然图像上表现出色，但直接应用于3D医学分割存在维度差异（2D→3D）和领域差异（自然→医学）两大障碍。

Method: 两阶段渐进框架：第一阶段通过多切片嵌入模块引入伪3D上下文弥合维度鸿沟，并利用分割代理任务进行领域适应；第二阶段在冻结的主干网络中添加轻量3D适配器以增强全局切片间连续性；同时设计并行细节恢复流保留高频边界线索以补偿嵌入过程中的空间信息损失。

Result: 在五个公开医学分割数据集上的实验表明，该方法成功将DINOv3适应至医学领域，并显著优于当前最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的DINO-Med3D框架有效解决了预训练视觉基础模型从自然领域向3D医学领域迁移时的维度与领域双重鸿沟问题。

Abstract: Although DINOv3 has demonstrated remarkable semantic discrimination in natural imagery, its direct application to volumetric medical segmentation is hindered by inherent dimension and domain disparities. To resolve these issues, we propose DINO-Med3D, a two-stage progressive framework that repurpose the pre-trained DINOv3 encoder for 3D medical tasks. In the first stage, we mitigate the dimension gap by introducing a multi-slice embedding module that incorporates pseudo-3D context, while simultaneously employing a segmentation proxy task to adapt representations learned from natural scenes to the medical domain. Subsequently, we further enhance volumetric understanding by adding lightweight 3D adapters into the frozen backbone to enforce global inter-slice continuity. Finally, to compensate for the spatial information loss inherent in the embedding process, we design a parallel detail recovery stream to explicitly preserve high-frequency boundary cues. Extensive experiments on five public datasets demonstrate that our approach successfully adapts DINOv3 to the medical domain and significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [83] [Automatic ply-specific analyses of CFRP micrographs using shortest-path-based ply distinction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18894)
*Jonas Naumann,Jonas P. Appels,Julius Biermann,Christopher Gorsky,Timo de Wolff,Christoph Brauer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种自动方法，通过将语义分割掩码视为图并利用最短路径算法，从高分辨率碳纤维增强聚合物显微图中区分 ply 实例，从而桥接语义分割与实例分割，实现全面的 ply 微观结构定量分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义分割只能识别纤维、基体等像素类别，但无法区分不同 ply 层实例，而 ply 实例信息对于分析制造缺陷、层间不均匀性以及微观结构与力学性能的关系至关重要，亟需一种自动化的 ply 实例分割方法。

Method: 将语义分割掩码转化为图结构（像素为顶点），利用最短路径算法计算 ply 间的分隔路径，从而将语义分割提升为 ply 实例分割。基于得到的路径将每个纤维像素分配到对应 ply，进而计算局部纤维体积分数、ply 层厚度、层间厚度等微观结构属性。

Result: 成功应用于高分辨率显微图，涵盖多种特征：单/多层中人工添加的间隙、不同堆叠顺序、贯穿 ply 的裂纹等。实现了每个纤维像素的 ply 分配，并完成了全面的定量 ply 分析。

Conclusion: 该方法能够揭示制造引起的不均匀性，为制造参数优化提供依据，并将力学性能与微观结构缺陷关联起来，具有重要的工程应用价值。

Abstract: We present an automated approach to distinguish between ply instances in semantic segmentation masks of high-resolution carbon-fiber reinforced polymer micrographs. Interpreting the segmentation mask as a graph with pixels as vertices, enables us to use a shortest-path algorithm yielding the ply-separating paths. Thereby, we bridge the gap between semantic segmentation and ply instance segmentation using global information. We successfully apply our approach on high-resolution micrographs featuring a broad range of characteristics like artificially added gaps in single or multiple plies, different stacking sequences and ply traversing cracks. Assigning each fiber pixel to a ply based on the calculated paths, allows for a comprehensive, quantitative ply analysis with respect to its microstructural properties like the local fiber volume fraction as well as locally resolved ply and interleaf layer thickness. These insights help to reveal manufacturing-induced inhomogeneities, draw conclusions on manufacturing parameters and link mechanical properties to underlying microstructural imperfections.

</details>


### [84] [BindEdit: Taming Attention Leakage for Precise Multi-Object Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18906)
*Chaewon Park,Soyoon Lee,Naeun Lee,Minjung Shin,Seogkyu Jeon,Kibeom Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BindEdit通过注意力约束机制解决多目标图像编辑中的注意力泄漏问题（编辑-令牌泄漏和源主导泄漏），在单次扩散轨迹中实现鲁棒的多目标编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑方法在复杂多目标场景中常出现语义混合、目标重复或编辑不完整等问题，归因于去噪过程中的注意力泄漏（空间区域与文本令牌之间的信号纠缠）。

Method: 提出BindEdit，包含：1) 联合正则化交叉注意力和自注意力以绑定目标令牌组到对应空间区域；2) 交叉注意力再平衡机制放大目标令牌影响并衰减残留源语义；3) 区域保真度项确保目标概念在编辑掩码内连贯表达。

Result: 在多目标基准测试中，BindEdit在单次扩散轨迹内一致优于现有方法，在单目标和多目标编辑场景下均保持鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: 识别并解决了两类注意力泄漏问题，提出的注意力级约束方法有效提升了多目标图像编辑的质量和稳定性。

Abstract: Real image editing enables precise manipulation of visual content, yet existing methods often fail in complex multi-object scenarios, causing semantic blending, object duplication, or incomplete edits. We attribute these failures to attention leakage, where signals across spatial regions and text tokens become entangled during the denoising process. Specifically, we identify two distinct forms of leakage: Edit-Token Leakage, where ambiguous token-region alignment leads to object blending, and Source Dominance Leakage, where tokens of unchanged source objects overwhelm the attention intended for target entities. To resolve these leakages, we propose \textbf{BindEdit}, which enforces attention-level constraints within a single diffusion trajectory. To suppress Edit-Token Leakage, BindEdit jointly regularizes cross- and self-attention so that each target token group is bound to its corresponding spatial region while maintaining instance-level separation. To suppress Source Dominance Leakage, a cross-attention re-balancing mechanism amplifies target token influence and attenuates residual source semantics within editable regions. Moreover, a region fidelity term ensures that each target concept is expressed coherently across the entire editing mask. Additionally, we propose a comprehensive multi-object benchmark encompassing diverse object counts and categories. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BindEdit consistently outperforms existing methods within a single diffusion trajectory, maintaining robust performance across both single- and multi-object editing scenarios.

</details>


### [85] [Physics-IQ Verified](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18943)
*Tim Rädsch,Yuki M Asano,Hilde Kuehne,Stefan Bauer,Priyank Jaini,Robert Geirhos,Carsten T. Lüth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对Physics-IQ基准进行了系统审计，揭露其缺陷并提出三项改进方案，最终得到Physics-IQ Verified基准，优化了57.6%的样本和34.8%的提示，在六个图像到视频生成模型的对比中观察到中等程度的排名变化（Kendall's τ = 0.46）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型需理解物理现实，但现有Physics-IQ基准存在缺陷，无法可靠衡量模型对物理世界的理解能力。作者旨在通过审计和修正该基准，提供更可靠的评估信号。

Method: 系统审计Physics-IQ基准，提出三项改进：1) 提升提示质量和真实标注质量以减少混淆因素影响；2) 引入样本级评分系统，对每个样本和指标进行等权加权。最终得到Physics-IQ Verified基准。

Result: Physics-IQ Verified优化了57.6%的样本和34.8%的提示。在六个图像到视频生成模型的对比中，观察到排名变化，Kendall's τ = 0.46，表明中度但有意义的变化。

Conclusion: Physics-IQ Verified为社区提供了更可靠的信号，有助于推动生成物理准确视频的VGM发展。代码已开源。

Abstract: Video generative models ( VGMs) have become a new frontier that can be used not just for video generation but for a multitude of downstream tasks, including world modeling. To advance these tasks, a good video model must understand the physical reality of the world. Evaluating this understanding is an emerging field and has led to the Physics-IQ benchmark, which quantifies this explicitly by comparing model-generated videos to real-world videos of physical experiments. In this work, we present a systematic audit of the Physics-IQ benchmark, expose shortcomings and propose three solutions that sharpen how we can measure physical understanding of VGMs. Specifically, we improve prompt and ground-truth quality to reduce the influence of confounding factors and further introduce a sample-level scoring system that weights each sample and metric equally. Our resulting benchmark, Physics-IQ Verified, refines 57.6\% of all samples and improves over 34.8\% of prompts. In a comparison study using six image-to-video generative models, we observe moderate but meaningful ranking changes (Kendall's $τ= 0.46$). We hope Physics-IQ Verified advances the community by providing a more reliable signal toward physically accurate VGMs. The code for the benchmark can be accessed at https://github.com/google-deepmind/physics-iq-benchmark

</details>


### [86] [SP-TransientBench: A Real-Captured Single Photon Perception Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18952)
*Hongzhou Dong,Zili Zhang,Ziting Wen,Yiheng Qiang,Runrong Deng,Wenle Dong,Ziwen Jiang,Xinyang Li,Rui Lu,Shuoyao Sun,Wenyu Wang,Ziyi Xia,Haitao Zheng,Guodong Shi,Xiaoqiang Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SP-TransientBench (STB)，一个基于固态单光子LiDAR真实采集的多任务基准数据集，包含10个场景、10,297个视角、256×192分辨率的时间飞行直方图及3D语义标注，用于深度估计、多视图重建和3D语义理解等单光子感知任务的系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单光子LiDAR感知研究多局限于模拟数据或小规模受控实验，缺乏真实场景下跨深度估计、多视图重建和3D语义理解的系统性评估基准。

Method: 使用固态单光子LiDAR以256×192分辨率采集10个多样场景的10,297个视图，提供完整时间飞行直方图、多返回行为、标准化元数据、校准相机位姿及13类3D语义标注，并为各任务设计专用数据划分和评估协议。

Result: 构建了首个真实采集的多任务单光子感知基准数据集STB，支持深度估计、多视图重建和3D语义理解的标准化评估。

Conclusion: STB填补了真实场景下单光子感知系统评测的空白，为可重复、一致性的单光子3D视觉研究提供了标准化平台。

Abstract: Single-photon LiDAR (SPL) based on single-photon avalanche diode (SPAD) sensing enables time-resolved photon measurements with extreme sensitivity, offering unique potential for active 3D perception in photon-starved scenarios.However, real-world single photon perception remains fundamentally challenging due to unique measurement noise and complex multi-return transient phenomena, which jointly complicate geometric reconstruction and semantic scene understanding. Despite growing interest in SPAD-based sensing, existing studies are largely limited to simulated data or small-scale controlled captures. As a result, systematic evaluation of real-world single photon perception across depth estimation, multi-view reconstruction, and 3D semantic understanding remains underexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce SP-TransientBench (STB), a real-captured multi-task benchmark for single photon perception. SP-TransientBenc comprises 10 diverse scenes and 10,297 views captured using a solid-state single-photon LiDAR at $256\times192$ resolution. Each view provides full time-of-flight histograms with multi-return behavior,standardized metadata, and calibrated camera poses for multi-view evaluation. We further provide 13-class 3D semantic annotations for selected scenes. By providing dedicated data splits and evaluation protocols for each task, STB enables consistent and reproducible benchmarking of real-world single photon perception across multiple 3D vision problems. The dataset and code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [87] [Motion-Focused Latent Action Enables Cross-Embodiment VLA Training from Human EgoVideos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18955)
*Runze Xu,Yiluo Zhang,Jian Wang,Yu Wang,Jincheng Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于潜动作的框架，利用无标签人类视频预训练通用VLA模型，通过混合解耦VQ-VAE构建跨本体动作码本，并采用意图-感知解耦策略减少动作幻觉，仅需50条轨迹即可在下游任务中达到与大规模标注数据集训练的SOTA相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现存VLA模型依赖大规模、多样化且带有精确动作标注的机器人数据集，而丰富的人类第一人称操作视频缺乏动作标签，难以被直接利用。本文旨在从未标注的人类视频中提取通用的动作先验知识。

Method: 1) 提出混合解耦VQ-VAE架构，通过物理掩码将运动动力学与环境背景分离，构建跨本体动作码本；2) 在人类视频上预训练码本，使VLM骨干网络学习动作意图的深度表征；3) 采用意图-感知解耦策略，VLM预测动作意图，冻结的视觉编码器提供状态特征给动作专家，减少动作幻觉。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中，仅使用无标签人类视频预训练的方法，在下游仅需50条轨迹微调即可达到与在大规模标注数据集上训练的SOTA VLA模型相当的性能。

Conclusion: 验证了从无标签人类视频中提取通用动作先验的有效性，大幅降低了对昂贵机器人动作标注数据的依赖，为扩展VLA模型训练数据来源提供了新路径。

Abstract: Training generalist Vision-Language-Action(VLA) models typically requires massive, diverse robotic datasets with high-fidelity action annotations. While egocentric human manipulation videos are abundant and capture significant environmental diversity, the absence of action labels makes them difficult to use in conventional training paradigms. To address this, we propose a latent-action-based framework designed to extract general action priors from unlabeled human videos. The architecture features a Hybrid Disentangled VQ-VAE that decouples motion dynamics from environmental backgrounds through physical masks, enabling the construction of a cross-embodiment action codebook. By pre-training on human videos with the codebook, the VLM backbone learns deep representations of action intent. For adaptation to specific embodiments, we introduce an intent-perception decoupling strategy where the VLM predicts the action intent while a separate frozen visual encoder provides state-specific features to the action expert, thereby reducing action hallucinations. Results in simulation and real-world environments show that our method, pre-trained exclusively on unlabeled human videos, performs competitively with state-of-the-art VLA models trained on massive annotated datasets, requiring only 50 trajectories for downstream adaptation.

</details>


### [88] [Mem-World: Memory-Augmented Action-Conditioned World Models for Persistent Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18960)
*Zirui Zheng,Jiaqian Yu,Xiongfeng Peng,jun shi,Mingyi Li,Chao Zhang,Weiming Li,Dong Wang,Huchuan Lu,Xu Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Mem-World，一种基于4D腕部视角中心曲面索引记忆（W-VMem）的多视角动作条件世界模型，通过几何感知的历史帧检索解决机械臂操作中的持续世界建模问题，在长时域任务中提升了策略评估的准确性和策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型在机械臂操作中存在持续建模困难：末端执行器频繁遮挡和腕部相机快速运动导致当前观测不足以预测未来视图，模型会遗忘或幻觉早期帧中的场景细节；已有的记忆检索策略难以在动态操作场景中识别信息丰富的历史帧。

Method: 提出Mem-World框架，核心是W-VMem（4D腕部视角中心曲面索引记忆），将历史观测锚定到随时间演化的表面元素上。通过显式建模场景元素被观测的时间和位置，W-VMem支持基于未来动作条件的几何感知相关历史帧检索。生成时通过基于曲面渲染和评分选择相关历史帧，为预测提供信息丰富且无冗余的上下文。

Result: Mem-World在复杂操作场景中生成长时程滚动预测；相比Ctrl-World，策略评估与真实世界性能的皮尔逊相关性提升14.5%；通过合成数据生成支持策略改进，长时域任务成功率从58%提升至72%。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模历史观测的几何时空锚点，有效解决了操作场景中世界模型的持续预测退化问题，提升了策略评估可信度和数据生成的实用价值。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models have emerged as a promising paradigm for robot learning, offering a scalable alternative to costly real-world experimentation by generating action-consistent video rollouts. However, persistent world modeling remains challenging in manipulation: frequent end-effector occlusions and rapid wrist-camera motion make the current observation insufficient for predicting future views, causing models to forget or hallucinate scene details seen in earlier frames. Existing memory retrieval strategies often fail to identify informative history in dynamic manipulation scenarios. To address this limitation, we propose Mem-World, a memory-augmented multi-view action-conditioned world model. At its core, we present W-VMem, a 4D wrist-view-centered surfel-indexed memory that anchors historical observations to temporally evolving surface elements. By explicitly modeling when and where scene elements are observed, W-VMem enables geometry-aware retrieval of relevant history frames conditioned on future actions. During generation, relevant history frames are selected via surfel-based rendering and scoring, providing informative and non-redundant context for prediction. Extensive experiments show that Mem-World generates persistent rollouts in complex manipulation scenarios, enables more reliable policy evaluation than Ctrl-World, improving the Pearson correlation with real-world performance by 14.5\%, and supports effective policy improvement through synthetic data generation, increasing success rates from 58\% to 72\% on long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [89] [Visual-OPSD: Cross-Modal On-Policy Self-Distillation for Efficient Unified Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18974)
*Pengyu Li,Zhitao Gao,Lingling Zhang,Muye Huang,Yuanming Li,Fangzhi Xu,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 统一多模态模型中的"视觉思维"推理成本高但收益有限，本文提出Visual-OPSD方法，通过师生同权值自蒸馏，将生成路径中的语义知识转移到纯文本学生模型，在9个基准上平均提升3.40pp，同时实现14.3倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前UMMs通过插入扩散生成的"视觉思维"来提升空间推理能力，但这种多步扩散带来了约一个数量级的推理成本。研究发现移除或噪声化VT对准确性几乎没有影响，但KL诊断表明生成路径本身编码了有用的推理信息。因此，作者希望在不付出高推理成本的前提下，获取生成路径中的语义知识。

Method: 提出Visual On-Policy Self-Distillation (Visual-OPSD)：教师和学生共享相同权重但上下文不同——教师能看到特权VT（含生成路径信息），学生只能看到问题。在教师生成的学生轨迹（on-policy）上进行token级JSD蒸馏，将教师的推理能力转移到纯文本学生模型中。

Result: 在9个基准上，Visual-OPSD相比生成式教师提升+3.40pp，加速14.3倍（每样本10.0s vs 142.8s）；相比同规模VLM提升+63.83pp（VSP基准）。高斯噪声对照实验显示真实VT带来+10.28pp增益（噪声仅+0.40pp），KL差距闭合58.4%，证明收益来源于生成路径的语义内容。

Conclusion: 视觉思维的价值不在于渲染出的像素本身，而在于生成路径中编码的语义推理过程。Visual-OPSD通过自蒸馏有效捕获了这一隐含知识，实现了推理性能提升和推理成本大幅降低的双赢。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) interleave generated ''visual thoughts'' (VTs) with text reasoning to improve spatial tasks. This incurs roughly an order-of-magnitude inference cost from multi-step diffusion. We find this cost yields limited direct benefit. On ThinkMorph, removing or noising VTs barely changes accuracy across nine benchmarks. Once rendered, attention concentrates on the VT regardless of content. Yet a KL diagnostic shows that conditioning on a privileged VT trace shifts the model's completion distribution. This suggests the generation pathway encodes useful reasoning beyond the rendered pixels. Motivated by this gap, we propose Visual On-Policy Self-Distillation(Visual-OPSD). Teacher and student share identical weights but differ in context: the teacher sees privileged VTs while the student sees only the question. Token-level JSD distillation on on-policy student trajectories transfers the teacher's reasoning to a text-only student. Across nine benchmarks, Visual-OPSD improves over its generative teacher by $+3.40$pp with $14.3\times$ speedup (10.0s vs. 142.8s per sample) and outperforms same-scale VLMs by $+63.83$pp on VSP. A Gaussian-noise control ($+0.40$pp vs. $+10.28$pp for real VTs) and $58.4\%$ closure of the KL gap confirm that gains come from the semantic content of the generation pathway.

</details>


### [90] [Show, Don't Ask: Generative Visual Disambiguation for Composed Image Retrieval with Turn-Valid Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18992)
*Amsisan Tran,Baogh Le,Tuan Kiet Pham,Sui Yang Guang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLARA是一个用于组合图像检索的视觉澄清框架，通过向用户展示少量候选图像而非提问文本问题来解决检索意图模糊问题，利用一致性预测理论保证多轮交互中的覆盖可靠性，并在视图和细粒度属性等模糊场景下优于文本提问基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组合图像检索方法使用文本修改查询时，用户意图往往存在歧义（一个查询可能对应多张目标图像）。虽然已有方法通过一致性预测估计模糊性并提问文本澄清问题，但存在两个局限：(1) 覆盖保证仅在第一轮交互有效；(2) 文本问题难以解决细粒度视觉差异（如外观、属性、视角）。因此需要更有效的澄清机制。

Method: 提出CLARA框架，核心思路是：(1) 向用户展示一小面板视觉候选图像（原型），用户直接选择最接近目标的图像，而非回答文本问题；(2) 利用用户选择诱导的似然比对校准集重新加权，以在多轮交互中维持有效的一致性覆盖保证；(3) 展示的原型受约束：必须代表当前候选集且从真实语料库图像中选取，防止生成图像人为提升覆盖率。

Result: 在开放域和时尚基准数据集上：(1) CLARA在单轮检索性能上与当前最优方法持平；(2) 在多轮交互中维持标称覆盖率；(3) 相比强文本提问基线，在更少轮数内找到目标图像；(4) 在涉及视角或细粒度属性的模糊场景下，视觉澄清的优势尤为显著。

Conclusion: CLARA通过视觉候选面板替代文本提问，结合重加权一致性预测保证多轮覆盖可靠性，有效解决了组合图像检索中的意图歧义问题，尤其在细粒度视觉差异场景下效果显著。

Abstract: Composed image retrieval (CIR) uses a reference image and a text modification to search for a target image. However, such queries often describe several possible images rather than one exact target, making the user's intent ambiguous. Recent methods address this by using conformal prediction to estimate ambiguity and by asking users clarifying text questions. However, these methods have two limitations: their coverage guarantee only holds at the first interaction, and text questions are often insufficient for resolving fine-grained visual differences such as appearance, attributes, or viewpoint. We propose CLARA, a clarification framework that resolves ambiguity by showing users a small panel of visual alternatives. Instead of answering text questions, the user simply selects the prototype image closest to the intended target. This provides a direct visual signal and avoids relying on a model to predict the user's answer. To maintain valid conformal guarantees across multiple interaction rounds, CLARA reweights calibration using the likelihood ratio induced by the user's selection. The displayed prototypes are also constrained to represent the current candidate set and are snapped to real corpus images, ensuring that generated images cannot artificially improve coverage. Experiments on open-domain and fashion benchmarks show that CLARA matches single-turn state-of-the-art retrieval performance, maintains nominal coverage across interaction rounds, and finds the intended target in fewer rounds than strong text-question baselines. Its advantage is especially clear when ambiguity involves viewpoint or fine-grained attributes, where visual clarification is more effective than textual questioning.

</details>


### [91] [FlowObject: Flow Steering for Bridging Generative Priors and Reconstruction Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19019)
*Yuchen Rao,Xuqian Ren,Yinyu Nie,Sayan Deb Sarkar,Biao Zhang,Vincent Lepetit,Friedrich Fraundorfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowObject通过无训练引导策略将流匹配模型与3DGS优化相结合，在稀疏视角下同时实现几何完整性与观测一致性，显著优于当前SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D生成模型（如Flow-Matching）存在“合成偏差”，生成结果与真实观测不一致；而优化方法（如3DGS）可保持观测一致性但无法推理未观测区域。目标是结合两者优势，实现稀疏视角下的完整3D重建。

Method: 提出FlowObject框架，将稀疏视角3D重建转化为无训练引导逆问题。采用双空间引导策略调控流匹配模型的ODE轨迹，利用生成先验补全未观测区域，同时强制与真实观测一致；最后通过3DGS细化阶段弥合生成输出与真实感重建之间的差距。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，FlowObject在几何完整性和视角相关外观保真度上显著优于现有生成模型和优化方法，尤其在严重遮挡场景下。

Conclusion: FlowObject有效统一了生成先验与观测约束，为稀疏视角3D重建提供了兼顾完整性与真实感的新范式。

Abstract: Recovering complete 3D representations of objects from few casual image captures remains a significant challenge. Recent 3D generative models, particularly those based on Flow-Matching (FM), can synthesize high-quality textured assets; however, they often suffer from ''synthetic bias'' where learned priors override observational evidence, alongside a lack of alignment with the observed instance. Conversely, optimization-based methods like 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provide high fidelity on visible surfaces but fail to reason about unobserved geometry. In this paper, we present FlowObject, a framework that reformulates sparse-view 3D reconstruction as a training-free, guided inverse problem. Our approach applies a dual-space guidance strategy to steer the Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) trajectory of a flow-matching model, enabling the completion of unseen regions through learned generative priors while enforcing strict consistency with real-world observations. By integrating a 3DGS refinement stage, FlowObject further bridges the gap between ''synthetic-looking'' generative outputs and photorealistic reconstructions. Comprehensive benchmarks on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that current state-of-the-art methods often struggle to achieve geometric completeness and observational consistency simultaneously, especially under severe occlusions. In contrast, our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art generative models and optimization-based frameworks in both geometric completeness and view-dependent appearance fidelity.

</details>


### [92] [Low-Rank Tensor Completion Based on Fractional Regularization with Ky Fan p-k Norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19046)
*Shan Fan,Feng Zhang,Jianjun Wang,Xi-Le Zhao,Tingwen Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出张量核范数与张量Ky Fan p-k范数之比（TNPK）作为张量tubal秩的非凸代理，用于低秩张量补全。该代理具有尺度不变性、参数灵活性等性质，在特定参数下可退化为TNK或TNF。基于张量零空间性质证明低秩张量是局部最优解，并推导了Ky Fan p-k逆范数的近端算子，开发了具有收敛保证的ADMM算法。实验验证了方法优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩张量补全方法中，张量核范数作为凸松弛不能准确逼近张量tubal秩，而非凸代理函数存在参数选择困难或缺乏理论保证等问题。本文旨在提出一种更好的非凸代理来近似张量tubal秩，同时保持优良的理论性质和计算可行性。

Method: 提出TNPK（张量核范数与张量Ky Fan p-k范数之比）作为非凸张量秩代理；构建LRTC模型并基于张量零空间性质（NSP）证明低秩张量是局部极小值点；推导Ky Fan p-k逆范数的近端算子；开发基于交替方向乘子法（ADMM）的高效算法并证明子序列收敛性。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，所提方法在性能上优于当前最先进的低秩张量补全方法。

Conclusion: TNPK是一个有效的非凸张量秩代理，兼具理论性质和计算优势；所提ADMM算法具有收敛保证，在实际应用中表现优异。

Abstract: This paper addresses low-rank tensor completion (LRTC) by proposing a novel nonconvex surrogate, namely the ratio of the tensor nuclear norm to the tensor Ky Fan p-k norm (TNPK), to accurately approximate the tensor tubal rank. The TNPK possesses appealing properties, including scale invariance, parameter flexibility, and the existence of closed-form solutions under specific choices of p and k. With specific parameter settings of p and k, it reduces to the ratio of the tensor nuclear norm to the tensor Ky Fan k norm (TNK) or the ratio of the tensor nuclear norm to the tensor Frobenius norm (TNF). We construct a LRTC model and, under the tensor null space property (NSP), prove that low-rank tensors are local minimizers of the proposed model. Moreover, we derive the proximal operator of the Ky Fan p-k inverse-norm and further develop an efficient alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) algorithm with guaranteed subsequential convergence under mild conditions. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets validate the superior performance of our method against state-of-the-art competitors.

</details>


### [93] [Benchmarking Large Vision-Language Models on Fine-Grained Image Tasks: From Evaluation to Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19053)
*Hong-Tao Yu,Chen-Wei Xie,Yuxin Peng,Serge Belongie,Xiu-Shen Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FG-BMK基准，包含101万问题与28万图像，系统评估大视觉语言模型在细粒度图像任务上的能力，发现现有模型在视觉表征、语义对齐和类别知识方面存在交织瓶颈，并提供诊断性分析与改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要从整体或任务特定角度评估LVLM，但对其在细粒度图像任务（计算机视觉基础任务）上的能力理解不足，因此需要构建专门的细粒度评估基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建FG-BMK基准（101万问题、28万图像），覆盖通用物体中心到专业领域场景；通过人导向和机器导向范式，联合评估对话级细粒度语义识别与特征级视觉可辨别性；设计诊断分析框架区分失败源于视觉表征不足、视觉-语义接地薄弱还是细粒度知识有限。

Result: 当前LVLM在细粒度识别任务上表现不足，失败源于视觉表征、语义接地、模态对齐和类别知识四个交织瓶颈。分析还揭示了训练设计因素及视觉/语言扰动对细粒度能力的影响。

Conclusion: 该基准为理解当前LVLM在细粒度视觉任务中的局限性提供了诊断性见解，并为未来数据构建和模型设计提供指导。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have demonstrated remarkable multimodal perception and reasoning capabilities. While numerous benchmarks have evaluated LVLMs from holistic or task-specific perspectives, their capabilities on fine-grained image tasks-fundamental to computer vision-remain insufficiently understood. To address this gap, we introduce FG-BMK, a comprehensive fine-grained evaluation benchmark containing 1.01 million questions and 0.28 million images, covering diverse scenarios from common object-centric domains to specialized domains. FG-BMK jointly evaluates dialogue-level fine-grained semantic recognition and feature-level visual discriminability through human-oriented and machine-oriented paradigms, enabling diagnostic analysis of whether LVLM failures arise from insufficient visual representations, weak visual-to-semantic grounding, or limited fine-grained knowledge. Through extensive experiments on a diverse set of representative LVLMs/VLMs, we find that current LVLMs remain inadequate fine-grained recognizers, with failures arising from intertwined bottlenecks in visual representations, semantic grounding, modality alignment, and category-level knowledge. We further analyze training design factors for improving fine-grained capabilities and examine how visual and linguistic perturbations affect LVLM predictions. These findings provide diagnostic insights into the limitations of current LVLMs and offer guidance for future data construction and model design in developing more reliable LVLMs for fine-grained visual tasks. Our code is open-source and available at https://fg-bmk.github.io/.

</details>


### [94] [DREAM: Extending Vision-Language Models with Dual-Objective Encoding for Cross-Modal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19062)
*Kaleem Ullah,Altaf Hussain,Muhammad Munsif,Sung Wook Baik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DREAM模型，通过双路径表示增强与对齐方法（层级视觉编码器+混合语言建模）提升视频与文本的跨模态语义匹配，在MSRVTT、MSVD和LSMDC基准上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频检索系统依赖手工特征或浅层跨模态映射，难以捕捉复杂语义和时间动态；而大规模视觉-语言模型虽改善了跨模态对齐，但仍难以建模细粒度时间依赖和细致的语言结构。

Method: DREAM包含两条路径：1）混合语言建模策略，结合掩码语言建模和排列语言建模目标，捕捉局部和全局语言语义；2）层级视觉编码器，采用级联组注意力，通过多阶段token交互和由粗到细的注意力精化，融合时空信息。

Result: 在MSRVTT、MSVD和LSMDC数据集上分别取得R1分数49.4%、49.7%和27.3%，均达到新SOTA。定性分析显示模型能保持跨帧注意力一致性，并将复杂查询与动态视频内容对齐。

Conclusion: 层级注意力和双目标文本建模能有效实现鲁棒的、上下文感知的视频检索，为跨模态表示学习研究开辟新方向。

Abstract: In today's media-driven world, the exponential growth of video content across domains such as surveillance, education, and entertainment has made retrieving semantically relevant videos via natural language queries increasingly critical. Early video retrieval systems relied on handcrafted features or shallow cross-modal mappings, limiting their ability to capture complex semantics and temporal dynamics. While large-scale vision-language models have improved cross-modal alignment, challenges remain in modeling fine-grained temporal dependencies and nuanced linguistic structures. In this paper, we introduce DREAM: Dual-path Representation Enhancement and Alignment Model, a novel multimodal framework that addresses these limitations through enhanced visual and textual encoding. DREAM incorporates a hybrid language modeling strategy that combines masked and permuted language modeling objectives to capture both local and global linguistic semantics. On the visual side, we design a hierarchical vision encoder with cascaded group attention, which integrates spatial and temporal information through multi-stage token interaction and coarse-to-fine attention refinement. We validate DREAM through comprehensive evaluations on the widely-used MSRVTT, MSVD and LSMDC benchmark datasets, where it achieves new state-of-the-art R1 scores of 49.4%, 49.7% and 27.3%, respectively. Qualitative analyses further show the model's ability to maintain coherent attention across frames and align complex queries with dynamic video content. These findings underscore the effectiveness of hierarchical attention and dual-objective textual modeling in enabling robust, context-aware video retrieval, and pave the way for future research in advancing cross-modal representation learning.

</details>


### [95] [Taming I2V models for Image HOI Editing: A Cognitive Benchmark and Agentic Self-Correcting Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19073)
*Jiayi Gao,Qingchao Chen,Yuxin Peng,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了HOI-Edit基准数据集和HOI-Eval自动化评估指标，用于解决现有图像编辑方法无法处理复杂人-物交互（HOI）的问题。发现图像到视频（I2V）模型天然适合动态HOI编辑，并提出了自纠正框架SCPE，通过迭代优化提示词约束I2V模型生成，在HOI-Edit上取得了与SOTA编辑模型竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑方法在处理复杂人-物交互（HOI）时表现不佳，而现有基准将HOI与静态属性混为一谈，且依赖无法同时评估动态交互有效性和人-物对保持的全局指标。

Method: 1) 构建HOI-Edit基准，包含三个渐进认知层级；2) 提出HOI-Eval自动化指标，通过VLM对包含grounded人-物对的图像进行思考后问答来评估实例级交互；3) 提出SCPE（自纠正过程编辑）框架，通过迭代优化提示词约束I2V模型生成，实现自纠正的图像编辑。

Result: SCPE在HOI-Edit上达到了与SOTA编辑模型（如Nano Banana）竞争的性能。

Conclusion: I2V模型因其时序生成能力天然适合动态HOI编辑，不仅能取得优越性能，还能通过"回放失败过程"提供独特的可诊断性，SCPE框架进一步提升了交互准确性。

Abstract: Current image editing methods excel at static attributes but fail at complex Human-Object Interactions (HOI), a critical challenge unaddressed by existing benchmarks that conflate HOI with static attributes, relying on global metrics incapable of simultaneously assessing dynamic interaction validity and entangled human-object pair preservation. Thus, we first introduce HOI-Edit, a comprehensive benchmark with three progressive cognitive levels, which features an automated metric HOI-Eval that reliably evaluates instance-level interaction by letting VLM Q&A after thinking with images containing grounded Human-Object pairs. Considering the task's essence of remodeling dynamic relationships, we benchmark Image-to-Video (I2V) models, finding them inherently suited for dynamic editing due to their temporal generation capabilities. Crucially, beyond superior performance, this capability provides a "replay of the failure process," offering unique diagnosability into why errors occur. We thus propose SCPE (Self-Correcting Process Editing), a novel, agentic self-correcting framework that constrains the generation of I2V models through iteratively refined prompts, enabling the generated videos to more accurately present the target HOI. Extracted frames from these videos are the final editing results. On HOI-Edit, SCPE achieves performance competitive with state-of-the-art (SOTA) editing models like Nano Banana on interaction. Code is available at https://github.com/oceanflowlab/HOI-Edit.

</details>


### [96] [PorTEXTO: A European Portuguese Benchmark for Visual Text Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19096)
*João Cardeira,Diogo Glória-Silva,Manuel Letras da Luz,Rafael Ferreira,Diogo Tavares,David Semedo,João Magalhães*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了PorTEXTO，首个面向现代欧洲葡萄牙语（pt-PT）的OCR基准测试，发现专用多语言数据比模型规模或分辨率更能提升pt-PT性能，并发布了开源资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲葡萄牙语（pt-PT）在现有OCR基准测试中严重缺失，现有少量基准多聚焦历史文献，缺乏对现代、文化相关场景的覆盖。

Method: 构建PorTEXTO基准，采用结合前沿LVLM转录与母语者全面审核的标注流程，确保数据质量。

Result: 大多数模型在真实样本上的性能显著低于合成样本；专用多语言数据对pt-PT性能的驱动效果优于模型规模或分辨率预算。

Conclusion: 当前应优先构建高质量的pt-PT多语言OCR数据资源，而非追求更大的模型或更高分辨率。

Abstract: European Portuguese (pt-PT) is largely absent from OCR benchmarks, which skew toward high-resource languages. The few benchmarks that cover pt-PT focus on historical artifacts and literature. This work addresses modern OCR applications, introducing PorTEXTO, the first benchmark for contemporary and culturally relevant pt-PT visual text extraction. To ascertain quality, we employ an annotation pipeline combining transcriptions from a frontier LVLM with exhaustive review by native speakers. We observe a sharp performance drop from synthetic to real world samples in most models, and find that, currently, specialized multilingual data is a better driver for pt-PT performance than model size or resolution budget, motivating the release of open pt-PT OCR resources.

</details>


### [97] [DVANet: Degradation-aware Visual-prior Alignment Network for Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19097)
*Yanjie Tu,Qingsen Yan,Axi Niu,Tao Hu,Haokui Zhang,Jiantao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DVANet，一种基于半二次分裂优化的深度展开网络，通过退化感知观测一致性与视觉先验引导重建的协同展开，实现复杂退化下的统一图像修复，在多种退化场景中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一图像修复方法多为黑盒映射，缺乏显式优化解释；而深度展开方法依赖固定退化假设或预定义退化信息，难以适应复杂退化和局部损坏内容的统一修复需求，在退化抑制和结构细节恢复方面性能受限。

Method: DVANet受半二次分裂优化算法启发，将统一图像修复建模为退化感知观测一致性分支与视觉先验引导重建分支的协同展开过程。前者通过退化表示模块提取全局和局部退化线索，利用退化条件映射增强对不同退化类型的适应性；后者引入DINOv3提供分层结构语义视觉先验，补充损坏区域的缺失结构信息。

Result: 在多种退化场景和跨域图像修复任务中，DVANet达到优越或具有竞争力的性能，展现出良好的退化适应性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: DVANet通过将优化算法与深度展开相结合，有效解决了复杂退化下的统一图像修复问题，在退化抑制和细节恢复方面取得显著提升。

Abstract: All-in-One image restoration aims to develop a unified restoration framework for handling diverse degradation types. Existing end-to-end methods usually regard the restoration process as a black-box mapping, lacking an explicit optimization interpretation. Although deep unfolding provides an interpretable iterative modeling paradigm for image restoration, existing methods mostly rely on fixed degradation assumptions or predefined degradation information, making them difficult to adapt to unified restoration requirements under complex degradations and locally damaged content. This limitation restricts their performance in degradation suppression and structural detail recovery. To address these issues, this paper proposes DVANet, a deep unfolding network inspired by the half-quadratic splitting optimization algorithm, which formulates unified image restoration under complex degradations as a collaborative unfolding process between degradation-aware observation consistency and visual-prior-guided reconstruction. Specifically, in the degradation-aware observation consistency branch, a degradation representation module is employed to extract global degradation attributes and local degradation cues, and degradation-conditioned mapping is used to enhance the model's adaptability to different degradation types. In the visual-prior-guided reconstruction branch, DINOv3 is introduced to provide structural and semantic information as hierarchical visual priors, thereby complementing the missing structural information in damaged regions and improving detail recovery. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DVANet achieves superior or competitive performance on multi-scenario degradation and cross-domain image restoration tasks, showing favorable degradation adaptability and generalization ability.

</details>


### [98] [AMALIA-VL: A Native European Portuguese Open-Source Vision and Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19100)
*Diogo Glória-Silva,João Cardeira,Manuel Letras da Luz,Afonso Simplício,Gonçalo Vinagre,Diogo Tavares,Rafael Ferreira,Inês Calvo,Inês Vieira,David Semedo,João Magalhães*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AMALIA-VL 是首个面向欧洲葡萄牙语（pt-PT）构建的开源指令微调大视觉语言模型，采用高分辨率视觉编码器、动态图像分块技术及pt-PT优化的语言模型，通过三阶段训练（视觉-语言对齐、通用视觉指令微调、偏好优化）和专门的多模态数据混合策略，填补了现有开源模型对pt-PT语言的严重空白，并建立了强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源多模态大语言模型严重缺乏对欧洲葡萄牙语（pt-PT）的支持，要么将其与巴西葡萄牙语混为一谈，要么在训练数据中极少覆盖，导致pt-PT在多模态领域被系统性忽视。

Method: 构建AMALIA-VL模型，包括高分辨率视觉编码器与动态图像分块技术、pt-PT优化的开源语言模型及可学习的连接器；设计三阶段训练流程（视觉-语言对齐、通用视觉指令微调、偏好优化）；构建以pt-PT为中心的多模态数据混合策略，融合筛选和翻译的公开数据集以及新增的专用数据集。

Result: AMALIA-VL为开源pt-PT大视觉语言模型建立了强基线，并将发布模型权重、训练数据、构建流程以及机器翻译的pt-PT评估基准。

Conclusion: AMALIA-VL填补了欧洲葡萄牙语在多模态大语言模型领域的空白，通过开源全部资源和数据，推动了pt-PT LVLM开发的民主化。

Abstract: Large Vision and Language Models (LVLMs) have advanced rapidly, yet European Portuguese (pt-PT) remains systematically underserved by existing open-source multimodal models, which either conflate it with Brazilian Portuguese or severely under-represent it in their training data mixes. We introduce AMALIA-VL, the first open-source instruction-tuned LVLM built natively for pt-PT, pairing a high-resolution vision encoder with dynamic image tiling and a fully open pt-PT-optimized language model via a learned connector. We contribute with a purposefully designed three-stage training process - vision-language alignment, general visual instruction tuning, and preference optimization - together with a pt-PT-centric multimodal data mix combining curated and translated public datasets with novel datasets that address the near-total absence of European Portuguese multimodal resources. Our evaluation shows that AMALIA-VL establishes a strong baseline for open-source pt-PT LVLMs.We will release model weights, training data, and construction pipelines along with machine-translated pt-PT evaluation benchmarks to help democratize pt-PT LVLM development.

</details>


### [99] [ProductConsistency: Improving Product Identity Preservation in Instruction-Based Image Editing via SFT and RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19103)
*Mukund Khanna,Raj Singh Yadav,Kunal Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ProductConsistency数据集（含87k SFT样本、869 RL图像及专用基准），并提出循环一致性奖励用于强化学习，以改善指令式产品图像编辑中产品特征、品牌和文本元素的保真度。基于Qwen-Image-Edit-2511和Flux.1-Kontext-dev的微调实验表明，该方法在OCR指标、感知质量及MLLM评估上均有显著提升，字符错误率降低5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令式图像编辑模型在产品场景中难以保持产品特征、品牌元素和文本的精细对象一致性，且缺乏专门用于产品图像编辑且具有文本保真约束的数据集。

Method: 构建ProductConsistency数据集，包含87k样本的监督微调集、869张产品图像的强化学习集及标准化基准；提出循环一致性奖励，通过计算原始产品描述与编辑后图像生成描述之间的字幕相似度来约束产品身份语义保持。

Result: 在Qwen-Image-Edit-2511和Flux.1-Kontext-dev上微调后，OCR指标、感知指标和MLLM评估均优于基线；Qwen模型字符错误率降低5倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据集和循环一致性奖励方法有效提升了产品图像编辑中的产品一致性、文本渲染和视觉质量，验证了针对产品场景设计专门数据与训练策略的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in instruction-based image editing have enabled models to perform complex visual edits from natural language instructions. However, in product-centric scenarios where preserving product features, branding, and textual elements are critical, current open and closed source models often struggle to maintain this fine-grained object identity. This issue is further compounded by the lack of datasets for instruction-based product image editing with text fidelity constraints, leaving it largely treated as an implicit capability of instruction-based image editing models.
  In this work, we introduce the ProductConsistency dataset which is designed to improve product-centric image editing. Our approach includes a supervised fine-tuning (SFT) dataset of 87k samples for product editing, a reinforcement learning (RL) dataset with 869 unique product images, and a new benchmark dataset, the ProductConsistency Benchmark, to allow rigorous and standardized evaluation of editing models. To guide RL training, we propose a Cyclic Consistency reward that enforces semantic preservation of product identity by using caption similarity between the original product description and captions generated from the edited image. We fine-tune both Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 and Flux.1-Kontext-dev using our dataset and demonstrate consistent improvements over baseline models in OCR and Perceptual metrics, and MLLM-based evaluations as well, indicating stronger product consistency, text rendering, and overall visual quality; with the Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 model achieving a 5x reduction in the character error rate. The code and pipeline is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ProductConsistency-6FCC/README.md

</details>


### [100] [Urdu Katib Handwritten Dataset: A Historical Document Dataset for Offline Urdu Handwritten Text Recognition with CRNN-Based Baseline Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19139)
*Ramza Basharat,Muhammad Usman Ali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Urdu Katib手写数据集(UKHD)，这是首个专为历史Katib书法家手写文本构建的离线乌尔都语手写文本行数据集，并评估了多种CRNN混合模型，其中CNN-BGRU-CTC模型在字符错误率和词错误率上表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乌尔都语手写文本识别研究相对滞后，主要原因在于其草书脚本的独特挑战以及基准数据集的稀缺和不可获得性。

Method: 构建了名为Urdu Katib手写数据集(UKHD)的专门真实数据集，该数据集收集自历史时期Katibs书写的材料，涵盖Nastalique书法风格中多种平尖笔书写变体。同时评估了多种基于CRNN的混合模型，以确定最优架构。

Result: CNN-BGRU-CTC模型在分析模型中表现最为鲁棒，取得了较低的字符错误率(CER)和词错误率(WER)。

Conclusion: 该研究工作旨在支持和鼓励研究社区开发鲁棒的识别系统，以保护乌尔都语手写文学遗产。

Abstract: Automatic Handwritten Text Recognition (HTR) is inherently a challenging task, and its complexity is further increased when dealing with cursive scripts. Although significant efforts have been made on various cursive scripts, research regarding Urdu Handwritten Text Recognition (UHTR) has been relatively limited. This lag of research is primarily due to the unique challenges posed by its script, and the scarcity and unavailability of benchmark datasets. Therefore, to advance research in UHTR, this study presents a specialized real dataset called the Urdu Katib Handwritten Dataset (UKHD). To the best of our knowledge, this is the first offline Urdu handwritten text lines dataset specifically curated from the materials written by Katibs in historical times. It encompasses a diverse range of flat nib writing variations in the Nastalique calligraphic style. Additionally, the effectiveness of different CRNN-based hybrid models has been evaluated to identify the optimal architecture for Urdu Katib Handwriting Recognition (UKHR). Among the analyzed models, the CNN-BGRU-CTC model showed more robust performance, with low Character Error Rate (CER) and Word Error Rate (WER). This research work aims to support and encourage the research community in developing a robust recognition system for preserving Urdu handwritten literature.

</details>


### [101] [Hand-4DGS: Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting for 4D Hand Reconstruction from Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19156)
*Jeongmin Bae,Seoha Kim,Marc Pollefeys,Mahdi Rad,Youngjung Uh,Taein Kwon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Hand-4DGS是首个前馈式框架，能从第一人称视角视频中快速（~60 FPS）重建动态4D手部，利用网格引导表示和时序卷积，无需昂贵的3D标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视频中的动态3D手部重建对AR/VR和AI眼镜等下一代计算平台至关重要，但现有工作多聚焦多视角3D手部重建或4D人体重建，缺乏针对第一人称单目视频的4D手部重建方法。同时，头部快速运动、手部快速动态、严重遮挡及单视角观测的固有歧义使该任务极具挑战性。

Method: 提出Hand-4DGS框架，核心包含：(1) 前馈式网络实现快速推理（~60 FPS）和强泛化能力；(2) 网格引导的表示方法提供结构先验；(3) 时序卷积建模动态运动；(4) 基于高斯泼溅的2D图像监督，无需3D手部姿态真值标注。

Result: 在H2O和ARCTIC两个具有挑战性的第一人称数据集上评估，该方法显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: Hand-4DGS利用前馈网络的泛化能力和有效的2D图像监督，实现了从第一人称视频高效重建动态4D手部，为AR/VR等应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Dynamic 3D hand reconstruction from egocentric videos is essential for next-generation computing platforms such as AR/VR and AI glasses. Despite its importance, most prior works focus either on multi-view 3D hand reconstruction or on 4D human body reconstruction. Egocentric 4D hand reconstruction remains challenging due to fast head motion, rapid hand dynamics, severe occlusions, and inherent ambiguity from single-view observations. To address these challenges, we introduce Hand-4DGS, the first feed-forward framework for reconstructing dynamic 4D hands directly from egocentric videos, enabling both fast (~60 FPS) inference and strong generalization. Our approach incorporates a mesh-guided representation for structural priors and temporal convolutions to model dynamic motion. We evaluate our framework on two challenging egocentric datasets, H2O and ARCTIC, and demonstrate significant improvements over baselines. Our method benefits from the generalization capability of feed-forward networks and effective 2D image supervision through Gaussian splatting, without requiring expensive 3D hand pose ground-truth annotations.

</details>


### [102] [When AUC Misleads: Polarization-Aware Evaluation of Deepfake Detectors under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19184)
*Dat Nguyen,Cosmin Radoi,Romain Hermary,Marcella Astrid,Nesryne Mejri,Enjie Ghorbel,Djamila Aouada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Cross-AUC新指标，通过结合每域AUC与基于Wasserstein距离的预测极化度量，更真实地评估深度伪造检测器在域偏移下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测评估使用各数据集独立计算的AUC，无法反映检测器面对混合数据源和多种伪影类型的真实场景，缺乏对域偏移鲁棒性的合理度量。

Method: 提出Cross-dataset AUC（Cross-AUC），将每域AUC与预测极化度量（基于Wasserstein距离计算的类别分数分布差异）加权平均，以量化模型对域偏移的鲁棒性。

Result: 在七个基准数据集上验证，证明Cross-AUC比传统AUC更能反映检测器在域偏移下的实际泛化能力，且具有更好的可解释性。

Conclusion: Cross-AUC为深度伪造检测提供更贴近真实部署场景的评估指标，能解释性能下降的根源。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI, such as diffusion models and face-swapping tools, have enabled the creation of highly realistic deepfakes, leading to real-world harms including financial fraud and non-consensual explicit content. In response, deepfake detection has become an active research area, with recent methods increasingly focusing on improving generalization to unseen manipulations. This is typically evaluated using the Area Under the ROC Curve (AUC) measured separately across multiple datasets. However, such an evaluation fails to reflect real-world scenarios where detectors face a mixture of data sources and varying artifact types. To address this limitation, we introduce a novel metric, Cross-dataset AUC (Cross-AUC) that averages per-domain AUCs with a measure of prediction polarization for taking into account the robustness to domain shift. The polarization extent is quantified by the Wasserstein Distance between class score distributions. Cross-AUC not only assesses the generalization capabilities of deepfake detectors under domain shifts more realistically, but it is also interpretable as it better explains the reason behind a drop in performance. Experiments performed on seven benchmark datasets demonstrate its practical relevance.

</details>


### [103] [Moebius: 0.2B Lightweight Image Inpainting Framework with 10B-Level Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19195)
*Kangsheng Duan,Ziyang Xu,Wenyu Liu,Xiaohu Ruan,Xiaoxin Chen,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Moebius是一个超轻量级图像修复框架，通过局部-λ混合交互模块和自适应多粒度蒸馏策略，以不到2%的参数（0.22B vs 11.9B）和超过15倍的推理加速，达到了媲美甚至超越10B级工业模型FLUX.1-Fill-Dev的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 10B级工业基础模型虽在图像修复领域表现卓越，但其高昂的计算成本严重阻碍了实际部署。构建高度优化的轻量级专精模型是可行方案，但极端结构压缩会引发严重的表示瓶颈。

Method: 系统性地重构扩散骨干网络，引入局部-λ混合交互（LλMI）模块（包含Local-λ和Interactive-λ子模块），将空间上下文和全局语义先验压缩为固定大小的线性矩阵，在大幅减少参数的同时保留复杂潜在交互。配合自适应多粒度蒸馏策略，在潜在空间内动态平衡多种梯度损失以实现高保真对齐。

Result: 在自然图像和人像基准测试中，Moebius以0.22B参数（FLUX.1-Fill-Dev的1.85%）达到媲美或超越11.9B参数模型的生成质量，同时实现超过15倍的总推理加速。

Conclusion: Moebius通过精心设计的轻量架构与蒸馏策略的协同，在极端参数压缩下依然保持高保真修复能力，为高效图像修复设立了新的效率标准。

Abstract: While 10B-level industrial foundation models have pushed the boundaries of image inpainting, their prohibitive computational costs severely hinder practical deployment. Constructing a highly optimized task-specific specialist offers a promising solution; however, extreme structural compression inevitably triggers a severe representation bottleneck. To conquer this, we propose Moebius, a highly efficient lightweight inpainting framework. We systematically reconstruct the diffusion backbone by introducing the Local-$λ$ Mix Interaction ($LλMI$) block. Comprising Local-$λ$ and Interactive-$λ$ modules, it elegantly summarizes spatial contexts and global semantic priors into fixed-size linear matrices, preserving complex latent interactions while drastically shedding parameters. Furthermore, to unlock the full representational capacity of this highly compact architecture, we synergistically pair it with an adaptive multi-granularity distillation strategy. Operating strictly within the latent space to avoid expensive pixel-space decoding, this strategy dynamically balances multiple gradient-based losses to achieve high-fidelity alignment. Extensive experiments across natural and portrait benchmarks demonstrate that this optimal synergy enables Moebius to rival or even surpass the generation quality of the 10B-level industrial generalist FLUX.1-Fill-Dev. Remarkably, Moebius achieves this using less than 2\% of the parameters (0.22B vs. 11.9B) while delivering a $>15\times$ acceleration in total inference time, setting a new efficiency standard for high-fidelity inpainting. Project page at https://hustvl.github.io/Moebius.

</details>


### [104] [ROSA-TFormer: A Radar-Optical Sensor-Aware Temporal Transformer for Pinus sylvestris Plantation Classification in Northern Shaanxi Using GEE-Derived Sentinel-1/2 Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19204)
*Nengbo Zhang,Chang sheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ROSA-TFormer模型，融合Sentinel-1/2雷达与光学时序数据，用于陕北地区樟子松人工林点级分类，在半月尺度数据集上达到99.67%的整体准确率和98.91%的樟子松F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确识别樟子松人工林对监测陕北地区造林质量和生态恢复至关重要，现有方法对雷达-光学多源时序特征的融合利用不足。

Method: 提出ROSA-TFormer模型，包含独立的SAR和光学嵌入分支、传感器感知门控机制以及时序注意力池化模块，利用Google Earth Engine生成的Sentinel-1/2时序数据进行分类。

Result: 在半月尺度大数据集上，整体准确率99.67%，宏F1 99.56%，樟子松F1 98.91%。空间块验证和消融实验证明了雷达-光学时序融合和传感器感知建模的有效性。

Conclusion: ROSA-TFormer在点级樟子松人工林分类中表现出色，但更广泛的墙到墙验证仍有必要。

Abstract: Accurate identification of Pinus sylvestris var. mongolica plantations is important for monitoring afforestation quality and ecological restoration in northern Shaanxi. This paper proposes ROSA-TFormer, a radar-optical sensor-aware temporal Transformer for P. sylvestris classification using Sentinel-1/2 time-series data generated on Google Earth Engine. The model integrates separate SAR and optical embedding branches, a sensor-aware gate, and temporal attention pooling to capture multi-source seasonal features. Experiments on monthly and half-month point-level datasets show that ROSA-TFormer achieves strong classification performance, with 99.67% overall accuracy, 99.56% macro F1, and 98.91% P. sylvestris F1 on the HalfMonth-dataBig dataset. Spatial block validation and ablation results further indicate the effectiveness of radar-optical temporal fusion and sensor-aware modeling. The results demonstrate the potential of ROSA-TFormer for point-level P. sylvestris plantation classification, while broader wall-to-wall validation remains necessary.

</details>


### [105] [GUMP-Net: An interpretable model-data-driven intelligent algorithm for multi-class pelvic segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19215)
*Liheng Wang,Yinghui Zhang,Licheng Zhang,Hailin Xu,Qiyong Cao,Chong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GUMP-Net是一种结合改进测地线活动轮廓模型与深度神经网络的解释性多类骨盆分割算法，通过对象检测、边缘检测和迭代演化三个模块实现精确、鲁棒的骨盆分割，尤其在少量训练数据下表现优异，并在踝关节数据集上验证了通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 骨盆分割是骨盆骨折精准诊疗及手术规划导航中的基础且重要的研究问题，现有方法在准确性和可解释性方面存在不足，尤其是小样本训练场景下性能有限。

Method: 提出GUMP-Net，融合改进的测地线活动轮廓模型与深度神经网络，包含三个模块：1) 目标检测模块用于自动水平集初始化；2) 边缘检测器模块学习解剖感知的边缘检测函数；3) 迭代模块用于深层水平集演化。该方法结合了水平集表示与深度学习的优势。

Result: 在骨盆数据集上的大量实验证明算法的合理性和有效性；在踝关节数据集上的扩展实验表明可推广至其他解剖结构；相比现有方法，在小训练数据场景下展现出更精确、鲁棒且一致的性能。

Conclusion: GUMP-Net不仅为复杂骨折复位提供了高效分割方法，而且为理解深度学习分割提供了可解释的几何视角。

Abstract: Pelvic segmentation is one of the most important and fundamental research problems in precise and intelligent diagnosis and treatment, as well as surgical planning and navigation for pelvic fractures. By combining an improved geodesic active contour model with deep neural networks, we propose GUMP-Net, an interpretable model-data-driven intelligent algorithm for multi-class pelvic segmentation, in which three network modules are designed to constitute the overall segmentation framework together: the object detection module for automatic level set initialization, the edge detector module for learning an anatomy-aware edge detector function and the iteration module for deep level set evolution. Leveraging the advantages of level set representation and deep learning, GUMP-Net shows more accurate, robust and consistent segmentation performance, especially in small training data situation, compared to the state-of-the-art methods. Extensive experiments on pelvic datasets demonstrate the rationality and effectiveness of the proposed algorithm. Further experiments extended to ankle dataset indicate broader applications to other anatomies. The proposed algorithm not only provides an efficient segmentation method for complex fracture reduction, but also gives an interpretable geometric perspective for understanding deep learning segmentation.

</details>


### [106] [Transformer Geometry Observatory TGO-I: Spectral Geometry Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19249)
*Kaustubh Kapil,Kishor P. Upla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Transformer几何观测站（TGO）框架，用于系统研究Vision Transformer的表征几何与动力学。TGO-I聚焦谱几何分析，发现ViT训练过程中维度利用持续增加、各向异性降低、谱熵和参与比上升，与直觉相反，方差在表征维度上呈现再分布而非集中化，尤其CLS token表征具有最高有效维度和最低各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Vision Transformers在众多计算机视觉任务中取得广泛成功，但其维度与表征几何的基本理解仍相对未被充分探索。现有研究缺乏系统性框架来解析ViT内部表征的几何结构和动力学特性。

Method: 提出Transformer Geometry Observatory (TGO)系统实验与分析框架，TGO-I专注于ViT表征的谱几何分析，通过在ImageNet-100上训练的ViT-Small/16模型，分析有效秩、稳定秩、参与比、谱熵、谱平坦度、谱各向异性、协方差结构、特征谱和奇异值谱等指标。

Result: 训练过程中维度利用持续增加，各向异性降低，谱熵和参与比上升，特征谱逐渐平坦。方差在表征维度上呈现渐进再分布，而非集中到少数主导方向。CLS token表征具有最高的有效维度和最低的各向异性。

Conclusion: ViT训练过程中表征几何呈现维度利用增加、各向异性降低的趋势，与直觉相反，信息并非集中在少数方向，而是分散在更多维度上。CLS token在表征几何中扮演特殊角色。

Abstract: Despite the widespread adoption of Vision Transformers (ViTs) and their success across numerous computer vision applications, the fundamental understanding of their dimensional and representational geometry remains relatively underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce Transformer Geometry Observatory (TGO), a systematic framework of experiments and analysis pipelines designed to investigate the representational geometry and dynamics of Vision Transformers. TGO-I, the first installment of the framework, focuses on the spectral geometry of ViT representations. Using a ViT-Small/16 model trained on ImageNet-100, we analyze Effective Rank, Stable Rank, Participation Ratio, Spectral Entropy, Spectral Flatness, Spectral Anisotropy, covariance structure, eigenspectra, and singular value spectra throughout training. Our results reveal a consistent increase in dimensional utilization, accompanied by decreasing anisotropy, increasing spectral entropy, increasing participation ratio, and progressively flatter eigenspectra. Contrary to the common intuition that training should concentrate information into a small number of dominant directions, we observe a progressive redistribution of variance across representational dimensions. This phenomenon is particularly pronounced in the final CLS token representation, which exhibits the highest effective dimensionality and lowest anisotropy within the network.

</details>


### [107] [OneCanvas: 3D Scene Understanding via Panoramic Reprojection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19253)
*Bartłomiej Baranowski,Dave Zhenyu Chen,Matthias Nießner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OneCanvas通过将所有视角的补丁特征聚合到单个等距柱状全景画布上，并添加3D位置嵌入，使预训练VLM能够像处理普通图像一样进行3D场景理解，无需复杂几何编码器或大量训练预算，在SQA3D和VSI-Bench上取得SOTA结果，计算量比最强竞争方法少一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM进行3D场景理解的方法要么依赖复杂的、模型特定的几何编码器，要么需要大量训练预算来追求空间推理能力，成本高昂且缺乏通用性。

Method: 1) 将各视角的补丁特征通过深度和相机姿态反投影到3D世界坐标，再映射到等距柱状全景画布的经纬度上，无需光栅化或跨视角聚合；2) 为每个补丁添加3D位置嵌入，保留因将世界位置压缩到角度画布坐标而丢失的深度信息；3) 预训练VLM将这种表示视为普通图像处理；4) 引入空间预训练课程：通过过程性放置从真实图像中提取的对象补丁特征到指定3D世界位置，生成空间推理任务的监督信号。

Result: 在SQA3D和VSI-Bench上达到最先进准确率，在SPBench上泛化到分布外数据，训练计算量比最强竞争方法少一个数量级。
