Conclusion: OneCanvas表明，通过巧妙的表示设计（全景画布+3D位置嵌入），可以无需融合或修改骨干网络架构，使标准VLM具备强大的3D空间推理能力，且计算效率显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Existing approaches to 3D scene understanding in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) either rely on complex, model-specific geometry encoders or large training budgets in pursuit of spatial reasoning. Instead, OneCanvas aggregates patch features from all views onto a single equirectangular panoramic canvas. Namely, each patch is unprojected to a 3D world coordinate using its depth and camera pose, then placed on the canvas at the continuous longitude and latitude of that point as seen from the canvas origin, with no rasterization or aggregation across overlapping views. A 3D position embedding of the patch's metric coordinates is added to its feature, restoring the depth lost when collapsing the world position to an angular canvas coordinate. Patches from all frames thus share one spatial coordinate system with no fusion or major architectural modifications of the backbone. The pretrained VLM consumes this representation as if it were an ordinary image. Because the canvas can be centered on any pose of interest, the same representation directly supports situated reasoning from a specific viewpoint, a common requirement in robotics and embodied AI. Thanks to this representation, we can also introduce a spatial pretraining curriculum: by procedurally placing patch features of objects, drawn from real images, at chosen 3D world positions on an otherwise empty canvas, we generate on-the-fly supervision spanning a broad range of spatial reasoning tasks, with answer distributions controlled to reduce spatial reasoning shortcuts. OneCanvas achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on SQA3D and VSI-Bench, and generalizes to out-of-distribution data on SPBench, using an order of magnitude less training compute than the strongest competing methods.

</details>


### [108] [CABLE: Cloud-Assisted Bandwidth-efficient LMM-based Encoding for V2X Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19258)
*Haohua Que,Zhipeng Bao,Qianyi Wu,Handong Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CABLE提出了一种云辅助的带宽高效LMM编码框架，通过自车运动补偿和残差运动线索传播前帧云分割掩码，构建鲁棒ROI，仅上传ROI掩码图像，形成“掩码→ROI→LMM”反馈循环，在五个数据集上实现73-87%的ROI像素覆盖率降低和5-8倍LMM预填充加速，检测质量损失较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云托管的大型多模态模型（LMM）可为车联网系统提供强大的开放词汇感知能力，但将全分辨率帧从边缘传输到云端会导致严重的通信开销和高云侧预填充延迟。

Method: 1）利用自车运动补偿在边缘传播前一帧的云分割掩码；2）通过残差运动线索进行细化；3）通过走廊包络合并断开区域，形成鲁棒的感兴趣区域（ROI）；4）仅上传ROI掩码图像，同时将云分割输出作为下一帧的先验反馈，形成掩码→ROI→LMM的闭环。

Result: 在nuScenes、WOD-ZB、Waymo、KITTI和CADC五个数据集上，实现73%-87%的ROI像素覆盖率降低，5-8倍LMM预填充加速，检测质量仅有适度折衷。

Conclusion: CABLE通过掩码传播与ROI压缩的反馈循环机制，有效降低了边缘-云端通信带宽和云侧计算延迟，同时保持了良好的感知性能。

Abstract: Cloud-hosted large multimodal models (LMMs) can provide strong open-vocabulary perception for Vehicle-to-Everything systems, but naively transmitting full-resolution frames from edge to cloud causes severe communication overhead and high cloud-side prefill latency. We present CABLE, a cloud-assisted bandwidth-efficient LMM-based encoding framework for edge-cloud perception. CABLE propagates the previous cloud segmentation mask on the edge using ego-motion compensation, refines it with residual-motion cues, and consolidates disconnected regions via a corridor envelope to form a robust region of interest (ROI). Only ROI-masked images are uploaded, while the cloud segmentation output is fed back as the prior for the next frame, forming a mask-to-ROI-to-LMM feedback loop. Experiments on five datasets (nuScenes, WOD-ZB, Waymo, KITTI, and CADC) show consistent communication savings while largely preserving perception, achieving $73$--$87\%$ ROI pixel-coverage reduction with $5$--$8\times$ estimated LMM prefill speedup at a modest detection-quality trade-off relative to full-frame inference.

</details>


### [109] [A Multi-Domain Benchmark for Detecting AI-Generated Text-Rich Images from GPT-Image-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19259)
*Yijin Wang,Shuyi Wang,Wenhan Zhang,Yuqi Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个包含8,602张文本丰富图像的多领域基准数据集，用于检测OpenAI GPT Image 2生成的图像。评估发现现有检测器性能高度依赖领域，且对JPEG压缩敏感，多模态大模型虽有潜力但在结构化格式上存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测基准主要集中在以物体为中心的图像上，对文本语义和布局组织至关重要的场景覆盖不足，而文本丰富的图像往往包含隐私敏感、交易相关或决策相关信息，因此亟需针对此类图像的检测基准和方法。

Method: 构建了包含8,602张图像的跨领域基准数据集，涵盖商业海报、信息图、学术海报、收据、表格和UI截图六类；在零样本设置下评估五种代表性AI生成图像检测器，分析整体性能、类别性能和后处理鲁棒性，并探索性评估了多模态视觉语言模型。

Result: 检测器性能高度依赖领域，在部分类别表现良好的方法在其他类别上往往失败；最强的传统检测器对JPEG压缩极其敏感；多模态大模型在结构化格式上既有潜力也存在局限。

Conclusion: 需要对文本和布局感知的AI生成图像检测方法，以应对现代文本丰富图像的检测挑战。

Abstract: Text-rich images often contain privacy-sensitive, transactional, or decision-relevant information. As recent multimodal image generation models become increasingly capable of synthesizing realistic textual content and structured visual designs, detecting AI-generated text-rich images has become an important challenge for digital trust and content authenticity. Existing benchmarks, however, largely focus on object-centric images and provide limited coverage of scenarios where textual semantics and layout organization are central. In this paper, we introduce a multi-domain benchmark for detecting text-rich images generated by OpenAI's GPT Image 2. The benchmark contains 8,602 images across six representative categories: commercial posters, infographics, academic posters, receipts, tables, and UI screenshots. Using this benchmark, we evaluate five representative AI-generated image detectors in a zero-shot setting and analyze their overall, category-wise, and post-processing robustness. Our results show that detector performance is highly domain-dependent: methods that perform well in some categories often fail on others, and even the strongest conventional detector exhibits severe sensitivity to JPEG compression. We further conduct an exploratory evaluation with a multimodal vision-language model, revealing both its promise and its limitations on structured formats. These findings highlight the need for text- and layout-aware detection methods for modern AI-generated images. Our dataset is released at XXX.

</details>


### [110] [A Unified Framework for Efficient Remote Sensing Visual Question Answering: Adapting Dual, Hybrid, and Encoder-Decoder Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19277)
*Timothy Agboada,Shikha Chandel,Yadav Raj Ghimire,Leila Hashemi-Beni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出RS Adapter，一种参数高效微调(PEFT)策略，应用于三种视觉语言模型架构（CLIP、BLIP、FLAVA）解决遥感领域VQA问题，仅用不到5%的可训练参数即可实现高效适配，实验表明混合架构FLAVA在多模态推理和检索方面表现最佳，为灾害评估和城市监测建立了资源高效的新基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感领域VQA面临高分辨率、多尺度目标分布和语义复杂性的独特挑战，通用基础模型因巨大的领域差异和全微调的高计算成本而难以直接应用，亟需一种参数高效且有效的适配方法。

Method: 提出RS Adapter PEFT策略，设计统一架构手术管道，在冻结骨干网络的注意力层和MLP层中注入轻量级瓶颈适配器，分别应用于双编码器CLIP、编码器-解码器BLIP和混合架构FLAVA三种VLM，仅训练不到5%的参数。

Result: 在RSVQA x数据集上的实验结果表明，所有适配模型均能收敛，其中混合架构FLAVA在多模态推理和检索能力上相比单模态对应架构表现出更优的平衡性能。

Conclusion: RS Adapter策略有效克服了遥感领域域偏移问题，FLAVA混合架构在资源受限条件下展现出最佳的多模态能力，为灾害评估和城市监测等应用场景提供了新的资源高效基线。

Abstract: Visual Question Answering (VQA) in the Remote Sensing (RS) domain presents unique challenges due to the high resolution, multi scale object distribution, and semantic complexity of aerial imagery. While general domain Foundation Models have achieved remarkable success, their direct application to RSVQA is hindered by massive domain shifts and the computationally prohibitive nature of full fine tuning. This study presents a comparative analysis of RS Adapter, a Parameter Efficient Fine Tuning (PEFT) strategy, applied across three distinct Vision Language Model (VLM) architectures: the Dual Encoder CLIP, the Encoder Decoder BLIP, and the Hybrid FLAVA. We introduce a unified architectural surgery pipeline that injects lightweight bottleneck adapters into the attention and MLP layers of frozen backbones, enabling rapid adaptation with less than 5 percent of trainable parameters. Experimental results on the high resolution RSVQA x dataset demonstrate that while all adapted models achieve convergence, the Hybrid FLAVA architecture offers a superior balance of multimodal reasoning and retrieval capabilities compared to its unimodal counterparts. Our findings establish a new baseline for resource efficient VQA in disaster assessment and urban monitoring.

</details>


### [111] [Confidence is Not Reliability: Rethinking MC Dropout in Brain Tumour Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19300)
*Xin Ci Wong,Duygu Sarikaya,Kieran Zucker,Marc De Kamps,Nishant Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过126例BraTS21患者的两模型病例研究，评估MC Dropout在胶质瘤多参数MRI分割中的体素级不确定性估计。发现MC Dropout在保持分割精度的同时实现了强不确定性-误差对齐（AUROC≈0.97），但全局对齐掩盖了关键区域差异：UNet-Res在增强肿瘤区域熵值近零且ECE达0.915，Dice仅0.714，表明标准Dice和AUROC指标无法检测出临床关键子区域的校准失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分割模型在治疗关键子区域上的静默失败是患者安全风险，而Dice等重叠指标无法暴露此类问题。研究旨在探究MC Dropout能否可靠识别临床关键子区域的分割错误，以及标准报告指标是否足以检测校准失败模式。

Method: 基于126例BraTS21患者，采用两模型（预训练SegResNet和本地训练UNet-Res）病例研究，通过MC Dropout进行体素级不确定性估计，评估熵与分割错误的对齐程度，并进行患者分层与子区域校准分析。

Result: MC Dropout保持了分割精度（|ΔDice|<0.01），实现了强不确定性-误差对齐（熵AUROC≈0.97）。基于熵的患者分层可识别出分割性能显著较低的高不确定性亚组。但UNet-Res在增强肿瘤区域熵值仅0.054、ECE高达0.915，Dice仅为0.714，表明存在严重校准失败。

Conclusion: 强不确定性-误差对齐是临床安全的必要条件但不充分：模型部署时必须在AUROC评估之外，附加子区域特定的校准评估。

Abstract: Glioma segmentation in multiparametric MRI is a critical component of treatment planning. A segmentation model that fails silently on treatment-critical sub-regions represents a patient safety risk that overlap-based metrics such as Dice scores cannot expose. We ask whether voxel-level uncertainty estimation via Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout can reliably identify segmentation errors in clinically critical sub-regions, and whether calibration failure modes are detectable from standard reporting metrics alone. In an empirical two-model case study on 126 BraTS21 patients, we evaluate a high-performance pretrained SegResNet and a locally trained UNet with residual units (UNet-Res). MC dropout preserved segmentation accuracy ($|Δ\text{Dice}|$ $<0.01$) while achieving strong uncertainty-error alignment (AUROC for entropy (H) $\approx$0.97), indicating uncertainty correctly ranks erroneous voxels above correct ones. Entropy-based patient stratification identified a high-uncertainty subgroup with substantially lower segmentation performance (median whole-tumour Dice $0.835$ vs. $0.925$), supporting uncertainty as a practical triage signal. However, global alignment can mask important region-specific differences. Despite similar AUROC, UNet-Res exhibited near-zero enhancing tumour entropy ($0.054$) and Expected Calibration Error (ECE) of $0.915$, with a Dice of only $0.714$, indicating severely miscalibrated confidence on the most clinically critical sub-region, a failure mode invisible to standard Dice and AUROC reporting. These findings demonstrate that strong uncertainty-error alignment is necessary but insufficient for clinical safety: sub-region-specific calibration assessment must accompany AUROC evaluation when selecting models for clinical deployment.

</details>


### [112] [NeuMesh++: Towards Versatile and Efficient Volumetric Editing with Disentangled Neural Mesh-based Implicit Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19316)
*Chong Bao,Yuan Li,Bangbang Yang,Yujun Shen,Hujun Bao,Zhaopeng Cui,Yinda Zhang,Guofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuMesh++，一种基于网格的神经隐式表示方法，通过将神经辐射场解耦为几何、纹理和语义编码，支持网格引导的几何编辑、纹理编辑（交换、填充、绘制）和语义引导编辑等多种功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经渲染编辑方法功能有限（仅支持刚体变换和类别特定编辑），缺乏高效全面的编辑能力。

Method: 提出基于网格顶点的解耦表示（几何、纹理、语义编码），包含局部空间参数化、可学习顶点颜色修改、空间感知优化策略和语义辅助区域选择等技术。

Result: 在真实和合成数据集上的实验表明，该方法在表示质量和编辑能力上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: NeuMesh++通过网格引导的隐式场解耦表示，实现了高效、全面的3D场景编辑功能，兼顾渲染质量与编辑灵活性。

Abstract: Recently neural implicit rendering techniques have evolved rapidly and demonstrated significant advantages in novel view synthesis and 3D scene reconstruction. However, existing neural rendering methods for editing purposes offer limited functionalities, e.g., rigid transformation and category-specific editing. In this paper, we present a novel mesh-based representation by encoding the neural radiance field with disentangled geometry, texture, and semantic codes on mesh vertices, which empowers a set of efficient and comprehensive editing functionalities, including mesh-guided geometry editing, designated texture editing with texture swapping, filling and painting operations, and semantic-guided editing. To this end, we develop several techniques including a novel local space parameterization to enhance rendering quality and training stability, a learnable modification color on vertex to improve the fidelity of texture editing, a spatial-aware optimization strategy to realize precise texture editing, and a semantic-aided region selection to ease the laborious annotation of implicit field editing. Extensive experiments and editing examples on both real and synthetic datasets demonstrate the superiority of our method on representation quality and editing ability. Project page: https://zju3dv.github.io/neumeshplusplus/

</details>


### [113] [Beyond the Current Observation: Evaluating Multimodal Large Language Models in Controllable Non-Markov Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19338)
*Shengyuan Ding,Xilin Wei,Xinyu Fang,Haodong Duan,Dahua Lin,Jiaqi Wang,Yuhang Zang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RNG-Bench是一个用于评估多模态基础模型在部分可观测环境中重建历史观测并基于此行动能力的基准套件，包含Matching Pairs和3D Maze两个互补游戏，通过Memory Gap指标发现模型主要错误源于遗忘而非次优决策。在Qwen3.5-9B上的微调提升了基准性能并泛化到其他基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么暴露完整状态，将隐状态重建与其他技能混为一谈，要么仅在回合结束后测试记忆，无法独立评估模型在部分可观测环境中重建历史观测并基于此行动的能力。

Method: 构建RNG-Bench基准套件，包含Matching Pairs（回忆特定位置短暂出现的卡片身份）和3D Maze（将自我中心视图整合为空间地图）两种互补游戏；采用统一评估框架，设置网格大小、视觉模式、观测模态三个难度维度；引入一对一对抗协议控制实例级方差，并设计Memory Gap指标区分遗忘与次优动作选择。

Result: 最困难配置需要约128K token和每回合350张图像输入，前沿MLLM远未饱和；Memory Gap分析表明残差错误主要源于遗忘早期观测；在Qwen3.5-9B上微调提升了RNG-Bench性能并泛化到现有基准，且不降低通用多模态能力。

Conclusion: RNG-Bench有效隔离并评估了基础模型在部分可观测交互中的记忆与重建能力，揭示了当前多模态大模型的记忆瓶颈，并为提升该能力提供了可行的微调路径。

Abstract: Deploying multimodal foundation models as closed-loop policies increasingly requires conditioning actions on observations that are no longer visible. However, existing benchmarks either expose the full state, conflate hidden-state reconstruction with other agent skills, or test recall only after an episode has ended. We introduce RNG-Bench (Reconstructive Non-Markov Games), a benchmark suite designed to isolate a base model's ability to reconstruct past observations and act on them during multi-step interaction. RNG-Bench includes two complementary games: Matching Pairs, where card identities briefly revealed at specific locations must later be recalled, and 3D Maze, where egocentric views must be integrated into a spatial map. Both games are evaluated under a unified harness with three controlled difficulty axes: grid size, visual pattern, and observation modality. The benchmark further introduces a head-to-head duel protocol to control for instance-level variance and a Memory Gap metric that disentangles forgetting from poor action selection. The hardest configurations require contexts of roughly 128K tokens and 350 image inputs per episode, and remain far from saturated by frontier MLLMs. Memory Gap analysis shows that most residual errors stem from forgetting earlier observations rather than from suboptimal decision making. Finally, fine-tuning Qwen3.5-9B on optimal-policy rollouts and filtered model demonstrations improves performance on RNG-Bench and transfers to existing benchmarks without degrading general multimodal capability.

</details>


### [114] [Native Active Perception as Reasoning for Omni-Modal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19341)
*Zhenghao Xing,Ruiyang Xu,Yuxuan Wang,Jinzheng He,Ziyang Ma,Qize Yang,Yunfei Chu,Jin Xu,Junyang Lin,Chi-Wing Fu,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniAgent提出了一种基于POMDP迭代式“观察-思考-行动”循环的原生全模态智能体，通过选择性提取音视频线索到持久文本记忆，将推理复杂度与视频时长解耦，采用智能体监督微调与强化学习（TAURA）实现主动感知，在十个基准上达到开源SOTA，7B模型在LVBench上超越10倍大的Qwen2.5-VL-72B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统长视频被动理解模型采用“全量观看”范式，计算成本随视频长度线性增长；现有交互式框架依赖全局预扫描，上下文成本仍随视频长度扩展。作者旨在通过主动感知机制将推理复杂度与原始视频时长解耦。

Method: (1) 将视频理解建模为POMDP驱动的迭代Observation-Thought-Action循环，按需执行动作选择性提取音视频线索到持久文本记忆；(2) Agentic Supervised Fine-Tuning：通过最佳N轨迹合成与双阶段质量控制引导原生主动感知；(3) Agentic Reinforcement Learning with TAURA：利用回合级熵值重新缩放优势函数，将信用分配导向关键发现回合。

Result: 在VideoMME、LVBench等十个基准上达到开源模型SOTA。在LVBench上，7B模型以50.5%超越Qwen2.5-VL-72B的47.3%。展现出正向测试时扩展特性，性能随推理回合数增加而提升。

Conclusion: OmniAgent验证了主动感知在长视频理解中的有效性，通过解耦推理复杂度与视频长度，实现了更高效且可扩展的视频理解范式，且较小模型即可超越大规模模型。

Abstract: Passive models for long video understanding typically rely on a "watch-it-all" paradigm, processing frames uniformly regardless of query difficulty, causing computational cost to grow with video duration. Although interactive frameworks have emerged, they often rely on global pre-scanning, and their context cost still scales with video length. We propose OmniAgent, the first native omni-modal agent that formulates video understanding as a POMDP-based iterative Observation-Thought-Action cycle. OmniAgent executes on-demand actions to selectively distill audio-visual cues into a persistent textual memory, effectively decoupling reasoning complexity from raw video duration. To operationalize this, we introduce (1) Agentic Supervised Fine-Tuning to bootstrap native active perception via best-of-N trajectory synthesis with dual-stage quality control, and (2) Agentic Reinforcement Learning with TAURA (Turn-aware Adaptive Uncertainty Rescaled Advantage), which leverages turn-level entropy to steer credit assignment toward pivotal discovery turns. Crucially, OmniAgent exhibits positive test-time scaling, where performance improves as the number of reasoning turns increases, validating the efficacy of active perception. Empirical results across ten benchmarks (e.g., VideoMME, LVBench) demonstrate that OmniAgent achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source models. Notably, on LVBench, our 7B agent outperforms the 10$\times$ larger Qwen2.5-VL-72B (50.5% vs. 47.3%).

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Conflict-Aware Retriever Editing for Knowledge Injection Attacks on LLM-Based RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18310)
*Xinru Liu,Xianglong Zhang,Di Cai,Zhumin Chen,Pengfei Hu,Xin Xin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CAREATTACK，一种以检索器为中心的RAG系统恶意知识注入攻击框架，通过冲突感知的参数编辑和攻击保持的锚点修复，在开放检索模型上实现高效攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG注入攻击主要依赖操纵外部知识库（数据中心方法），但合成文本容易被检测导致攻击失败；随着开源检索模型的普及，模型中心的攻击面（直接编辑检索器参数）尚待探索。

Method: CAREATTACK包含两阶段：1) 冲突感知检索器编辑：将闭式参数编辑适配至稠密检索模型，通过图基冲突检测和参数编辑投影解决参数冲突，提升恶意知识的检索排名；2) 攻击保持锚点修复：对编辑后的检索器进行轻量校准，消除对非目标提示的影响同时保持对目标提示的攻击效果。

Result: 在Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B和BGE-M3上，基于三个基准数据集的实验表明，该方法能显著提升恶意知识在RAG系统检索结果中的排名，并支持对批量目标提示和段落进行攻击。

Conclusion: 由于大多数RAG系统基于开源检索模型构建，本工作揭示了RAG系统中一种实用的攻击面，对AI安全具有警示意义。

Abstract: Injecting malicious knowledge into retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems can manipulate retrieved evidence and mislead downstream generation, posing a serious security threat for AI applications. Existing RAG injection attacks mainly rely on manipulating external knowledge bases, such as crafting malicious corpus. However, the synthetic text crafted by such data-centric methods could be detectable, leading to the failure of attacks. Beyond corpus manipulation, open-source retrievers are increasingly exposing RAG systems to model-centric attacks. In this paper, we propose conflict-aware retriever editing, i.e., CAREATTACK, a model-centric retriever attack framework for malicious knowledge injection in RAG. Specifically, CAREATTACK consists two stages of conflict-aware retriever editing and attack-preserving anchor repair. Conflict-aware retriever editing adapts efficient closed-form parameter editing to the dense retrieval model, promoting malicious knowledge above benign competing passages and resolving potential parameter conflicts through graph-based conflict detection and parameter editing projection. Then, attack-preserving anchor repair performs lightweight calibration on the edited retriever to further eliminate the impact on non-target prompts while preserving the attack effectiveness for target prompts. We instantiate CAREATTACK on Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B and BGE-M3, and conduct evaluation on three benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate our method substantially promote malicious passages into the retrieved knowledge of RAG systems and can perform attacks for batches of target prompts and passages, given the access of retrieval model parameters. Since most RAG systems are built upon open-source retrieval models, this work reveals a practical attack surface in RAG systems. Codes are public accessible at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CareAttack-3F1C.

</details>


### [116] [TIGER: Inverting Transformer Gradients via Embedding-Subspace Distance Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18312)
*William Kalikman,Ivo Petrov,Dimitar I. Dimitrov,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TIGER，一种基于连续优化的梯度反转攻击方法，通过将子空间信号转化为可微目标来优化token嵌入，在编码器模型上显著提升重建质量和速度，在解码器模型上首次实现DP防御下的成功重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度反转攻击在transformer模型上存在不足：基于优化虚拟输入的方法成本高且不稳定；基于注意力梯度低秩特性的离散token测试对数值噪声（如量化、差分隐私）脆弱，且在编码器模型中扩展性差。

Method: TIGER将子空间信号转化为可微的连续优化目标，直接优化token嵌入以最小化其到子空间的距离，替代了离散的token搜索或完整的梯度匹配。

Result: 在编码器模型上，TIGER显著提升了重建质量和运行速度；在解码器模型上，TIGER比现有子空间攻击更鲁棒，首次在DP防御的联邦学习设置中实现了成功重建。

Conclusion: TIGER作为一种连续优化的梯度反转攻击方法，有效克服了现有方法对数值噪声的脆弱性，在多种transformer架构和防御设置下均展现出更优的重建性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Federated learning allows multiple clients to jointly train a shared model by sending gradient updates to a central server while keeping raw inputs local. However, prior gradient inversion attacks show that these updates can reveal enough information to reconstruct client inputs. Existing attacks on transformers either optimize dummy inputs to match the true client updates, which is costly and unstable for modern models, or exploit the low rank of attention gradients to identify a subspace containing the true layer embeddings, followed by a discrete membership test for candidate tokens. However, this token test is brittle under numerical noise, i.e., from quantization or Differential Privacy (DP), and scales poorly for encoder models with non-causal attention. We introduce TIGER, a continuous gradient inversion attack that turns this subspace signal into a differentiable objective. Instead of searching over tokens or matching full gradients, TIGER directly optimizes token embeddings to minimize their distance to the subspace. Our experiments demonstrate that on encoder-only models, TIGER substantially improves both reconstruction quality and runtime over existing attacks, while on decoder models, TIGER is more robust than prior subspace-based attacks, enabling the first successful reconstructions in DP-defended federated learning settings.

</details>


### [117] [TopVenues: A Reproducible Corpus and Tooling Substrate for Cybersecurity Literature Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18320)
*Sidnei Barbieri,Ágney Lopes Roth Ferraz,Lourenço Alves Pereira Júnior*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TopVenues，一个开源系统，将文献语料库构建为版本化的可研究工件。它通过DBLP获取元数据，结合开放API和出版商提取器丰富记录，存储为SQLite快照，确保文献综述的可复现分母。2026年5月快照包含11个网络安全来源的9,925篇论文，摘要和BibTeX覆盖率分别达99.86%和99.99%，并支持可审计的网络安全测量分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全文献综述需要一个可复现的分母（筛选前的论文集合），但现有方法依赖出版商门户、书目索引和学术API，这些工具的覆盖范围、格式和查询语义随时间变化，导致综述难以复现和审计。

Method: TopVenues声明期刊和年份范围，以DBLP为元数据主干，通过开放学术API和出版商专用提取器丰富摘要和BibTeX条目，将结果存储为单调递增的SQLite快照，提供CLI、Web界面和导出路径。系统通过250项测试套件验证数据完整性。

Result: 2026年5月快照包含2017-2026年11个网络安全来源的9,925篇论文，摘要覆盖率99.86%，BibTeX覆盖率99.99%；全文关键词搜索<31ms。29.2%的2024-2025顶级安全会议论文以arXiv预印本形式出现（中位提前5个月），使用先前作者记录过滤器可在90%召回率下实现16.5倍精度提升。

Conclusion: TopVenues通过将语料库本身变为可执行、可检查和可引用的工件，将语料库构建与可审计的网络安全测量联系起来，使文献综述的分母固定且可复现。

Abstract: Cybersecurity literature reviews require a reproducible denominator: the set of papers that a protocol includes before screening and synthesis begin. Today, that denominator is often reconstructed from publisher portals, bibliographic indices, and scholarly application programming interfaces (APIs) whose coverage, formats, and query semantics change over time. This paper presents TopVenues, an open-source system that materializes corpus construction as a versioned research artifact. TopVenues declares a venue and year scope, uses DBLP Computer Science Bibliography (DBLP) as the metadata spine, enriches records with abstracts and BibTeX entries via open scholarly APIs and publisher-specific extractors, and stores the results in a monotonic SQLite snapshot, accessible via a command-line interface (CLI), a web interface, and export paths for review workflows. The May 2026 snapshot contains 9,925 papers from 11 cybersecurity sources over 2017 to 2026, with 99.86% abstract coverage and 99.99% BibTeX coverage; keyword search over the full corpus completes in under 31 ms, and a 250-test suite validates the data-integrity invariants. The fixed denominator also enables repeatable measurement: 29.2% of 2024 to 2025 papers from the four top-ranked security conferences in our scope appear as arXiv preprints, with a median of five months before publication, and a prior-author-track-record filter yields a 16.5x precision gain at 90% recall for triaging preprints that later appear in the same venue set. TopVenues links corpus construction to auditable cybersecurity measurement by making the corpus itself executable, inspectable, and citable. The artifact is available at https://github.com/sidneibarbieri/topVenues.

</details>


### [118] [Agentra: A Supervisable Multi-Agent Framework for Enterprise Intrusion Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18325)
*Raj Patel,Shaswata Mitra,Michele Guida,Stefano Iannucci,Sudip Mittal,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Agentra是一个可监督的多智能体入侵响应系统框架，通过角色化智能体、规划-验证循环、安全网关和风险评分机制，将告警转化为结构化的响应计划，在120事件数据集上将F1从0.61提升至0.84，并将有害动作率恢复至0.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业入侵响应仍依赖静态剧本和分析师手动分类，导致从告警生成到威胁遏制之间存在严重延迟，亟需自动化但可监督的响应机制。

Method: 提出Agentra框架，包含：(1) 基于MITRE ATT&CK、D3FEND和NIST CSF 2.0将告警转化为结构化响应计划；(2) 角色化多智能体分解响应推理；(3) Planner-Validator有界审查循环；(4) Moderator安全网关过滤威胁情报；(5) 基于Action Catalog和风险评分的门控动作执行；(6) 仅追加审计日志记录决策。

Result: 在ThreatHunter-Playbook、Splunk BOTSv3和DARPA OpTC构建的120事件语料上，最强配置将FP感知的IRS F1从0.61提升至0.84，将Planner-only配置下产生的有害动作率恢复至静态基线水平的0.0%。

Conclusion: 多智能体响应规划能够在保持分析师审批和可审计性的前提下，提升基于本体论的IRS覆盖范围，验证了框架的有效性和安全性。

Abstract: Enterprise intrusion response still depends on static playbooks and analyst-driven triage, creating delay between alert generation and containment. We present Agentra, a supervisable multi-agent Intrusion Response System (IRS) framework that converts alerts from IDS, EDR, and XDR platforms into structured incident response plans grounded in MITRE ATT&CK, MITRE D3FEND, and NIST CSF 2.0. Agentra decomposes response reasoning across role-scoped agents, validates proposed plans through a bounded Planner--Validator review loop, screens retrieved threat intelligence through a Moderator security gateway, gates actions through an Action Catalog and risk score, and records decisions in an append-only audit log. We evaluate Agentra against a static OASIS CACAO v2.0 cyber-playbook baseline on a 120-event corpus drawn from ThreatHunter-Playbook, Splunk BOTSv3, and DARPA OpTC. The strongest configuration improves FP-aware IRS F1 from 0.61 to 0.84 and restores the projected harmful-action rate to the static baseline level of 0.0% after Planner-only configurations introduce unsafe overreaction. These results indicate that multi-agent response planning can improve ontology-grounded IRS coverage while preserving analyst approval and auditability.

</details>


### [119] [SafeClawBench: Separating Semantic, Audit-Evidence, and Sandbox Harm in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18356)
*Yuchuan Tian,Mengyu Zheng,Haocheng Mei,Ye Yuan,Chao Xu,Xinghao Chen,Hanting Chen,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeClawBench 是一个面向工具使用型语言模型代理的安全性分阶段基准测试，包含600个对抗性任务，分别评估语义攻击接受度、审计可见危害证据和沙箱观察工具/状态危害三个端点。研究发现，不同端点捕捉不同失败模式，部分通过语义检查的任务仍会产生沙箱危害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估将工具使用型语言模型代理的安全失败（如泄露对象、写入内存、发送消息、修改数据库、触发有害代码等）压缩为单一攻击成功率，无法区分模型是仅仅同意了攻击者还是实际产生了可观察的危害。

Method: 构建 SafeClawBench 基准，包含600个跨六类攻击家族（直接/间接提示注入、工具返回注入、记忆投毒、记忆提取、歧义驱动不安全推理）的受控对抗任务。报告三个独立端点：语义攻击接受度、审计可见危害证据、沙箱观察工具/状态危害。在五个代理端点和四种提示策略下进行评估。

Result: 不同端点捕捉不同的失败模式。无额外提示保护时，语义失败率在9.0%至44.2%之间变化。审计危害证据范围小于语义失败。在12,000行匹配分析中，347个沙箱危害中有291个出现在语义检查通过的行中。

Conclusion: SafeClawBench 提供了一个可复现的框架，用于比较代理模型和提示策略条件，避免将文本合规性、证据支持危害和可执行状态变化混为一谈。

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents introduce security failures that go beyond unsafe text: they can disclose protected objects, write persistent memory, send messages, modify databases, or trigger harmful code and tool effects. Existing evaluations often collapse these stages into a single attack success rate, making it difficult to tell whether a model merely agreed with an attacker or actually produced observable harm. We introduce SafeClawBench, a staged benchmark for tool-using agent security with 600 controlled adversarial tasks across six attack families: direct and indirect prompt injection, tool-return injection, memory poisoning, memory extraction, and ambiguity-driven unsafe inference. SafeClawBench reports three separate endpoints: semantic attack acceptance, audit-visible harm evidence, and sandbox-observed tool/state harm. Evaluating five agent endpoints under four prompt-level policies, we find that these endpoints capture different failure modes. Without additional prompt protection, semantic failure rates vary widely across models, from 9.0% to 44.2%. Audited harm evidence is narrower than semantic failure, and under a separate executable protocol some matched task identities produce sandbox harm despite passing the Semantic Core call: in a 12,000-row matched analysis, 291 of 347 observed sandbox harms occur in rows that pass the semantic check. Prompt policies change endpoint outcomes, but their effects depend on both model and protocol. SafeClawBench provides a reproducible framework for comparing agent models and prompt-policy conditions without conflating textual compliance, evidence-supported harm, and executable state changes. The open-source dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/sairights/safeclawbench.

</details>


### [120] [Evaluating the Effectiveness of LLMs in Aiding Compliance Testing of PKCS#1-v1.5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18405)
*Polina Kozyreva,Endadul Hoque*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究基于语法级变异和LLM代码合成的方法，用于二进制协议规范合规性测试。在PKCS#1 v1.5签名验证的48个密码库实现中，成功复现了10/13个规范违规类别，并发现1个新差异。主要限制因素是LLM幻觉（82.5%的生成脚本存在幻觉），而非变异策略本身。识别出5种幻觉类型，且其分布随变异类别系统性地变化：结构变异保真度13.3%，约束变异正确率30.3%但完全忽略变异率8.1%。揭示了操作可靠性（99.8%）与语义保真度（17.5%）之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二进制协议实现需要同时满足结构约束和语义约束的测试输入，纯随机生成和原始变异方法不足以探索TLV编码协议中有意义的行为，而领域特定的合规测试工具需要深度协议专业知识和大量人工构建。本文旨在探索语法级变异结合LLM代码合成是否是一种更通用的规范合规测试方法。

Method: 采用语法级变异与LLM代码合成相结合的方法，以PKCS#1 v1.5签名验证（广泛部署的TLV编码标准，具有形式化验证的测试预言Morpheus）为评估对象，在48个密码库实现上进行实验。系统性地分析LLM幻觉对方法有效性的影响，识别幻觉类型并考察其在不同变异类别中的分布差异。

Result: 成功复现了10/13个非平凡规范违规类别（包括全部5个签名伪造类别），发现1个先前未报告的差异。82.5%的生成脚本存在LLM幻觉。结构变异保真度仅13.3%，约束变异正确率30.3%但有8.1%完全忽略变异。操作可靠性为99.8%，但语义保真度仅17.5%。

Conclusion: 语法级变异结合LLM代码合成在规范合规测试中具有一定可行性，但LLM幻觉是主要瓶颈。操作可靠性与语义保真度之间存在巨大差距，需要针对性地处理幻觉问题才能在实际规范驱动的测试管线中信任LLM代码合成。

Abstract: Testing implementations of binary protocols for specification compliance requires inputs that satisfy both structural and semantic constraints. Purely random generation and primitive mutations are often insufficient for exploring semantically meaningful behaviors in protocols that rely on Type-Length-Value (TLV) encoding, yet domain-specific compliance testing tools require deep protocol expertise and significant manual effort to construct. This work investigates whether grammar-level mutation combined with LLM-based code synthesis can serve as a viable, more generalizable approach to specification compliance testing.
  We evaluate the approach on PKCS#1 v1.5 signature verification -- a widely deployed TLV-encoded standard with a formally verified testing oracle (Morpheus) -- across 48 cryptographic library implementations. We reproduced 10 of 13 non-trivial specification violation categories previously identified by Morpheus, including all 5 signature forgery categories, and discovered 1 previously unreported discrepancy. We found that LLM hallucination -- occurring in 82.5% of generated scripts -- is the primary factor limiting effectiveness, not the mutation strategies. We identify five distinct hallucination types and show that their distribution varies systematically across mutation categories: structural mutations are implemented with 13.3% fidelity while constraint mutations achieve 30.3% correctness but suffer the highest rate of mutations being fully ignored (8.1%). These findings reveal a striking gap between operational reliability (99.8%) and semantic fidelity (17.5%), providing actionable guidance on when LLM-based code synthesis can be trusted in specification-driven testing pipelines.

</details>


### [121] [Understanding the "Airport" Censorship Circumvention Ecosystem in China](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18427)
*Rumaisa Habib,Mingshi Wu,Shiva Shahandeh,Min Ni,Eric Wustrow,Zakir Durumeric*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次系统研究中国“机场”（订阅制翻墙代理）生态系统，发现其是当前中国最主流的翻墙工具，超过半数受访者使用，具有多跳架构和高性能特点，但也面临政府打压、安全风险和自身审查政策等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中国地下市场存在大量称为“机场”的订阅制翻墙代理，但学界对此生态系统缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 结合用户调查（1,667份问卷）、社交媒体分析和主动网络测量三种方法，通过互联网扫描和Telegram公告频道抓取识别活跃机场，并订阅35个机场进行性能特征分析。

Result: 识别出3,431个活跃机场，发现其基于少数开源工具包构建；性能通常超越直接连接；机场使用支付宝等商业支付，频繁遭受政府打击，且部署自身审查策略。

Conclusion: 机场的使用远超学界研究的其他翻墙工具，但引入了新的脆弱性和控制形式，为未来翻墙研究提供了经验教训与机遇。

Abstract: In China, a burgeoning underground market sells citizens subscription-based censorship circumvention proxies known as ''airports''. We present the first systematic study of this ecosystem, combining user surveys, social media analysis, and active network measurements. We find that airports are by far the most popular off-the-shelf censorship circumvention tool in China, used by over half of our 1,667~survey respondents, who cite their ease of use, performance, and access to geo-restricted services like ChatGPT and Netflix. By scanning the Internet and scraping Telegram announcement channels, we identify 3,431 active airports built on a handful of open-source toolkits. We subscribe to 35 airports and characterize their performance, which often surpasses direct connections through the Great Firewall due to a distinctive multi-hop architecture. However, airports also pose new challenges and security risks: they accept payment through commercial services like Alipay, suffer frequent government takedowns, and are difficult for clients to configure optimally. Many airports also deploy their own distinct censorship policies. Airports are far more widely used than other circumvention tools from the academic literature, but introduce new forms of fragility and control, offering both lessons and opportunities for future circumvention research.

</details>


### [122] [Ghost Vectors: Soft-Deleted Embeddings Remain Reconstructible in HNSW Vector Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18497)
*Chandranil Chakraborttii,Jackeline García Alvarado,Sitora Abdulofizova,Shivanshu Dwivedi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了HNSW向量数据库中软删除操作的安全漏洞——被标记删除的嵌入向量在存储层仍可物理恢复，并能在多种数据模态（文本、图像）上成功恢复个人身份信息（PII）。作者提出"时期密钥轮换"（Epoch Key Rotation）方案，通过加密向量并在删除时丢弃密钥，将PII恢复率降至0%，同时生成ECDSA签名加密证明作为可审计删除记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代RAG系统使用HNSW向量数据库进行相似性搜索，但当用户请求数据删除时，系统仅进行软删除（标记删除），而嵌入向量在磁盘上物理保持不变。这违反了GDPR第17条（被遗忘权）和HIPAA等数据擦除与保留合规要求。论文旨在揭示这一安全漏洞并提出解决方案。

Method: 1) 对三种HNSW实现进行安全性分析，确认删除向量可从原始索引文件中物理恢复；2) 使用Vec2Text反转模型（无需领域微调）在多种真实数据集上演示恢复攻击；3) 提出"时期密钥轮换"机制：加密所有向量，每次删除操作后生成新加密密钥并丢弃旧密钥，同时生成ECDSA签名加密证明记录删除事件。

Result: 在Wikipedia BLP数据集中恢复25.5%精确人名和46.4%地理位置（ROUGE-L 0.185）；在NIH Synthea高度结构化敏感数据中，患者年龄和性别标记恢复率达100%（ROUGE-L 0.290）；图像嵌入方面，组织病理学切片组织分类恢复100%（p=1.02e-07），面部嵌入Top-1身份恢复达99%（p<0.01）。时期密钥轮换将PII恢复率降至0%，处理500个删除向量仅需2.5ms（约0.005ms/记录）。

Conclusion: HNSW向量数据库的软删除机制存在严重安全漏洞，可导致PII泄露。提出的时期密钥轮换方案能以极低计算开销（0.005ms/记录）彻底消除这一风险，并提供可审计的加密删除证明，满足GDPR等合规要求。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) allows large language models to access external and private corpora for factual, domain-specific responses. Modern RAG pipelines use hierarchical navigable small world (HNSW) vector databases for efficient similarity search. When a user requests data deletion, the systems typically only mark the record as deleted, leaving the embedding on disk physically unchanged. This soft-delete operation raises compliance concerns under data-erasure and retention requirements such as GDPR Article 17 and HIPAA. Analysis on three HNSW implementations confirms that deleted vectors remain physically recoverable by accessing the raw index files at the storage layer, bypassing API access. Using the Vec2Text inversion model without domain-specific fine-tuning, we show this vulnerability on multiple real-world datasets and data modalities. On Wikipedia biographical living persons dataset (BLP), we successfully recover 25.5% of exact person names and 46.4% of geographic locations (ROUGE-L 0.185). Recovery reaches 100% for both patient age and gender markers (ROUGE-L 0.290) on highly structured, sensitive data (NIH Synthea dataset). On soft-deleted image embeddings, we show 100% tissue classification on histopathology patches (p=1.02e-07) and top-1 identity recovery reaches 99% on facial embeddings (p<0.01). This work introduces Epoch Key Rotation, which encrypts vectors and discards the key upon deletion. Epoch key rotation reduces observed PII recovery to 0% and completes in 2.5 ms for 500 deleted vectors (approximately 0.005 ms/record). Additionally, it generates an ECDSA-signed cryptographic proof as an auditable record of the deletion event.

</details>


### [123] [From Bits to Mixed-Radix Keys: Horner Decomposition, Uniform Sampling, and the Information-Theoretic QKD Interface of the MR-OTP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18526)
*Fabio F. G. Buono*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出混合基数一次性密码本（MR-OTP），将经典OTP扩展到异构字母表同时保持完善保密性。通过Horner方法及其逆映射实现QKD原始二进制熵到均匀混合基数密钥的无偏转换，证明拒绝采样可恢复均匀性且具有最优期望成本，建立了单会话和多会话管道的端到端信息论安全性，量化了效率增益，并给出了批处理提取器及基恢复问题的无条件与条件性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典OTP仅适用于同质字母表（如二进制），而实际应用中常需处理不同长度/基数的字母表（如字母、数字、符号混合）。现有方法直接使用模运算会引入偏差，破坏完善保密性。因此需要一种能在异构字母表上保持完美保密性的实用方案。

Method: 1) 识别Horner方法及其逆映射为二进制整数与混合基数元组之间的自然双射；2) 证明朴素模归约会引入偏差，提出拒绝采样法恢复均匀性并证明其最优期望成本；3) 构建端到端信息论安全模型，覆盖单会话与多会话QKD管道；4) 设计批处理提取器提升效率；5) 分析基恢复问题的无条件与条件性结果。

Result: 1) 证明了Horner映射是实现无偏转换的正确方法；2) 拒绝采样能在最优期望成本下消除偏差；3) 建立了完整的端到端信息论安全性证明；4) 量化了相比传统方法的效率提升；5) 批处理提取器进一步降低了通信开销；6) 给出了基恢复问题的理论界限。

Conclusion: MR-OTP将OTP的完善保密性从同质字母表成功推广到混合基数场景，提供了完整的理论框架和实用实现方案，为QKD等物理层安全方案在异构通信环境中的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: The Mixed-Radix One-Time Pad (MR-OTP) extends the classical OTP to heterogeneous alphabets while preserving perfect secrecy. We provide a practical, bias-free method to convert raw binary entropy from a QKD source into uniform mixed-radix keys by identifying Horner's method and its inverse as the natural mapping between binary integers and mixed-radix tuples. We show that naive modular reduction induces bias and prove that rejection sampling restores uniformity with optimal expected cost. We establish end-to-end information-theoretic security for single and multi-session pipelines, quantify efficiency gains, present a batched extractor, and give unconditional and conditional results on the Base Recovery Problem.

</details>


### [124] [Evaluating Prompting-Based Defenses Against Domain-Camouflaged Injection Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18530)
*Aaditya Pai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了五种提示工程防御方法（重点标注、释义、提示夹层及两种组合）抵御域伪装注入攻击的效果，在三个模型族和三个部署域共3510次试验中，发现释义法最有效（降低攻击成功率55-84%），防御效果高度依赖模型，金融领域面临最高的残余风险（26-33%基线攻击成功率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域伪装注入攻击使用领域专业词汇将恶意指令嵌入检索内容中，能够规避依赖语法注入标记的标准检测器。当检测失效时，实践者需要了解哪些防御架构能降低攻击成功率，但目前缺乏对此类攻击的提示工程防御的系统性评估。

Method: 在Claude Haiku、Llama 3.1 8B、Gemini 2.0 Flash三个模型族和金融、法律、通用三个部署域上，通过3510次试验，评估了五种提示工程防御方法（重点标注、释义、提示夹层及两种组合）抵御域伪装注入攻击的效果，并与Llama Guard 4配置进行对比。

Result: 释义法是最一致有效的防御手段，可将伪装攻击成功率降低55-84%（取决于模型）；在所有测试模型上，其攻击成功率均低于Llama Guard 4配置。防御效果高度依赖模型：重点标注在Claude Haiku上使攻击成功率减半，但在Llama 3.1 8B上无效。金融域面临最高残余风险（26-33%基线攻击成功率），弱模型上无提示工程防御能完全消除威胁。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统评估了针对伪装类注入攻击的提示工程防御方法，建立了基准推荐方案。但所有任务均使用合成构建的专业文档，这些基准排名是否能推广到真实企业文档仍有待验证。

Abstract: Domain-camouflaged injection attacks embed malicious instructions in retrieved content using domain-appropriate vocabulary, evading standard detectors that rely on syntactic injection markers. When detection fails, practitioners need to know which defense architectures reduce attack success. We evaluate five prompting-based defenses (spotlighting, paraphrasing, prompt sandwiching, and two combinations) against domain-camouflaged injection across three model families (Claude Haiku, Llama 3.1 8B, Gemini 2.0 Flash) and three deployment domains (financial, legal, general) using 3,510 trials. Paraphrasing retrieved content before agent processing is the most consistently effective defense in this benchmark, reducing camouflage attack success rate by 55-84\% depending on model, and achieves lower attack success rates than our Llama Guard 4 configuration on every model tested. Defense effectiveness is strongly model-dependent: spotlighting halves attack success on Claude Haiku but provides no benefit on Llama 3.1 8B. Financial domain deployments face the highest residual risk at 26-33\% baseline attack success rate, with no prompting-based defense fully eliminating the threat on weaker models. These results provide the first systematic evaluation of prompting-based defenses specifically against camouflage-class injection attacks and establish benchmark-based recommendations for practitioners. All tasks use synthetically constructed professional documents; whether these benchmark rankings generalize to real enterprise documents remains an open question.

</details>


### [125] [AI Sandboxes: A Threat Model, Taxonomy, and Measurement Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18532)
*Inderjeet Singh,Haitham Mahmoud,Andrés Murillo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统性地提出了AI沙盒的保障性分析框架，形式化了沙盒边界与最弱链接规则，区分了主要沙盒原型，定义了包含对保障装置本身攻击的网络-物理威胁模型，并建立了涵盖保真度、可控性、可观测性、可复现性等维度的测量框架，通过三个真实案例验证了该框架对安全、安保和监管保障的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统（特别是物理AI、AIoT和网络-物理系统）越来越多地在结合隔离、仿真、仪器化、监督和证据捕获的有界环境中进行评估，现有工作缺乏对沙盒作为保障工具的正式化理解，亟需一个系统性的保障导向框架来阐明沙盒能有效测试什么、能控制哪些风险、以及能支持何种形式的证据。

Method: 本文采用理论形式化与案例分析相结合的方法：首先形式化沙盒边界及基于最弱链接规则的跨维度证据组合方式；划分主要沙盒原型；定义包含元攻击（对保障装置本身的攻击）的网络-物理威胁模型；建立涵盖保真度、可控性、可观测性、可复现性、治理工件等维度的测量框架；并通过三个真实沙盒案例进行实证验证。

Result: 提出了完整的AI沙盒保障分析框架，包括：(1)沙盒边界形式化与最弱链接证据组合规则；(2)沙盒原型分类学；(3)包含元攻击的网络-物理威胁模型；(4)多维度测量框架；(5)三个真实案例的验证结果，证明了框架对安全、安保和监管保障的支持能力。

Conclusion: 本文的威胁模型、分类学和测量框架明确了沙盒的有效测试范围、可控风险类型以及可支持的证据形式，为AI系统特别是物理AI和网络-物理系统的安全、安保和监管保障提供了系统的理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly evaluated in bounded environments that combine isolation, simulation, instrumentation, supervision, and evidence capture. For physical AI, AIoT, and cyber-physical systems, this shift is not a matter of terminology: the system under test may sense, decide, actuate, communicate, and fail through physical processes, networked devices, and human operators. This article develops an assurance-oriented account of AI sandboxes as controlled environments for testing, evaluation, verification, and validation across digital AI, embodied autonomy, and cyber-physical deployments. We formalize the sandbox boundary and a weakest-link rule for composing per-dimension evidence into a bounded deployment claim; separate major sandbox archetypes; define a cyber-physical threat model that includes attacks on the assurance apparatus itself; and introduce a measurement framework spanning fidelity, controllability, observability, containment, reproducibility, and governance artifacts, instantiated on three worked case studies of real sandboxes. The resulting threat model, taxonomy, and measurement framework clarify what a sandbox can validly test, which risks it can contain, and what forms of evidence it can support for safety, security, and regulatory assurance.

</details>


### [126] [Confident yet Concerned: Inconsistencies in Computing Students' Attitudes on Cybersecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18541)
*Victor Adama,Robert Biddle,Nalin Arachchilage,Danielle Lottridge*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 通过对236名计算机专业大学生的调查，发现他们虽具备一定网络安全意识，但存在错误认知、学习来源以课外为主、曾遭受网络犯罪侵害、以及对自身安全信心和担忧并存等矛盾。聚类分析识别出两类群体，其中一类感到准备不足、信心较低但渴望学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代年轻人深度沉浸在技术中，但同时在管理多平台在线隐私方面感到无力，且特别容易受到钓鱼攻击。这引发了对他们网络安全整体态度和管理的疑问，本文旨在了解年轻技术用户如何应对网络安全问题。

Method: 对236名计算机专业大学生（作为"领先用户"）进行问卷调查，结合开放式回答的定性主题分析与定量数据，并通过聚类分析识别态度群体。

Result: 学生具备合理的网络安全意识，但经验差异大，在实践、责任认知和支持结构方面存在不一致。发现四个关键主题张力：1)知识丰富但有持续错误信念；2)安全知识主要来自课堂外；3)曾受害且获得帮助有限；4)部分自信部分担忧。聚类分析识别出两个群体，其中一个感到准备不足、信心较低但渴望学习。意图和客观知识的测量与准备度相关，自我效能感与信心相关并可预测聚类归属。

Conclusion: 年轻技术用户的网络安全认知和实践存在显著矛盾，需要超越课堂教育，提供更多实践支持和针对性干预，特别是针对缺乏信心但有意愿学习的群体。

Abstract: Today's young adults are most immersed in technology, leading in feelings of powerlessness in managing online privacy across many platforms, and particularly susceptible to phishing attacks. This raises questions about their general, wide-ranging attitudes towards and management of cybersecurity. How do young, tech-savvy adults approach cybersecurity? We seek a better understanding of their cybersecurity knowledge, attitudes and experiences, in particular in addressing deceptive online communications. We surveyed a group of `lead users': computing university students (n = 236). By combining thematic analysis of open-ended responses with quantitative data, we provide insights into their experiences and perceptions. While students demonstrate reasonable cybersecurity awareness, their cybersecurity experiences vary, and inconsistencies exist around their practices, perceptions of responsibility, and support structures. Findings also reveal four key thematic tensions: 1) Computing students are knowledgeable yet have persistent incorrect beliefs, 2) They learn more about keeping safe from sources outside the classroom, 3) They have limited assistance and have fallen victim to cybercrime, and 4) Many are confident, yet others are concerned about their own safety and responsibility. Through cluster analysis of attitudes, we identify two groups, with one feeling less prepared, less confident, yet expressing a desire to learn more. Established measures of intentions and objective knowledge were correlated to preparedness. Self-efficacy correlated to confidence and predicted cluster membership.

</details>


### [127] [The Gate Is Only as Honest as Its Contracts: ContractGuard for the Contract Layer of Risk-Aware Causal Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18550)
*Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer,Rahul Suresh Babu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出风险感知因果门控（RACG）机制，通过从智能体可见动作空间移除危险工具来防御间接提示注入，并指出该结构保证的关键在于工具合约（前置条件、效应、风险、授权）的完整性，而非风险标签。作者进一步引入ContractGuard验证器，在基准测试中实现了对多种攻击的零注入成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强型LLM智能体易受间接提示注入攻击，攻击者通过操纵工具合约可使RACG门控机制做出错误决策，因此需要更 robust 的合约完整性保障机制。

Method: RACG采用因果门控先于准入门控的双层结构，仅将处于因果路径上的工具暴露给智能体；ContractGuard在注册中心与门控之间增加验证层，包括签名来源、类型化合约认证和运行时效应验证。

Result: ContractGuard在受控基准上对所有建模攻击（包括穷举白盒自适应攻击）实现零注入成功率，且不过度拒绝诚实合约。该结构预测在六种当前代托管模型上得到验证。

Conclusion: 工具效应的完整性（而非风险标签）是RACG安全性的核心承载假设；ContractGuard通过多层验证有效弥补了该假设被破坏时的安全缺口。

Abstract: Risk-Aware Causal Gating (RACG) defends tool-augmented LLM agents against indirect prompt injection by removing dangerous tools from the agent's visible action space, so that even a fully injection-compliant agent cannot call a tool it cannot see. We make three points. First, this structural guarantee does not eliminate the trust assumption behind safe tool use; it relocates it into the integrity of the tool contracts -- declared preconditions, effects, risk, and authorization -- that the gate reads, so an attacker who corrupts a contract can make the gate mis-decide without ever persuading the agent. Second, forging a tool's effects is strictly more dangerous than tampering with its risk label, because RACG applies a causal gate before its admissibility gate: an off-path tool is never exposed, so risk-relabeling alone fails, whereas effect forgery routes the dangerous tool onto the causal path and succeeds. Effect integrity, not the risk label, is the load-bearing assumption. Third, we introduce ContractGuard, a verifier between the registry and the gate that layers signed provenance, typed contract attestation, and runtime effect verification; on a controlled benchmark it restores injection success to zero against every modeled attack -- including an exhaustive white-box adaptive attacker -- without over-rejecting honest contracts, and the structural prediction is confirmed on six current-generation hosted models (Claude Opus 4.8, Sonnet 4.6, Haiku 4.5; Amazon Nova Premier and Nova 2 Lite; GPT-OSS-120B).

</details>


### [128] [QSignAI: Quantum-Randomness-Seeded Identity Signatures at the Intersection of AI for Science and Science for AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27729)
*Dongping Liu,Aoyu Zhang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍QSignAI，一个在生产环境中部署的双向量子-AI交互平台，通过对话机器人结合量子随机数生成（Toeplitz双源提取器、Hadamard测量、Bell态）为每个参与者生成独特的量子随机身份签名，并在实时活动中成功验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管2024-2025年诺贝尔和图灵奖同时表彰了AI和量子科学，但尚无实际部署的系统将二者结合服务于公众。作者希望弥合这一空白，验证AI与量子技术双向融合的可行性。

Method: 构建QSignAI平台，通过对话机器人路由用户消息，经过量子流水线（包括基于SV1和DM1模拟器上独立单比特Hadamard测量的Toeplitz双源提取器，以及2比特Bell态），为每位参与者生成独特的量子随机性种子身份签名。

Result: 前两个问题通过系统架构和现场活动的定性部署证据得到回答；第三个问题通过成功的生产部署得到验证。当前部署使用云端量子模拟器，物理QPU随机性是近期扩展方向。

Conclusion: QSignAI成功展示了AI与量子计算在实际生产系统中的双向融合，使量子现象对普通受众变得可感知，但可量化基准测试被列为优先未来工作。

Abstract: The 2024-2025 Nobel and Turing awards recognised AI and quantum science simultaneously. Yet no deployed system has brought these streams together for the public. This paper presents QSignAI, a production-deployed platform demonstrating a bidirectional AI-quantum relationship in a real-time event participation system. We address three questions: can quantum-randomness generation via a two-source extractor be embedded in an AI-driven social platform with acceptable latency; can an AI bot make quantum phenomena perceptually legible to general audiences; and does the combined system work in practice? A conversational bot routes each participant's first message through a quantum pipeline comprising a Toeplitz two-source extractor over independent single-qubit Hadamard measurements on SV1 and DM1 simulators, plus a 2-qubit Bell state, producing a unique quantum-randomness-seeded identity signature per participant. The first two questions are answered through system architecture and qualitative deployment evidence from live events; the third through successful production deployment. The current deployment uses cloud quantum simulators; physical QPU randomness is the near-term extension. Measurable benchmarks are identified as priority future work.

</details>


### [129] [MIDS: Detecting Stealthy Masquerade and Tampering Attacks on CAN Bus via Bidirectional Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18599)
*Qiqi Liu,Runhan Song,Lei Cui,Heng Zhang,Yuyan Sun,Limin Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Mamba入侵检测系统(MIDS)，采用双流双向选择性状态空间模型处理CAN标识符和载荷，针对更难检测的伪装攻击（替代合法帧而非注入新帧），在特斯拉Model 3采集的超过1亿条CAN帧和54种伪装攻击变体上达到96.94%的F1分数，并在四个公开基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAN总线入侵检测系统主要针对注入式攻击（DoS、模糊测试、ID欺骗），利用帧间到达间隔等统计特征即可检测。然而内部对手发起的伪装攻击（在原发送时隙替换合法帧）保留了流量周期性，使基于流量统计的防御失效。本文旨在解决这一更具挑战性的伪装攻击检测问题。

Method: 提出MIDS（Mamba入侵检测系统），采用创新的双流框架：并行处理CAN标识符和载荷数据，通过双向选择性状态空间建模重建它们的联合时间语义。使用Mamba模型（一种状态空间模型）进行时序建模。

Result: 在特斯拉Model 3采集的超过1亿条CAN帧数据集上，针对54种伪装攻击变体（涵盖仅ID修改、仅数据修改、组合修改）达到96.94%的F1分数，比最强可复现基线高出8个百分点以上。单窗口推理延迟1.147ms，满足实时部署需求。在ROAD、CrySyS、OTIDS、CT&T四个公开基准上F1达93.70%-99.61%，在统一5折协议下比8个复现基线最高提升13.94个百分点。

Conclusion: MIDS有效解决了CAN总线伪装攻击检测这一难题，在真实车辆数据和多个公开基准上均显著超越现有方法，且推理延迟极低，适合实时车载部署，具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: The Controller Area Network (CAN) protocol is the primary communication standard for Electronic Control Units (ECUs) in modern vehicles, but its lack of encryption and authentication exposes it to a range of security threats. Existing intrusion detection systems are largely tuned to fabrication-style attacks (DoS, fuzzing, ID spoofing realised by frame injection), in which detection signals such as per-ID inter-arrival statistics are readily available. We instead address the harder \emph{masquerade} setting~\cite{b37}, in which an internal adversary substitutes a legitimate frame in-situ at its original transmission slot, preserving traffic periodicity and rendering traffic-statistic defences ineffective. We propose the Mamba Intrusion Detection System (MIDS), an innovative dual-stream framework that processes CAN identifiers and payloads in parallel and reconstructs their joint temporal semantics through bidirectional selective state-space modelling. To evaluate MIDS, we collected over 100 million CAN frames from a physical Tesla Model 3 across three driving regimes and synthesised 54 masquerade attack variants spanning ID-only, data-only, and combined modifications. MIDS attains an F1 of 96.94\% on this dataset, exceeding the strongest reproducible baseline by more than 8 percentage points, while sustaining a 1.147~ms single-window inference latency -- ample headroom for real-time onboard deployment. To verify generalisation, we further evaluate MIDS on four public benchmarks (ROAD, CrySyS, OTIDS, CT\&T) covering both masquerade and injection scenarios; MIDS attains F1 from 93.70\% to 99.61\%, outperforming the strongest of eight reproduced baselines by up to 13.94 percentage points under a unified 5-fold protocol.

</details>


### [130] [Code-Augur: Agentic Vulnerability Detection via Specification Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18619)
*Zhengxiong Luo,Mehtab Zafar,Dylan Wolff,Abhik Roychoudhury*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种"安全规约优先"的范式，通过显式化智能体的隐性假设为安全规约，并利用运行时反证不断细化这些规约，从而提升自主漏洞检测的可信度与效果。在真实项目中检测出22个新漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM智能体的自动化漏洞检测虽然取得了突破性进展，但智能体的推理过程高度不透明且缺乏验证。智能体在判断某个函数安全时所依赖的隐性假设（如对输入范围的假设）可能失败，导致漏报和信任度降低。

Method: 提出Code-Augur框架：(1) 安全规约优先范式，将智能体的隐性假设显式化为安全规约（in-source assertions）；(2) 利用引导式模糊测试（guided fuzzer）在运行时尝试反证这些规约，触发断言即发现漏洞或规约缺陷，从而迭代优化智能体的理解。

Result: 在真实世界项目中，Code-Augur比现有最先进智能体检测出更多漏洞，并在关键开源项目中发现了22个新漏洞。基于广泛可用的LLM（Sonnet、DeepSeek）即可达到甚至超越专用模型（Claude Mythos）的效果。

Conclusion: 通过显式化规约与运行时反证机制，Code-Augur有效提升了智能体漏洞检测的可信度、可解释性和实际效果，证明了"安全规约优先"范式的有效性。

Abstract: The advent of agentic vulnerability detection is already becoming a watershed moment for software security. Audits conducted entirely by autonomous LLM agents are uncovering critical vulnerabilities in fundamental software underpinning digital society. Many of these vulnerabilities remained masked for years, surfacing only now with AI agents. Yet the reasoning behind these discoveries remains alarmingly opaque and unvalidated. What assumptions did the agent make about a function's inputs when it deemed that function to be secure? Failures in reasoning and incorrect assumptions can lead to missed vulnerabilities and reduce trust in agentic analysis.
  We propose a security-specification-first paradigm that (1) exposes the agent's tacit assumptions explicitly as security specifications and (2) continuously refines those specifications via runtime falsification. We realize our approach in Code-Augur, a novel harness for agentic vulnerability detection. Given a codebase, Code-Augur analyzes each component of the system for vulnerable code. When it deems a component to be secure, it commits the local invariants behind that judgment as in-source assertions. In parallel, Code-Augur leverages a guided fuzzer to attempt to falsify those assumptions. When the fuzzer triggers an assertion, this either reveals a genuine vulnerability or a flawed specification to refine. In both cases, this process grounds the agent's understanding, aligning its view of code intent with how the code actually behaves. On real-world subjects, Code-Augur effectively leverages security specifications to detect more vulnerabilities than other state-of-the-art agents. Additionally, Code-Augur found 22 new vulnerabilities in key open-source projects. Compared to curated specialized models like Claude Mythos, Code-Augur offers effective agentic vulnerability detection built on widely available LLMs like Sonnet and DeepSeek.

</details>


### [131] [TGCM: Topic-Guided Generative Disentanglement of Interleaved APT Technique Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18651)
*Guo-Wei Wong,Ming-Chuan Yang,Shou-De Lin,Wang-Chien Lee,Meng~Chang Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TGCM生成式解缠框架，利用一致性模型和MITRE ATT&CK主题先验，解决多APT攻击日志中未知数量攻击序列的分离问题，在合成和真实数据集上优于15种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业环境中多个APT攻击同时发生，产生的审计日志中不同攻击源的技术序列交错混合，现有方法（统计模式挖掘、溯源分析等）通常假设单APT场景或依赖刚性启发式规则，无法有效处理重叠攻击、共享技术和可变执行长度等现实条件。

Method: 提出Topic-Guided Consistency Modeling (TGCM)框架：1) 使用一致性模型学习从交错多攻击观测到结构化单攻击序列的直接逆映射，单步推理完成解耦；2) 引入基于MITRE ATT&CK叙事的主题引导先验，在分解过程中提供高层战术约束以促进语义连贯的攻击链。

Result: 在合成数据集、混合数据集及DARPA TC-E3/E5真实攻击轨迹上评估，与15种基线（模式挖掘、深度学习、LLM方法）对比，在高交错度和技术共享条件下分离鲁棒性显著提升，并在DARPA TC-E5上实现零样本泛化。

Conclusion: TGCM有效解决了未知数量交错攻击序列的分离问题，通过生成式解缠和领域知识引导实现了强鲁棒性和零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: In enterprise environments, multiple Advanced Persistent Threat (APT) campaigns often unfold concurrently, producing audit logs in which attack techniques across actors (sources) are interleaved over time. This setting naturally gives rise to an Unknown-K Interleaved Sequence Demixing (UKISD) problem: recovering multiple latent campaigns from an interleaved technique sequence while jointly inferring their number and technique-level assignments. Existing approaches, ranging from statistical pattern mining to provenance-based analysis, typically assume single-campaign settings or rely on rigid heuristics, limiting their effectiveness under realistic conditions involving overlapping campaigns, shared techniques, and variable execution lengths.
  We present Topic-Guided Consistency Modeling (TGCM), a generative disentanglement framework to tackle the UKSID problem. TGCM leverages Consistency Models to learn a direct inverse mapping from interleaved multi-campaign observations to structured single-campaign sequences in a single inference step. To favor semantically coherent attack chains, TGCM incorporates a topic-guided prior derived from MITRE ATT\&CK narratives, providing high-level tactical constraints during decomposition. We evaluate TGCM on synthetic datasets, established mixed datasets, and incident traces from DARPA TC-E3 and TC-E5, comparing against 15 representative baselines spanning pattern mining, deep learning, and LLM-based methods. Results indicate improved separation robustness over baselines under heavy interleaving and technique sharing, and show that TGCM generalizes zero-shot to a naturally interleaved in-the-wild benchmark (DARPA TC-E5) without retraining.

</details>


### [132] [Understanding and Mitigating Prompt Leaking Attacks in Real-World LLM-Based Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18673)
*Yong Yang,Chong Fu,Tong Zhang,Rui Zeng,Qingming Li,Tianyu Du,Zonghui Wang,Shouling Ji,Wenzhi Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 系统提示（system prompt）是LLM应用的核心知识产权，但超过80%的真实部署存在提示泄露漏洞。本文通过注意力机制分析揭示了注意力漂移（attention drift）是根本原因，并提出AREA防御方法，在保持强防御能力的同时将可用性提升33%以上、优化开销降低近3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统提示作为LLM应用的核心逻辑和知识产权，易受提示泄露攻击。尽管已有控制环境下的研究，但真实部署中的泄露普遍性、成因和防御效果尚不清楚。

Method: 1) 对六大商业平台1200个应用进行真实攻击测试；2) 进行注意力层级的机械分析，发现注意力漂移（query-key对齐偏差与softmax放大导致模型逐步忽略防御约束）；3) 提出AREA防御方法，通过可优化的软提示重新锚定模型注意力。

Result: 超过80%的部署在真实对抗查询下泄露系统提示；现有防御常在牺牲可用性的情况下仍无法阻止泄露；AREA在泄露抵抗力上达到SOTA水平，同时可用性提升33%以上、优化开销降低近3倍。

Conclusion: 系统提示泄露是真实且普遍的安全问题，注意力漂移是根本机制原因。AREA提供了实用高效的防御方案。负责任披露后，两家供应商将其归类为中危漏洞。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based applications rely on system prompts to encode core logic and developer-defined constraints, making these prompts important intellectual property. However, system prompts are vulnerable to prompt leaking attacks. Although prior work has shown such attacks in controlled settings, their prevalence, causes, and defenses in real-world deployments remain unclear.
  This paper presents a systematic study of prompt leaking in real-world LLM-based applications. We measure 1,200 applications across six major commercial platforms and find that over 80% of deployments leak system prompts under realistic adversarial queries, sometimes exposing sensitive information such as third-party API keys. We also show that existing defenses often fail to prevent leakage without degrading usability. To explain these failures, we conduct an attention-level mechanistic analysis and identify attention drift, where query-key alignment bias and softmax amplification cause LLMs to progressively ignore defensive constraints. Guided by this insight, we propose AREA, a practical defense that re-anchors the model's attention using an optimizable soft prompt. Experiments and real-world case studies show that AREA matches the leakage resistance of state-of-the-art defenses while improving average usability by over 33% and reducing optimization overhead by nearly 3x. Our responsible disclosure led two affected vendors to classify these leaks as medium-severity vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [133] [Image Prompt Reconstruction Attacks on Distributed MLLM Inference Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18710)
*Xinjian Luo,Hongyan Chang,Jianxin Wei,Yuncheng Wu,Xiaofeng Gao,Meikang Qiu,Ting Yu,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究分布式多模态大模型（MLLM）推理中图像提示的隐私泄露问题，提出两种被动黑盒图像重建攻击方法（MPAA和IEDA），在四个代表性MLLM家族上验证了高效的图像重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式LLM推理框架中，中间嵌入向量会泄露私有文本提示；随着LLM演进为多模态大模型（MLLM），图像提示的中间嵌入同样包含丰富的视觉和语义信息，存在隐私泄露风险，但该问题尚未被探索。

Method: 首先分析从图像像素到中间表示的信息流；设计图像嵌入提取算法（在几乎所有MLLM层达到100%提取准确率）；提出两种被动黑盒攻击：MPAA（通过分块信息提取与组装实现细粒度像素级重建）和IEDA（通过嵌入引导的扩散生成实现粗粒度语义重建）。

Result: 在Gemma 3、Phi 4 Multimodal、Qwen 2.5 VL和Llama 4 Scout四个MLLM家族上，两种攻击方法均展现出优异的重建性能；进一步分析了MoE架构、图像预处理、模型规模和文本-图像依赖关系对攻击性能的影响。

Conclusion: 这是首个针对MLLM的图像重建攻击研究，揭示了分布式MLLM推理中图像提示的隐私风险，对分布式AI系统的安全性具有重要警示意义。

Abstract: Distributed large language model (LLM) inference frameworks connect isolated consumer-grade devices for large-scale model inference, substantially reducing hardware constraints. However, recent studies show that intermediate embeddings transmitted among participants can leak private prompts. As LLMs evolve into multimodal LLMs (MLLMs), this risk extends beyond text: image prompts contain rich visual and semantic information, making their intermediate embeddings highly privacy-sensitive. Yet, image-prompt leakage in distributed MLLM inference remains largely unexplored.
  In this paper, we investigate privacy risks to input images caused by intermediate embeddings in distributed MLLM frameworks. We first analyze the information flow from image pixels to intermediate representations. Since image and text embeddings are often intertwined across MLLM layers, we design an image embedding extraction algorithm as a prerequisite for reconstruction attacks, achieving 100% extraction accuracy across almost all MLLM layers in our experiments. Building on this, we develop two passive black-box image reconstruction attacks, MPAA and IEDA, reflecting realistic threats from normal participants with limited knowledge and capability. MPAA performs fine-grained pixel-level reconstruction via patch-wise information extraction and assembly, while IEDA performs coarse-grained semantic reconstruction through embedding-guided diffusion generation.
  We evaluate our attacks on four representative MLLM families: Gemma 3, Phi 4 Multimodal, Qwen 2.5 VL, and Llama 4 Scout. Results show consistently superior reconstruction performance in various settings. We further analyze the effects of MoE architecture, image preprocessing, model size, and text-image dependency on attack performance. To our knowledge, this is the first study of image reconstruction attacks on MLLMs.

</details>


### [134] [A Predictive Neural Network Architecture for Early Detection of Low-Rate Cyberattacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18771)
*Mert Nakıp*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出IDQS，一种基于QoS预测的轻量级主动框架，用于IoT网络中低速率拒绝服务（LDoS）攻击的早期检测。该框架结合RTP-QoS（循环趋势预测神经网络）和PDM（成对决策模型），在SDN-SlowRate-DDoS和CIC-IDS2017数据集上分别达到79%和91%以上的检测准确率，推理时间仅0.28秒，适用于资源受限的IoT环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LDoS攻击因其隐蔽性和持久性，难以被传统入侵检测系统识别，对IoT网络安全构成重大挑战，亟需轻量级且高效的早期检测方法。

Method: IDQS框架包含两个核心组件：(i) RTP-QoS，一种循环趋势预测神经网络，利用历史流量模式学习和预测未来QoS；(ii) PDM，成对决策模型，通过比较预测QoS与实际QoS之间的差异来识别潜在攻击。

Result: 在SDN-SlowRate-DDoS数据集上达到79%以上检测准确率，在CIC-IDS2017数据集上达到91%以上检测准确率，召回率高，假阴性低，端到端推理时间仅0.28秒。

Conclusion: IDQS在资源受限的IoT环境中能够实现高效、实时的LDoS攻击检测，验证了其有效性和适用性。

Abstract: Low-Rate Denial of Service (LDoS) attacks pose a significant challenge to IoT networks due to their subtle and prolonged nature, often evading traditional intrusion detection systems. This paper presents IDQS (Intrusion Detection via QoS Prediction), a lightweight and proactive framework for early LDoS attack detection. IDQS integrates two new key components: (i) RTP-QoS, a Recurrent Trend Predictive Neural Network that learns and forecasts future Quality of Service (QoS) based on historical traffic patterns, and (ii) PDM, a Pairwise Decision Model that evaluates discrepancies between predicted and actual QoS to identify potential attacks. Evaluated on the public SDN-SlowRate-DDoS and CIC-IDS2017 datasets, IDQS respectively achieves over 79% and 91% detection accuracy across most attack scenarios with high recall and low false negatives, while maintaining an end-to-end inference time of just 0.28 seconds. The results demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of IDQS for real-time deployment in resource-constrained IoT environments.

</details>


### [135] [TRAP: Benchmark for Task-completion and Resistance to Active Privacy-extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18996)
*Moon Ye-Bin,Nam Hyeon-Woo,Baek Seong-Eun,Yejin Yeo,Tae-Hyun Oh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TRAP框架评估大语言模型在文档密集型任务中隐私保护与任务准确性的权衡。研究发现所有模型都存在隐私泄露，指令遵循能力与泄露率正相关。现有提示防御会严重损害任务准确性，且理论上证明软约束防御无法同时实现高任务成功率和零泄露概率。作者提出结构性私密字段隔离方法，用哈希键替换私密字段，可有效防止泄露同时保持任务准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在文档密集型工作流中需要处理敏感隐私信息（如护照号），但模型在完成任务的同时可能被诱导泄露这些信息。现有研究缺乏系统评估任务准确性与隐私泄露之间权衡的方法，亟需建立评估框架并探索有效防御手段。

Method: 1) 提出TRAP（Task-completion and Resistance to Active Privacy-extraction）评估框架，每个场景包含含隐私信息的文档、需要调用工具的任务查询和试图诱出隐私的攻击查询。2) 对22个模型（涵盖前沿闭源和开源模型）进行系统评估，分析指令遵循能力与泄露率的相关性。3) 提出结构性私密字段隔离方法，在模型处理前用哈希键替换私密字段。

Result: 1) 所有模型家族都存在非微不足道的隐私泄露。2) 指令遵循能力与泄露率呈正相关。3) 现有提示防御会严重降低任务准确性。4) 理论上证明对任何基于softmax的模型，软约束防御无法同时实现高任务成功率与零泄露概率。5) 结构性私密字段隔离方法能有效防止泄露同时保持任务准确性。

Conclusion: 隐私保护与任务准确性存在根本性矛盾，软约束防御存在理论上的不可能性。结构性私密字段隔离（哈希替换）是一种有效的替代方案，可从根本上规避模型接触原始私密信息，从而打破这一权衡困境。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly deployed in document-intensive workflows where sensitive private information is not an edge case but a routine input, e.g., an agent booking a flight needs passport numbers. In such settings, the agent must use private information to complete tasks accurately while never exposing it in its responses, because it cannot verify who is actually at the keyboard. These two obligations are in fundamental tension. A model capable enough to use private information for task completion can, by the same capability, be induced to reveal it. To evaluate the trade-off of task accuracy and privacy leakage, we introduce Task-completion and Resistance to Active Privacy-extraction (TRAP). Each scenario includes a document containing private information, a task query that requires the agent to invoke the correct tool using private fields, and an attack query that attempts to elicit the same information in natural language. Evaluating 22 models spanning frontier proprietary and open-source models at multiple scales, we find that all model families exhibit non-trivial leakage, and that instruction-following ability correlates with leakage rate. Existing prompt-based defenses reduce leakage but at significant cost to task accuracy. Prompt optimization fails to escape this trade-off. We demonstrate that this failure is not incidental. For any softmax-based model, no soft-constraint defense, e.g., prompt-based defenses, can jointly achieve high task success with zero leakage probability. Motivated by this impossibility result, we propose structural private field isolation, which replaces private fields with hash keys before they reach the model. This approach largely prevents leakage while keeping task accuracy.

</details>


### [136] [Lifecycle-Aware Dynamic Analysis for Secure ML Model Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19023)
*Gabriele Digregorio,Marco Di Gennaro,Francesco Pastore,Stefano Zanero,Stefano Longari,Michele Carminati*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Moat，一种动态生命周期感知的ML模型执行安全方法，通过监控模型执行各阶段与宿主系统的结构化交互来检测恶意行为，在77,974个真实模型、31个CVE PoC和334个攻击模型上实现了接近零误报的全面攻击检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型扫描方案依赖静态、格式特定的规则或已知攻击签名，难以跨框架泛化且无法检测新型攻击路径；ML模型的恶意行为可嵌入模型工件中绕过现有防御。

Method: 基于ML模型在明确定义的生命周期阶段内与宿主系统的交互高度结构化且可预测的观察，提出Moat动态生命周期感知方法，并在Re-Moat中实现参考实现，通过动态分析监控模型执行过程中的系统交互行为。

Result: 在Hugging Face Hub的77,974个真实模型、31个CVE PoC和334个模型上评估，检测所有攻击类别，误报率接近零，优于现有模型扫描方案。

Conclusion: 动态生命周期感知分析能有效检测ML模型执行中的嵌入恶意行为，验证了基于执行阶段结构化交互的直觉，为ML模型执行安全提供了新方向。

Abstract: The growing reliance on pre-trained Machine Learning (ML) models has introduced new attack surfaces. Recent vulnerabilities demonstrate that malicious behavior can be embedded within model artifacts, often bypassing existing defenses. Current model-scanning solutions primarily rely on static, format-specific rules or known attack signatures, which limit their ability to generalize across frameworks and to detect novel exploitation paths. In contrast, we propose a solution that focuses on the effects an attack has on the host system executing the model and builds on foundational intuitions about ML model execution. In particular, we observe that ML models operate within well-defined lifecycle phases and that, within each phase, interactions with the host system are highly structured and predictable. We translate these intuitions into Moat, a dynamic lifecycle-aware approach for securing ML model execution, and instantiate this design in Re-Moat, our reference implementation. We evaluate Re-Moat across multiple ML frameworks using 77,974 real-world model artifacts from the Hugging Face Hub, 31 Proofs-of-Concept (PoCs) from CVEs, and 334 models from a state-of-the-art dataset, and compare it against state-of-the-art model-scanning solutions. Our results show that our approach detects all evaluated attack classes while maintaining a close-to-zero false-positive rate, validating our intuitions and motivating dynamic analysis for securing ML model execution.

</details>


### [137] [PYPILINE: Malicious PyPI Package Detection via Suspicious API Knowledge and Agent Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19063)
*Siyuan Pang,Zhengwei Jiang,Yepeng Yao,Zijing Fan,Haozhe Li,Baoxu Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PYPILINE方法，结合可疑API知识库与Agent工作流检测恶意PyPI包，在精确率96.7%、召回率99.6%、F1-score 98.1%上显著优于现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恶意PyPI包检测方法依赖规则或传统机器学习，可解释性差且难以适应新型攻击，亟需一种更有效且可解释的检测方案。

Method: PYPILINE首先对已知恶意包进行静态分析，提取抽象语法树并生成API调用图，自动构建结构化可疑API知识库；检测阶段结合Agent工作流进行深度语义分析，输出结构化可解释的恶意评估报告。

Result: 精确率96.7%、召回率99.6%、F1-score 98.1%，精确率超越基线工具5.7~24.2个百分点；实证分析揭示了常见攻击策略和滥用API。

Conclusion: PYPILINE通过知识库增强推理与Agent自动化工作流，提供了一种实用、高效、便捷的恶意包检测解决方案，有效加强了开源生态系统安全。

Abstract: The detection of malicious PyPI packages is crucial for maintaining the security of the open source software supply chain. Existing methods, which primarily rely on rules or traditional machine learning, suffer from poor interpretability and difficulty in adapting to novel attacks. To address this, we propose PYPILINE, a novel detection method that combines a suspicious API knowledge base with an Agent workflow. PYPILINE first conducts static analysis on known malicious packages, extracting abstract syntax trees and generating API call graphs, from which it automatically extracts and constructs a structured suspicious API knowledge base. During the detection phase, this knowledge base is used to enhance reasoning capabilities. Through an Agent workflow, PYPILINE performs in depth semantic analysis of unknown packages and outputs a structured, interpretable maliciousness assessment report. The experimental results show that PYPILINE significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art tools in precision of 96.7\%, recall of 99.6\%, and F1-score of 98.1\%, with its precision surpassing baseline tools by 5.7 to 24.2 percentage points. Additionally, we conducted an empirical study on malicious packages, systematically revealing prevalent attack strategies, as well as the most commonly abused APIs. Equipped with tool-calling AI agent workflows for automated vector database retrieval of suspicious API knowledge and mail server delivery of analysis reports, PYPILINE delivers a practical, efficient, and convenient malicious package detection solution to strengthen open-source ecosystem security.

</details>


### [138] [Compute-Budgeted Exploitability Evidence Graphs for Prospective Vulnerability Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19076)
*Faruk Alpay,Taylan Alpay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种泄漏安全的漏洞可利用性前瞻评估协议，通过时间证据图和预算选择器对CVE进行评分，实验表明预算证据选择可将前瞻召回率提升2.6倍，而朴素随机分割会虚假膨胀召回率8.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防御者无法同时修补所有新披露漏洞，因此可利用性预测必须以前瞻性（prospective）而非回顾性（retrospective）方式进行评估。

Method: 将公告、利用存档、修复提交、黑客社区讨论表示为时间证据图；使用预算选择器限制每个CVE仅允许少量证据文档；每个分数附带可审计的证书，列出支持信号、时间戳、源层和泄漏标志。

Result: 在12012个前瞻性CVE上，预算证据选择将前瞻Recall@50从基线0.010提升至0.026（2.6倍），每个CVE仅需2个证据文档即可捕获大部分价值。跨编码器重排序器前瞻Recall仅为0.016。朴素随机分割会虚假膨胀前瞻Recall达8.5倍，EPSS-high Recall达5.0倍。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出泄漏安全评估协议和可复现的证据证书，用于可争议的漏洞优先级判定声明，并表明语义相关性与可利用性证据并不等同。

Abstract: Defenders cannot patch every newly disclosed vulnerability at once, so exploitability prediction must be evaluated prospectively rather than retrospectively. We study compute-budgeted vulnerability triage in which each CVE is scored only from public evidence visible by a fixed decision time. Advisories, exploit archives, fix commits, and hacker-community discourse are represented as a temporal evidence graph; a budgeted selector admits only a few evidence documents per CVE, and every score is paired with an auditable certificate listing the supporting signals, timestamps, source layers, and leakage flags. On 12012 prospective CVEs from public sources, budgeted evidence selection raises leakage-safe prospective recall@50 from 0.010 for a severity-only baseline to 0.026, while two evidence documents per CVE capture most of the value. A strong cross-encoder reranker lowers prospective recall to 0.016, showing that semantic relevance to a CVE is not the same as evidence of exploitation. Most importantly, a naive random split with unfiltered evidence inflates apparent prospective recall by 8.5x and EPSS-high recall by 5.0x. The main contribution is a leakage-safe evaluation protocol and reproducible evidence certificates for contestable vulnerability-prioritization claims.

</details>


### [139] [Quantifying Compromise Risk in Exceptional Access Architectures Under Sparse and Indirect Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19106)
*Alan Woodward*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个结构化不确定性框架，用于评估合法例外访问（EA）架构中的系统性妥协风险。核心发现：任何EA架构的风险均高于无EA基线；传输层EA（T-EA）风险以中心趋势为主，而OTT层EA（OTT-EA）在关联攻击下呈厚尾分布；T-EA的年妥协概率范围为1.4%-12.9%。密钥材料泄露不可逆，对OTT-EA影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏EA特定妥协事件的公开数据集，且过往研究将结构差异巨大的设计（传输层EA与OTT层EA）视为等同，导致风险评估长期停留在定性争论层面。本文旨在通过结构化的不确定性框架，填补对EA架构系统性妥协风险的量化评估空白。

Method: 构建四层分析框架：三个实证支柱（历史类比、蒙特卡洛场景层、信道独立性分解）加上平行子图攻击图上的贝叶斯结构风险模型。该框架不产生预测性预测，而是分离对假设稳健的发现与依赖校准的结论。

Result: 1）任何EA架构的风险均严格高于无EA基线，结论稳健不依赖校准；2）T-EA风险分布以中心趋势为主，OTT-EA在关联攻击下呈厚尾分布；3）T-EA年妥协概率范围为1.4%-12.9%（基于结构化判断的目标溢价区间）。

Conclusion: EA架构带来不可逆的密钥泄露风险，在多十年时间跨度上累积妥协概率远高于零，且OTT-EA因其更大用户群体承担更高风险。框架仅量化妥协概率，不涉及预期危害或收益建模。

Abstract: Lawful exceptional access (EA) systems hold the cryptographic keys that decrypt protected communications for authorised parties. The debate over their risks has been long and qualitative, complicated by two problems: no public dataset of EA-specific compromise events exists, so assessment must use sparse, indirect evidence; and prior work has treated structurally different designs as equivalent, though transmission-layer EA in carrier infrastructure (T-EA) and over-the-top EA at the platform layer (OTT-EA) differ in how cryptographic keys relate to ciphertext data. This paper builds a structured uncertainty framework for evaluating systemic compromise risk in EA architectures. It does not produce predictive forecasts, which the evidence cannot support; it separates findings robust to assumptions from those that depend on calibration. Four analytical layers are applied to T-EA and OTT-EA: three empirical pillars (historical analogues, a Monte Carlo scenario layer, a channel-independence decomposition) plus a Bayesian Structural Risk Model on a parallel-subgraph attack graph. The central findings are structural. First, EA-equipped architectures of either class carry strictly higher modelled risk than their no-EA counterfactual, an ordering independent of calibration. Second, the classes differ in distribution shape: T-EA risk is dominated by central tendency, OTT-EA by the tail under correlated campaigns. Third, calibration-conditional annual probability ranges span 1.4% to 12.9% for T-EA across the structured-judgement targeting-premium interval. Over multi-decade horizons, cumulative compromise is well above zero; key-material exfiltration is irreversible, weighing heavily on OTT-EA's larger user populations. The framework quantifies compromise probability, not expected harm; consequence modelling and benefit estimation are outside its scope.

</details>


### [140] [Giskard : Byzantine Robust and Confidential Aggregation for Large-Scale Decentralized Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19129)
*Ousmane Touat,César Sabater,Mohamed Maouche,Sonia Ben Mokhtar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Giskard是一个去中心化学习协议，通过将参与者组织成大小为O(log n)的委员会树，并使用BGW风格MPC进行委员会内分布式二分搜索来评估坐标近似中位数，实现了机密性和拜占庭鲁棒性的平衡，在最多n/4拜占庭节点下将每方通信复杂度从O(n)降低到O(log n)级别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化学习中同时保证机密性和拜占庭容错是困难的，因为机密性要求隐藏梯度/参数（如使用密码学技术），而拜占庭鲁棒性需要检查这些信息。现有研究大多分别处理这两个目标，而结合MPC与鲁棒聚合的方法扩展性差，要么需要全连接通信，要么将计算委托给小子集导致负载随网络规模线性增长。

Method: Giskard将n个参与方组织成大小为O(log n)的委员会树，在每个委员会内部使用BGW风格的MPC进行坐标近似中位数的分布式二分搜索，实现拜占庭鲁棒聚合同时保持机密性。

Result: 理论上证明了安全性和机密性；实验上在百万级参与方规模下，与最接近的竞品相比，降低每方通信复杂度渐近值，并在最多n/4拜占庭节点下保持可比的模型效用。

Conclusion: Giskard通过树状委员会结构和委员会内MPC二分搜索，有效解决了去中心化学习中机密性与拜占庭鲁棒性的矛盾，显著降低了通信复杂度，具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Dealing simultaneously with confidentiality and Byzantine behaviors in decentralized learning is a challenging problem. Indeed, in decentralized learning, clients train a machine learning model while keeping their data locally and share their model parameters or gradients with a set of neighbors. While enforcing confidentiality calls for hiding the exchanged model parameters/gradients (e.g., by using cryptographic techniques), dealing with Byzantine contributions often requires inspecting the latter. Hence, most research works address these objectives separately. A recent line of work proposes to employ secure multi-party computation (MPC) to implement robust aggregators against model poisoning, thereby enforcing both confidentiality and Byzantine resilience.
