However, these solutions scale badly: they either require all-to-all communication between participants or delegate the entire computation to a small subset, whose computational and communication load grows proportionally with the size of the network.
  In this paper, we present Giskard, a protocol for confidential and Byzantine-robust decentralized aggregation. Giskard organizes $n$ parties into a tree of committees of size $O(\log n)$ and evaluates a coordinate-wise approximate median via a committee-adapted distributed binary search over the value domain, using BGW-style MPC within each committee. We assess Giskard both theoretically by proving its security and confidentiality properties and experimentally through extensive experiments involving up to one million participants. Compared to its closest competitors, Giskard reduces per-party communication complexity asymptotically while exhibiting comparable model utility under up to $n/4$ Byzantine parties.

</details>


### [141] [OpenAnt: LLM-Powered Vulnerability Discovery Through Code Decomposition, Adversarial Verification, and Dynamic Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19149)
*Nahum Korda,Gadi Evron*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: OpenAnt是一个开源漏洞发现系统，通过将静态程序分析与LLM推理结合在多阶段流水线中，实现大规模代码库的自动化安全分析。它采用可达性过滤将分析面减少97%、对抗性验证评估可利用性、以及动态验证自动生成并执行利用环境。在OpenSSL、WordPress和Flowise等项目中发现了先前未知的漏洞，同时保持了可管理的分析成本和较低的误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态分析误报率高，动态方法（如模糊测试）需要大量基础设施且针对的漏洞类别狭窄，而LLM虽能进行语义推理但在仓库级安全分析中面临上下文管理、成本和验证等挑战。因此需要一种结合静态分析与LLM推理的可扩展、低误报的自动化漏洞发现方法。

Method: OpenAnt采用多阶段流水线架构：(1) 将代码库分解为自包含分析单元，通过从外部入口点的可达性过滤，将分析面减少高达97%；(2) 候选漏洞通过对抗性验证——在受限攻击者模拟下评估可利用性；(3) 发现结果通过动态验证确认，自动生成利用环境并在沙箱容器中执行后丢弃。

Result: 在OpenSSL、WordPress和Flowise等广泛使用的开源项目中，OpenAnt识别出了先前未知的漏洞，同时保持了可管理的分析成本并大幅降低了误报率。

Conclusion: 结合语义推理与利用验证的闭环漏洞发现流水线，为实现可扩展的自动化安全分析提供了一条实用路径。OpenAnt已以Apache 2.0许可证开源发布。

Abstract: Automated vulnerability discovery in large codebases remains challenging: traditional static analysis produces high false-positive rates, while dynamic approaches such as fuzzing require substantial infrastructure and often target narrow classes of bugs. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) enable semantic reasoning about program behavior, but applying LLMs to repository-scale security analysis introduces challenges related to context management, cost, and verification.
  We present OpenAnt, an open-source vulnerability discovery system that integrates static program analysis with LLM-based reasoning in a multi-stage pipeline. OpenAnt introduces three key techniques. First, codebases are decomposed into self-contained analysis units filtered by reachability from external entry points, reducing the analysis surface by up to 97% while preserving attack-relevant code. Second, candidate vulnerabilities undergo adversarial verification through constrained attacker simulation, where the model evaluates exploitability under realistic attacker capabilities. Third, findings are validated through dynamic verification, in which exploit environments are generated automatically, executed in sandboxed containers, and discarded after use.
  Evaluation on widely used open-source projects including OpenSSL, WordPress, and Flowise shows that this architecture can identify previously unknown vulnerabilities while maintaining manageable analysis cost and substantially reducing false positives. Our results suggest that closed-loop vulnerability discovery pipelines, combining semantic reasoning with exploit validation, provide a practical path toward scalable automated security analysis.
  OpenAnt is released as open source under the Apache 2.0 license at https://github.com/knostic/OpenAnt.

</details>


### [142] [PhantomSkill: Malicious Code Injection in Agent Skill Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19191)
*Yu-Ting Lin,Chia-Mu Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PhantomSkill是一种针对LLM编码代理的供应链攻击框架，通过将恶意行为隐藏在技能辅助资源中而非文本描述中，利用VulMask技术将显式恶意脚本重写为漏洞式实现，仅在攻击者控制的触发条件下激活恶意行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理利用第三方技能包获取领域特定能力，但技能生态系统引入了新的供应链攻击面；现有安全机制主要检查技能文本描述，忽略了隐藏在辅助资源中的恶意行为。

Method: 提出PhantomSkill攻击框架，核心是VulMask技术——将显式恶意脚本重写为看似普通的不安全代码（漏洞式实现），仅在攻击者控制的触发条件下才激活恶意行为，从而将检测信号从显式恶意意图转变为普通的不安全代码。

Result: 在多种宿主技能、攻击目标、编码代理、生成模型和自动化审查器上，VulMask在保持良性实用性的同时，相比显式恶意脚本显著降低了警告率和恶意软件级检测率。

Conclusion: 技能生态系统需要资源级审查、执行时隔离以及安全策略，将代理技能中可利用的漏洞视为潜在的恶意载荷。

Abstract: Agent skills allow LLM-based coding agents to acquire domain-specific capabilities from third-party packages, but they also introduce a new supply-chain attack surface. We present PhantomSkill, an attack framework that hides malicious behavior in a skill's auxiliary resources rather than in its textual description. Its core technique, VulMask, rewrites overt malicious scripts into vulnerability-shaped implementations whose malicious behavior is activated only under attacker-controlled trigger conditions. This design shifts the visible signal from explicit malicious intent to ordinary-looking insecure code. Across representative host skills, attack goals, coding agents, generation models, and automated reviewers, VulMask preserves benign utility while reducing warning and malware-level detection compared with overt malicious scripts. Our results show that skill ecosystems require resource-level vetting, execution-time containment, and security policies that treat exploitable vulnerabilities in agent skills as potential malicious payloads.

</details>


### [143] [CodeSentinel: A Three-Layer Defense Against Indirect Prompt Injection in Code Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19235)
*Po-Han Cheng,Chia-Mu Yu,Ying-Dar Lin,Yu-Sung Wu,Wei-Bin Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出CodeSentinel，一种三层推理时净化器，通过语法引导预过滤、CST引导的动态Min-K%评分和节点扰动分析，检测并移除代码中的间接提示注入攻击，在六类攻击中达到0.80节点级F1，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码大语言模型在检索外部代码上下文时面临间接提示注入攻击面，攻击者可隐藏恶意指令于注释、字符串、标识符或诱饵代码中，现有防御方法不足以检测自然语言风格的语义触发器。

Method: 三层次推理时净化器：1）Tree-sitter提取高风险模型面向CST节点；2）语法引导预过滤 + CST引导动态Min-K%评分 + 节点扰动分析；3）检测到的节点被移除或中和后送入下游Code LLM。

Result: 在六类最新攻击家族上，节点级F1平均值达0.80，优于CodeGarrison、DePA和KillBadCode。

Conclusion: CodeSentinel通过多层次的语法与语义分析，有效防御代码上下文中隐藏的提示注入攻击，为代码LLM的安全部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Code large language models increasingly retrieve external code context from repositories, documentation, issue threads, and coding-agent environments, creating an indirect prompt-injection surface where attackers hide instructions in comments, strings, identifiers, or decoy code. We propose CodeSentinel, a three-layer inference-time sanitizer. It uses Tree-sitter to extract high-risk model-facing CST nodes, then combines syntax-guided pre-filtering, CST-guided Dynamic Min-K\% scoring, and node perturbation analysis to detect adversarial and natural-looking semantic triggers. Detected nodes are removed or neutralized before reaching the downstream Code LLM. Across six recent attack families, \CodeSentinel achieves 0.80 average node-level F1, outperforming CodeGarrison, DePA, and KillBadCode.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [On Local Population-Risk Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19147)
*Mingzhi Song*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于局部置信带的模型更新规则，通过构造关于风险增量的双边置信带，仅当更新候选集的上端点非正时才接受更新，从而实现对风险的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型更新方法难以保证更新后的模型风险不增加，需要一个能够提供严格风险控制的形式化框架。

Method: 为当前模型周围局部候选集构造风险增量的双边置信带，利用置信带上端点作为决策准则。

Result: 提出了风险可控的更新规则：仅当置信带上端点非正时才接受更新，否则保留当前模型。

Conclusion: 该方法为模型更新提供了理论保证的风险控制机制。

Abstract: This paper develops local certificates for population-risk increments around a current model. For a local candidate set \(\mathcal D\), the certificate is a two-sided confidence band for \(P({\ell_{θ+v}-\ell_θ})\) over \(v\in\mathcal D\). As an application, the upper endpoint of this band yields a risk-controlled update rule: an update is accepted only when its certified upper endpoint is nonpositive; otherwise the current model is retained.

</details>


### [145] [Pointwise is Pointless? A Multimodal Ablation Study for Precipitation Nowcasting with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18436)
*Ophélia Miralles,Máté Mile,Christoffer Artturi,Thomas Nipen,Ivar Seierstad*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过北欧雷达区域的多模态图神经网络临近预报系统，系统评估了稀疏点观测（地面站、卫星等）对密集雷达场预报的贡献。研究表明，不同数据源改善不同预报方面：MEPS数值预报稳定雷达外推，Netatmo地面观测改善局地站点和降水 onset 诊断，卫星数据减少站点偏差但可能过早激活降水，CRPS损失提供最一致的雷达网格增益。稀疏观测对局地约束有用，但对雷达场性能的提升取决于训练损失、不确定性表示和观测编码方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏点观测数据越来越多地可用于降水临近预报，但尚不清楚它们对密集雷达场预报的改进程度。本文旨在部分回答这一问题，通过消融实验评估各种操作相关数据源和训练目标对预报性能的影响。

Method: 构建多模态图神经网络临近预报系统，覆盖北欧雷达区域。模型每5分钟预测未来2小时的降雨率，使用不同的组合：雷达历史、MEPS数值天气预报、Netatmo地面观测、MSG卫星通道、随机噪声和CRPS损失。设计为操作相关数据源和训练目标的消融实验，通过雷达网格、站点位置、降水 onset、oracle、位移和振幅评分等多维度诊断进行对比。

Result: 1) MEPS稳定雷达外推；2) Netatmo观测改善局地站点和 onset 诊断；3) 卫星数据减少站点偏差但确定性使用可能过早激活降水；4) CRPS损失提供最一致的雷达网格增益；5) 卫星+CRPS组合在oracle/DAS评分上最优。

Conclusion: 稀疏点观测对临近预报有用，但局地观测技能和空间一致的雷达场技能是不同目标。稀疏观测提供有用局地约束，但对雷达场性能的提升取决于训练损失、不确定性表示和观测编码方式。

Abstract: Sparse point observations are increasingly available for precipitation nowcasting, but it is unclear how much they improve dense radar-field forecasts. We partially address this question with a multimodal graph neural network nowcasting system over the Nordic radar domain. The model predicts rain rate every five minutes up to two hours ahead and is trained with different combinations of radar history, MEPS numerical weather prediction, Netatmo surface observations, MSG satellite channels, stochastic noise, and CRPS-based ensemble losses. The study is designed as an ablation of operationally relevant information sources and training objectives. We compare radar-only, NWP-informed, station-informed, satellite-informed, noise-augmented, and CRPS-based configurations using complementary diagnostics on the radar grid, at station locations, for rain onset, and through oracle, displacement, and amplitude scores. The results show that each source improves a different part of the forecast problem. MEPS stabilises radar-only extrapolation, Netatmo observations improve local station and onset diagnostics, and satellite predictors reduce some station-level biases but may activate rain too early when used deterministically. CRPS-based configurations provide the most consistent radar-grid gains, while the combined satellite and CRPS setup gives the best overall oracle/DAS score. These results do not support the conclusion that point observations are uninformative for nowcasting, but they show that local observational skill and spatially coherent radar-field skill are distinct targets. The practical implication is that sparse observations can provide useful local constraints, but their benefit for radar-like fields depends on the training loss, uncertainty representation, and how observation support is encoded in the model.

</details>


### [146] [ToolChain-CRC: Conformal Risk Control for Agentic AI Under Retrieval and Tool-Use Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18467)
*Jeffery Opoku,David Banahene*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出ToolChain-CRC方法，通过轨迹级别的风险控制（而非仅最终答案）来监控AI代理在检索增强和工具使用过程中的中间步骤风险，确保在数据漂移下仍能控制风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI代理在最终答案产生前会经历检索文档、调用工具、检查中间信息等多个步骤，但仅检查最终答案无法发现检索薄弱、工具输出错误或中间步骤不可靠等问题，存在风险控制盲区。

Method: 将每个代理运行视为完整的动作-观测-输出轨迹，构建步骤级风险分数并组合为轨迹风险分数，校准接受或干预规则，并引入任意时刻报警机制。在可交换性假设下证明轨迹级风险控制，提出漂移感知扩展和超鞅构造的任意时刻升级规则。

Result: 在合成工具链漂移、RAG/工具使用压力测试、SQuAD检索任务、无API代理QA案例等多个实验设置下，仅校准最终答案会遗漏检索和工具故障，而轨迹级校准将接受轨迹的风险控制在目标值以下。

Conclusion: 轨迹级别的风险控制比仅依赖最终答案的校准更能有效检测和管控AI代理在中间步骤中的各类故障，对数据漂移具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Modern AI agents retrieve documents, call tools, check intermediate information, and then produce a final answer or action. This creates a risk-control problem that is not visible from the final answer alone. A final response may look acceptable even when the retrieval was weak, a tool output was wrong, or an earlier step was unsupported. We propose ToolChain-CRC, a conformal risk-control method for retrieval-augmented and tool-using agents under drift. The method treats each agent run as a full trajectory of actions, observations, and final output. It builds step-level risk scores, combines them into a trajectory risk score, calibrates an accept-or-intervene rule, and adds an anytime alarm that can stop risky runs before the final answer. We prove trajectory-level risk control under exchangeable calibration runs, give a drift-aware extension with auditable constants, and prove an anytime escalation rule through a supermartingale construction. Experiments cover synthetic tool-chain drift, RAG/tool-use stress tests, public SQuAD-derived retrieval tasks, an API-free agentic QA case study, ablations, target-risk sensitivity checks, 20-seed robustness checks, a drift-margin audit, and a live RAG/tool-use agent benchmark. Across these settings, final-answer-only calibration can miss retrieval and tool failures, while trajectory-level calibration keeps accepted-trajectory risk below the target.

</details>


### [147] [Compact Geometric Representations of Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18520)
*Prashant Gokhale,Piotr Indyk,Yuhao Liu,Sandeep Silwal,Tony Chang Wang,Haike Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究层次检索中图的可达性嵌入问题，证明了有向树可在常数维度3中嵌入，基于树宽的图可在O(t log n)维度嵌入，并给出了匹配或近匹配的下界，实验表明该方法在高召回率下维度远小于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作表明有效嵌入在子节点数少时存在，但对于深层层次结构所需维度会退化到节点总数级别，因此需要研究更紧致的可达性嵌入表示，使其维度依赖于结构图参数而非节点总数。

Method: 理论证明：有向树存在常数维度3的可达性嵌入；推广到树宽为t的图，构建O(t log n)维度的嵌入；给出下界：一般DAG需要Ω(n)维度，树宽t的图需要Ω(t/ log(n/t))维度；还给出了基于交叉边数量的参数化上下界。

Result: 有向树可在常数维度3嵌入；树宽t的图可在O(t log n)维度嵌入；一般DAG下界Ω(n)；树宽t图下界Ω(t/ log(n/t))；实际数据集上在高召回率下维度远小于先前理论保证的嵌入。

Conclusion: 本文通过结构图参数（树宽、交叉边数量）给出了层次检索中可达性嵌入的更紧致维度上下界，理论成果与实际数据集实验均表明所提方法能显著降低嵌入维度，同时保持高召回率。

Abstract: Computing geometric representations of data is a cornerstone of modern machine learning, typically achieved by training dual encoders which map queries and documents into a shared embedding space. Recent work of You et al. [NeurIPS '25] has extended this approach to hierarchical retrieval, where relevance is determined by the ancestor-descendant relationships in a Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG). While previous work has shown that valid embeddings exist when the number of descendants is small, these bounds degrade significantly for deep hierarchies, requiring dimensions as large as the total number of nodes.
  In this paper, we investigate compact reachability embeddings for more general graph classes and provide theoretical guarantees for representing hierarchies using embeddings whose dimension depends on structural graph parameters. We prove that for any directed tree, there exists a reachability embedding in constant dimension 3, independent of the tree's size or depth. We generalize this result to graphs characterized by treewidth $t$, constructing embeddings of dimension $O(t \log n)$, where $n$ is the number of nodes. Complementing these upper bounds, we provide matching or near-matching lower bounds, showing that dimension $Ω(n)$ is necessary for general DAGs and $Ω(t/\log(n/t))$ is required for graphs of treewidth $t$. We also obtain upper and lower bounds parameterized by the number of cross-edges in the DAG. We additionally show that our embeddings can be constructed on real world datasets, and that they give much smaller dimensions in high recall regimes compared to prior embeddings with theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [148] [Toward Simultaneously Optimal Regret in U-Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18527)
*Rafael Frongillo,Haipeng Luo,Nishant A. Mehta,Jon Schneider*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的在线预测算法，通过自协调噪声的FTPL变体，同时实现了对所有有界恰当损失函数的$\tilde O(\sqrt{T})$遗憾界和对有界光滑恰当损失函数的$O(\log T)$遗憾界，解决了现有U-校准算法无法自适应更简单损失函数的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有U-校准算法对所有有界恰当损失函数都只能实现$\Omega(\sqrt{T})$遗憾，即使对于平方损失等光滑损失也无法达到最优的$O(\log T)$遗憾。本文旨在证明这一局限性并非固有，设计一个能同时适应两类损失函数的预测算法。

Method: 提出一种Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader (FTPL)的新变体，其创新在于直接在预测空间中使用自协调(self-concordant)噪声进行扰动，并基于该噪声的复杂性质进行了全新的分析框架。

Result: 算法同时实现了：1) 对所有有界恰当损失函数$\tilde O(\sqrt{T})$的遗憾界；2) 对所有有界光滑恰当损失函数$O(\log T)$的遗憾界；3) 对相对对数障碍光滑的损失（包括非Lipschitz示例）也能达到对数遗憾。

Conclusion: 通过自协调噪声的FTPL变体，成功打破了U-校准算法无法适应简单损失函数的局限性，其分析框架也可能具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: U-calibration studies online forecasting algorithms whose predictions can be consumed by any unknown downstream agent, guaranteeing sublinear regret simultaneously for all proper loss functions. Existing U-calibration algorithms achieve worst-case optimal $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret for every bounded proper loss, but they fail to adapt to easier losses: as we show, even for smooth losses such as squared loss, they incur $Ω(\sqrt{T})$ regret instead of the optimal $O(\log T)$ regret.
  In this work, we show that this limitation is not inherent. Specifically, we design a single forecast algorithm that simultaneously achieves $\tilde O(\sqrt{T})$ regret for every bounded proper loss and $O(\log T)$ regret for every bounded smooth proper loss. More generally, our algorithm also attains logarithmic regret for losses that are smooth relative to the log-barrier, which include several non-Lipschitz examples. Our approach is based on a novel variant of Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader (FTPL) in which perturbations are applied directly in the prediction space using self-concordant noise. The resulting analysis also departs substantially from prior FTPL analyses due to the complex nature of this noise and may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [149] [When Does Trajectory-Level Supervision Permit Efficient Offline Reinforcement Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18531)
*Xuanfei Ren,Tengyang Xie*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了离线强化学习中仅使用轨迹级结果（而非过程级奖励）进行策略优化的统计理论。针对累积期望奖励目标，提出OPAC算法并证明其样本复杂度为$\widetilde O(H^2\sqrt{C_{sa}(\pi^\star)/n})$，匹配下界。扩展到偏好反馈和广义结果型离线RL，指出后者在一般情况下面临指数级样本复杂度瓶颈，并通过两个结构系数刻画可学习条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的许多序列决策数据集仅记录轨迹级结果（如最终胜负、总得分），而非每一步的过程奖励。现有离线强化学习理论大多假设过程级奖励可用，缺乏对轨迹级监督下策略优化统计代价的系统理解。

Method: 提出OPAC（悲观actor-critic算法），学习隐式奖励模型并从轨迹级标签优化策略。采用悲观原则处理分布偏移，用高概率界刻画统计误差。分别针对累积回报、偏好反馈和广义非线性聚合三种结果类型建立理论，并通过结构系数$\kappa_\mu(\sigma)$和$\chi_\mu(\sigma)$刻画可学习性。

Result: 1）累积回报场景：OPAC达到$\widetilde O(H^2\sqrt{C_{sa}(\pi^\star)/n})$样本复杂度，匹配下界，表明每个轨迹一个标签与过程级奖励之间的统计代价是$H\sqrt{n}$因子。2）偏好反馈：保持相同的horizon和concentrability依赖。3）广义结果：一般不可学习（需$\Omega(2^H)$条轨迹），但在两个结构系数良好时多项式可学习。

Conclusion: 系统刻画了轨迹级结果监督下离线策略优化的统计极限：当目标为累积期望奖励或偏好时，轨迹级标签仅带来可量化的多项式代价；当目标为广义非线性聚合时，缺失过程奖励可能造成指数级统计壁垒，但可通过结构系数识别可学习子类。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning is typically analyzed under process-level reward supervision, yet many sequential decision datasets
  record only trajectory-level outcomes. We develop a statistical theory for offline policy optimization from such outcome-level
  supervision. We first study the canonical setting where the target remains the expected cumulative reward, but each offline trajectory
  provides only a scalar label whose conditional mean is the cumulative return. We propose OPAC, a pessimistic actor-critic algorithm
  that learns a latent reward model and optimizes a policy from trajectory-level labels. We prove a high-probability guarantee of order
  $\widetilde O(H^2\sqrt{C_{sa}(π^\star)/n})$ and a matching lower bound, characterizing the sharp statistical cost of replacing
  process-level rewards with one trajectory-level label. We then extend the principle to preference-based feedback, preserving the
  leading horizon and concentrability dependence up to preference-model constants. Finally, we study generalized outcome-based offline
  RL, where both the supervision and the objective are trajectory-level quantities induced by a nonlinear aggregation of latent per-step
  rewards. This problem is not learnable in general: for all-success objectives, any offline learner may require $Ω(2^H)$
  trajectories even with deterministic transitions and constant concentrability. We then identify a tractable regime through two
  structural coefficients, $κ_μ(σ)$ and $χ_μ(σ)$, capturing information loss in outcome aggregation and
  generalized Bellman updates, under which generalized OPAC achieves polynomial sample complexity. Together, our results delineate when
  outcome-level supervision enables sample-efficient offline control and when missing process-level rewards create fundamental
  statistical barriers.

</details>


### [150] [Bridging Data Gaps in Structural Fragility Modeling through Transfer Learning: Methodology and Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18567)
*Narges Saeednejad,Jamie Ellen Padgett*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以方法为中心的迁移学习框架，用于在域偏移、类别不平衡和目标标签稀缺条件下进行易损性自适应，同时保留工程可解释性并支持不确定性下的决策。通过四个迁移学习策略（基于实例、基于参数、层次贝叶斯和多源）在三个案例研究中的验证，表明直接迁移源模型在域偏移和类别不平衡下失效，而针对性自适应显著提升了低数据场景下的失效检测和预测稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有易损性模型在域偏移、类别不平衡和目标标签稀缺的条件下适应性差，缺乏系统的方法论指导，亟需一种能够保持工程可解释性并支持不确定性决策的自适应迁移学习框架。

Method: 提出了一个方法论导向的迁移学习框架，包含四种策略：基于实例（重要性加权）、基于参数、层次贝叶斯（部分池化和后验不确定性量化）以及多源迁移学习（融合多个分析模型、学习源权重和正则化目标域自适应）。通过三个案例研究（飓风卡特里娜-海岸桥梁、飓风伊恩-住宅建筑、2001年尼斯奎利地震-桥梁）进行验证。

Result: 直接迁移现有最先进的源模型在域偏移和严重类别不平衡下失效；而针对性自适应迁移学习显著提升了低数据场景下的失效检测能力和预测稳定性。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在开发和自适应易损性模型时，需要系统性地指导诊断、策略选择以及不确定性报告。

Abstract: This paper presents a methodology-centered transfer learning framework for fragility adaptation under domain shift, class imbalance, and scarce target labels while preserving engineering interpretability and supporting decision-making under uncertainty. Four transfer learning strategies (instance-based, parameter-based, hierarchical Bayesian, and multi-source) are demonstrated through three complementary case studies: (i) instance-based transfer learning via importance weighting, demonstrated on coastal bridge fragility using Hurricane Katrina observations; (ii) parameter-based transfer learning together with hierarchical Bayesian transfer learning, enabling partial pooling across strata and posterior uncertainty quantification, demonstrated on residential building fragility using Hurricane Ian observations; and (iii) multi-source transfer learning that fuses multiple analytical fragility models with learned source weights and regularized target-domain adaptation, demonstrated on seismic bridge fragility using observations from the 2001 Nisqually earthquake. Across these case studies, direct transfer of source models (i.e. using existing state-of-the-art models) fails under domain shift and severe class imbalance, while targeted adaptation substantially improves failure detection and predictive stability in low-data regimes. These findings highlight the need for systematic guidance on diagnostics, strategy selection, and uncertainty reporting when developing and adapting fragility models.

</details>


### [151] [TimeLAVA: Learning-Agnostic Data Valuation for Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18729)
*Wenqin Liu,Weizhi Quan,Aoqi Zuo,Erdun Gao,Vu Nguyen,Dino Sejdinovic,Howard Bondell,Mingming Gong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: TimeLAVA提出了一种基于小波变换和最优传输的学习无关时间序列数据估值框架，通过计算时间片段对分布差异的边际贡献来量化数据价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据估值方法要么依赖模型（泛化性差），要么针对i.i.d.数据设计（无法捕捉时间序列的时间依赖、多尺度和非平稳特性），而医疗、金融等领域迫切需要有效的时间序列估值方法。

Method: 构建Selective Wavelet-based Wasserstein discrepancy，结合多尺度小波变换进行时间局部化和不平衡最优传输以应对分布偏移；通过敏感性分析高效计算片段价值，无需模型训练；聚合为逐点分数。

Result: 在异常检测、数据剪枝和标签噪声检测等多个任务上，TimeLAVA在多样化的真实数据集上产生了比现有方法显著更具信息量的价值分数。

Conclusion: TimeLAVA是首个学习无关的时间序列数据估值框架，提供了与模型无关泛化能力的理论保证，并对异常值污染具有有界敏感性。

Abstract: Data valuation quantifies the intrinsic quality of individual samples to enable principled data curation, quality control, and robust learning. For time series in critical domains such as healthcare, finance, and industrial monitoring, effective valuation methods are essential yet fundamentally lacking. Existing approaches are either model-dependent, limiting their generalizability, or designed for i.i.d. data and thus fail to capture temporal dependencies, multi-scale patterns, and non-stationary dynamics inherent to sequential data. We introduce TimeLAVA, a learning-agnostic framework that values temporal segments by their marginal contribution to minimizing distributional discrepancy between evaluated and reference data. At its core is a novel Selective Wavelet-based Wasserstein discrepancy combining multi-scale wavelet transforms for temporal localization with unbalanced optimal transport for robustness to distributional shifts. Segment values are efficiently computed via sensitivity analysis without requiring model training and aggregated into point-wise scores. We provide theoretical guarantees linking valuation to model-agnostic generalization and prove bounded sensitivity to outlier contamination. Extensive experiments across anomaly detection, data pruning, and label noise detection demonstrate that TimeLAVA produces significantly more informative value scores than existing methods on diverse real-world datasets.

</details>


### [152] [Kernel of Partition Paths: A Unified Representation for Tree Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18853)
*Nicolas Mahler*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出KPP方法，通过路径度量将森林中所有节点索引化为平方欧几里得路径等距嵌入的特征映射，构建统一非对角Gram矩阵，统一了预测、精确加性归因、确定性Lipschitz鲁棒半径以及均匀Rademacher风险界四个支柱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将单棵决策树重述为基于分裂特征的线性模型，但森林作为整体在几何上统一的表示仍不明确，特别是当特征映射按节点而非按分裂索引时。

Method: KPP通过路径度量对森林节点进行加权，将每个坐标转化为平方欧几里得路径等距嵌入的分量，构建一个非对角的Gram矩阵，该矩阵承载度量结构。

Result: KPP统一了预测、精确加性归因、确定性Lipschitz鲁棒半径（在KPP度量下）以及回归与分类的均匀Rademacher风险界（在固定、诚实或交叉拟合三种条件化机制下）。所有概率保证均以表示为条件。还提出了回归和分类的快速收敛率猜想作为开放问题。

Conclusion: KPP为森林提供了一个统一的几何框架，其鲁棒半径保证是确定性的且基于KPP度量而非原始输入空间的范数。快速收敛率的精细化结果被列为开放问题，未作为定理声称。

Abstract: A recent line of work has reframed individual decision trees as linear models on engineered features associated with their splits, opening routes for oracle inequalities and feature-importance reinterpretation, but leaving open the question of what unified geometric object a forest induces when one indexes its feature map by nodes rather than by splits. The present paper studies that object. KPP indexes the feature map by the nodes of the forest, weighted by a path metric that turns each coordinate into a component of a squared-Euclidean path-isometric embedding. KPP unifies four pillars under a single non-diagonal Gram that carries a metric: prediction, exact additive attribution, deterministic Lipschitz robust radius in the KPP metric, and uniform Rademacher risk bounds for regression and classification under fixed, honest, or cross-fit conditioning. All probabilistic guarantees are conditional on the representation and are stated under three explicit conditioning regimes; the robust-radius guarantee is deterministic in the KPP metric rather than in a norm on the raw input. Conjectured fast-rate refinements for both regression and classification are stated as open problems and are not claimed as theorems.

</details>


### [153] [FOSC-X: An Extended Framework for Optimal Local Cuts and Non-Horizontal Cluster Selection from Clustering Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18972)
*Connor Simpson,Ricardo J. G. B. Campello*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: FOSC-X是一个从层次聚类树中提取前M个全局最优平坦聚类解的框架，支持带或不带聚类数约束的优化，通过动态规划在多项式时间内保证最优解排序，并能发现被单一解方法忽略的多种替代聚类结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从层次聚类中提取单一平坦解是常见做法，但可能忽略层次结构中蕴含的多种高质量替代聚类结构。现有方法仅寻找单一最优解，无法为用户提供多个有意义的备选聚类方案。

Method: 提出FOSC-X框架，将问题建模为从层次聚类树的局部非水平切割中提取前M个全局最优平坦聚类。无约束时利用动态规划，利用子树中局部最优部分候选可组合成全局最优解的性质；有聚类数约束时，通过维护可行候选的紧凑集合并使用上下界剪枝不可行或支配组合，保证线性时间复杂度。

Result: FOSC-X在有无聚类数约束下均能以线性时间复杂度（相对于聚类节点数和数据集规模）保证前M个最优解的正确排序。实验表明该方法能有效揭示被单一解提取方法忽略的替代聚类结构。

Conclusion: FOSC-X为层次聚类中的多解提取提供了高效且理论保证的解决方案，克服了聚类数约束下动态规划最优子结构失效的难题，在实际应用中能提供更丰富的聚类分析视角。

Abstract: Extracting a flat clustering solution from a hierarchy is a common task in practical cluster analysis and can be formulated as an optimisation problem. Existing approaches focus on finding a single optimal solution. We introduce FOSC-X, a framework for extracting the top-M globally optimal flat clusterings from local, non-horizontal cuts of a hierarchical cluster tree, while optionally enforcing constraints on the number of clusters. This enables automatic identification of multiple high-quality alternative clusterings that capture different aspects of the hierarchical structure. Without constraints, the top-M problem can be solved in polynomial time using dynamic programming, exploiting the property that locally optimal partial candidates within subtrees can be combined to form globally optimal solutions while automatically determining the number of clusters. However, this can lead to solutions with numbers of clusters that are ultimately undesirable -- e.g., too large to be meaningful or practically analysed within a particular application domain. Imposing cluster-count constraints breaks the optimality property underlying the unconstrained dynamic programming approach, since locally optimal partial candidates may no longer combine into feasible globally optimal solutions. FOSC-X addresses this challenge through a dynamic programming strategy that maintains compact sets of feasible candidates using lower and upper feasibility bounds while pruning infeasible or dominated combinations. The resulting method guarantees optimal rankings of the top-M solutions with linear-time complexity in the number of cluster nodes and dataset size, both with and without cluster-count constraints. Experiments show that FOSC-X efficiently reveals alternative clustering structures overlooked by single-solution extraction methods.

</details>


### [154] [Sequential Kernel-based Conditional Independence Testing via Adaptive Betting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18993)
*Zheng He,Danica J. Sutherland*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对现有序列条件独立性检验在Model-X假设下对条件分布估计误差敏感的问题，提出了一种基于自适应优化核条件独立性统计量、归一化及截断-移位校准的新方法，在控制Type I错误膨胀的同时保持高统计功效，在合成和真实数据上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件独立性检验在不附加假设的情况下无法控制第一类错误，Model-X范式虽通过假设已知条件分布解决了此问题，但现有序列检验方法对该假设的微小偏差极为敏感，当条件分布需要估计时表现脆弱，因此需要一种对估计误差更鲁棒的检验方法。

Method: 采用testing-by-betting框架，结合自适应优化的核条件独立性（KCI）统计量，并引入归一化方案和截断-移位校准策略，以增强对条件分布估计误差的鲁棒性。

Result: 在高维合成基准和实际公平性任务中，所提方法显著降低了第一类错误膨胀，同时保持了高检验功效，优于现有的序列Model-X方法。

Conclusion: 提出的方法对Model-X条件分布估计误差具有更强的鲁棒性，有效解决了序列条件独立性检验中的脆弱性问题，在理论和应用上均具有优势。

Abstract: Testing conditional independence is fundamental yet intrinsically difficult: without additional assumptions, Type I error control is impossible in general. The "Model-X'' paradigm addresses this difficulty by assuming exact knowledge of a relevant conditional distribution. While small deviations from this assumption can sometimes be tolerated in classical one-shot testing, existing sequential conditional independence tests typically require the Model-X conditional to be known exactly, making them fragile when it must instead be estimated. We propose a new approach that is substantially more robust to such estimation error. Our method applies testing-by-betting to an adaptively optimized Kernel Conditional Independence statistic, together with a normalization scheme and a truncate-and-shift calibration strategy. These modifications greatly reduce Type I error inflation while preserving high power across high-dimensional synthetic benchmarks and real-world fairness tasks, outperforming existing sequential Model-X approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/he-zh/SKCI.

</details>


### [155] [Quantifying and Auditing LLM Evaluation via Positive--Unlabeled Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19057)
*Zilong Zhang,Yi-Ting Hung,Lei Ding,Chi-Kuang Yeh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出基于部分最优传输的几何审计框架，将LLM评估在选择性人工监督下建模为正-无标签学习问题，通过对齐少量人工验证的正样本与可靠的无标签输出来修正LLM评判者的系统偏差（如冗长偏差），无需重新训练即可提升与人类偏好的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge系统存在与语义质量解耦的系统性偏差（如冗长偏差），而人工监督成本高昂且通常具有选择性，只能获得可靠的正向判断，导致大部分输出无标签且质量参差。现有方法缺乏一种无需重新训练即可修正偏差的统计严谨方案。

Method: 将LLM评估在选择性人工监督下形式化为正-无标签（PU）学习问题；提出基于部分最优传输（Partial Optimal Transport）的几何审计框架，通过在固定嵌入空间中对齐少量人工验证的正样本与可靠的无标签子集，识别与人类一致的偏好并修正有偏的评判者。

Result: 实验表明该方法在以下方面有显著提升：与人类偏好的一致性、对呈现偏差的鲁棒性，并提供可解释的置信度估计。

Conclusion: 该方法为现有LLM-as-a-Judge流水线提供了一种可扩展且统计严谨的替代方案，无需重新训练模型即可有效修正系统性评判偏差。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as judges for scalable evaluation, yet such LLM--as--a--Judge systems exhibit systematic biases that are decoupled from semantic quality, most notably verbosity bias. Meanwhile, human supervision is costly and typically selective, yielding reliable positive judgments but leaving most outputs unlabelled and potentially mixed in quality. We formulate LLM evaluation under selective human supervision as a positive--unlabelled learning problem and propose a geometric auditing framework based on Partial Optimal Transport. By aligning a small set of human--verified positives with a reliable subset of unlabelled outputs in a fixed embedding space, our method identifies human--consistent preferences and corrects biased judges without retraining. Experiments demonstrate improved alignment with human preferences, increased robustness to presentation biases, and interpretable confidence estimates, offering a scalable and statistically grounded alternative to existing LLM--as--a--judge pipelines.

</details>


### [156] [Generalised Eigenvalue Geometry of Semantic Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19212)
*Martin Anthony,Kaveh Salehzadeh Nobari*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对金融情感分类器对语义等价改写易受攻击的问题，提出了一个描述代理模型-目标模型双模型结构的连续局部扰动模型。通过构建矩阵束(A,B)的最大广义特征值定义攻击性指标λ*(x)，该指标内蕴于局部改写几何和嵌入器选择，可闭式预测仿射读出器的翻转条件，并支持保守的总体和有限样本攻击性认证。此外还推导了二元攻击性指标的VC界和基于攻击性调整边际的尺度敏感边际界，建立了连续理论与离散改写搜索的联系，并在实际金融文本分类器上进行了实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实证表明语义等价的改写可以欺骗金融情感分类器，但已有鲁棒性理论要么假设单模型威胁模型，要么主要关注经验攻击算法。缺乏对双模型结构下局部语义扰动的形式化理论刻画。

Method: 1) 构建连续局部语义改写扰动模型，捕获代理模型-目标模型双模型结构；2) 定义矩阵束(A,B)的最大广义特征值作为攻击性指标λ*(x)；3) 推导仿射读出器下预测翻转的闭式条件；4) 建立总体和有限样本攻击性认证；5) 推导VC界和攻击性调整边际的尺度敏感边际界；6) 建立连续理论与离散改写搜索的联系及覆盖条件；7) 提出基于软token松弛和生成改写集的实证验证框架。

Result: 攻击性指标λ*(x)内蕴于局部改写几何和嵌入器选择，可实现闭式预测翻转条件；提供了双模型结构下攻击性的形式化理论刻画，并建立了连续与离散分析的联系。

Conclusion: 本文为双模型威胁场景下的语义改写攻击提供了完整的理论框架，包括攻击性量化、预测翻转条件、统计认证和实证验证方法，填补了现有鲁棒性理论在双模型结构方面的空白。

Abstract: Recent empirical work shows that semantically equivalent paraphrases can fool financial sentiment classifiers: although a paraphrase remains close to the original under a strong reference embedding, it may shift the target model's representation enough to change the predicted class. Existing robustness theory either assumes a single-model threat model or focuses mainly on empirical attack algorithms. We develop a continuous local model of semantic paraphrase perturbations that captures this two-model structure. We show that the worst-case local displacement of the target representation, subject to a proxy-model budget, is governed by the largest generalised eigenvalue of a matrix pencil $(A,B)$ constructed from the Jacobians of the two embedding maps. The resulting attackability index $λ^*(x)$ is intrinsic to the local paraphrase geometry and the chosen embedders, yields a closed-form prediction-flip condition for affine readouts, and supports conservative population and finite-sample attackability certificates. For uniform control over classes of affine readouts, we derive a distribution-free VC bound for binary attackability indicators and a scale-sensitive margin bound based on an attackability-adjusted margin that subtracts a local geometric penalty from the standard classifier margin. We also connect the continuous theory to discrete paraphrase search, identify an asymmetry between successful and unsuccessful finite searches, and give a covering condition under which the discrete and continuous settings agree. Finally, we propose an empirical verification framework using soft-token relaxations and generated paraphrase sets to assess the local eigenvalue geometry, prediction-flip condition, and finite-search approximation on a deployed financial-text classifier.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Inferential Models: The Power of Auxiliary Variables for Reasoning with Scientific Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18378)
*Chuanhai Liu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文探讨了推断模型（IMs）作为无需先验的概率推理框架，通过预测随机集（PRS）校准不确定性，实现了频率校准且情境特定的不确定性评估，并论证了无需精确先验分布即可延续Jaynes的科学逻辑理念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学推理的核心挑战在于产生既情境特定又频率校准的不确定性评估。传统贝叶斯方法要求精确先验分布，而频率学派方法缺乏情境特异性。作者试图通过IM框架在无需先验的情况下实现两者的统一。

Method: IM将抽样模型中的辅助变量视为不确定性来源，通过校准的预测随机集（PRS）预测未观测的辅助值，然后转移由此产生的可信度陈述。这种方法改变了Fisher信仰推断中先转移辅助随机性再应用概率演算的顺序。

Result: 这种顺序改变产生了有效的不确定性评估，厘清了Fisher信仰推理、Neyman置信理论、Dempster-Shafer信念函数、广义信仰推断与IM之间的关系。研究表明，校准的不精确性（而非精确先验）对于科学推理往往是必要的。

Conclusion: IM可以延续Jaynes的科学逻辑理念，无需将所有科学不确定性强行纳入精确先验分布。此外，IM的微分几何理论可能即将建立，为传统上以似然原理为框架的基础问题提供了可能的解决路径。

Abstract: A central challenge in scientific inference is to produce uncertainty assessments that are both situation-specific and frequency-calibrated. This article examines inferential models (IMs) as a framework for prior-free probabilistic reasoning with scientific uncertainty. The central IM idea is to view the auxiliary variables in a sampling model as the source of model-based uncertainty. R. A. Fisher's fiducial inference transfers auxiliary randomness to the parameter space before applying probability calculus; IMs instead predict the unobserved auxiliary value with calibrated predictive random sets (PRSs) and transfer the resulting plausibility statements only afterward. This change in order yields valid uncertainty assessments and clarifies the relations among Fisherian fiducial reasoning, Neymanian confidence theory, Dempster-Shafer belief functions, generalized fiducial inference, and IMs. By comparing IMs with objective-prior Bayesian inference, the article argues that E. T. Jaynes' logic-of-science ambition can be continued without forcing all scientific uncertainty into a precise prior distribution because calibrated imprecision is often essential. Finally, the article suggests that a differential-geometric theory of IMs may be within reach, offering a possible route to foundational questions traditionally framed in terms of the likelihood principle.

</details>


### [158] [A spectral based coefficient of determination for the fit of an MA(q) model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18445)
*Holger Dette,Sebastian Kühnert*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于谱的确定系数，用于衡量平稳过程的谱密度被MA(q)模型类表示的程度，并基于周期图估计量建立了渐近正态性，推导了MA(q)假设检验，构建了确定达到预定近似质量的最小阶数q的程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏一个谱域下的标准化度量来评估MA(q)模型对平稳过程谱密度的拟合优度，尤其是如何确定达到预定近似质量的最小阶数q这一问题尚未被系统解决。

Method: 提出基于谱的确定系数（类似于回归中的R²），采用周期图估计量进行估计，推导了该估计量的渐近正态性，据此构建MA(q)假设检验以及确定最小阶数q的选择程序。

Result: 建立了所提确定系数估计量的渐近正态性，构建了有效的MA(q)假设检验以及最小阶数q的确定程序。

Conclusion: 该工作为MA(q)模型在谱域的拟合优度评估提供了理论基础和实用工具，有助于在实际应用中确定适当的模型阶数。

Abstract: We develop a spectral based coefficient of determination to measure how well the spectral density of a stationary process is represented by the class of MA($q$) models. Using periodogram-based estimators, we establish asymptotic normality, derive tests for the MA($q$) hypothesis, and construct procedures for determining the smallest order $q$ achieving a prescribed approximation quality.

</details>


### [159] [Bayesian Prediction in Gamma Models: Admissibility and Infinitesimal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18700)
*Fumiyasu Komaki*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究Gamma模型在Kullback-Leibler损失下的估计与预测问题。对于形状参数α≤1，所有尺度不变估计量具有无限风险；对于α>0，基于Jeffreys先验的贝叶斯预测密度是可容许的，这解决了Gamma模型中贝叶斯预测密度的可容许性问题。文章还建立了基于Gamma过程的无穷小预测框架，将预测分布与Lévy测度联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Gamma模型中贝叶斯预测密度的可容许性问题，并揭示当α=1时估计问题发生的定性变化。

Method: 基于Gamma过程建立无穷小预测框架，将预测问题转化为Lévy密度估计问题；利用Kullback-Leibler损失连接预测分布与Lévy测度；采用Jeffreys先验进行贝叶斯分析。

Result: 当α≤1时，所有尺度不变估计量风险无穷大；当α>0时，基于Jeffreys先验的贝叶斯预测密度是可容许的；当α>1时，对应的贝叶斯估计量也是可容许的；Gamma模型中的无穷小预测不简化为参数估计，而简化为Lévy密度估计。

Conclusion: Gamma模型在α=1处存在估计问题的定性转变；所提出的Gamma过程无穷小预测框架有效解决了可容许性问题；该现象与均值混合曲率及信息几何相关。

Abstract: We study estimation and prediction in the Gamma model $\mathrm{Ga}(α,β)$, where the shape parameter $α$ is known and the scale parameter $β$ is unknown, under the Kullback--Leibler loss. For $α\le1$, all scale-invariant estimators of $β$ have infinite risk, indicating a qualitative change in the estimation problem at the boundary $α=1$. Our main result is that the Bayesian predictive density based on the Jeffreys prior is admissible for all $α>0$. This resolves the admissibility problem for Bayesian predictive densities in Gamma models. As a related result, we also establish the admissibility of the corresponding Bayesian estimator for $α>1$. To prove the predictive admissibility result, we develop an infinitesimal prediction framework based on Gamma processes. This framework naturally leads to a Kullback--Leibler loss for Lévy densities and establishes a connection between predictive distributions and Lévy measures. Under the resulting loss, the Bayesian predictive Lévy density is shown to be the posterior mean Lévy density. Unlike the normal and Poisson models, infinitesimal prediction in the Gamma model does not reduce to parameter estimation. Instead, it reduces to the estimation of a Lévy density. We relate this phenomenon to mean mixture curvature and discuss it from an information-geometric viewpoint.

</details>


### [160] [Group Efficient Randomized-Adaptive Designs with Delayed and Missing Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18942)
*Guijing Zhang,Li-Xin Zhang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文在ERADE基础上提出了一种分组响应自适应随机化设计，支持固定时间间隔的组招募，并考虑了数据随机缺失和响应延迟的情况，理论证明其保留原设计的渐近性质，模拟和实例验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ERADE采用逐例序贯入组方式，在实际临床试验中操作不便，且未考虑数据缺失和响应延迟等现实问题。本文旨在扩展ERADE框架，使其更贴合实际应用场景。

Method: 将原ERADE的逐例序贯入组替换为固定时间间隔（周、双周或月）的组招募，基于各组累积响应信息动态更新分配概率；同时显式建模随机缺失数据和响应延迟，保留原离散分配概率函数及鞅停时理论的分析框架。

Result: 理论分析表明新设计保留原ERADE的主要渐近性质（包括达到Cramér-Rao下界）；在响应延迟和数据缺失条件下仍表现良好；模拟研究和真实临床试验的重新设计验证了方法的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 提出的分组响应自适应设计在提升操作便捷性的同时保持了理论最优性，对缺失数据和响应延迟具有鲁棒性，具备实际推广应用价值。

Abstract: Response-adaptive randomization designs have attracted much attention in clinical trials. This paper proposes a new class of response-adaptive design which built on the efficient randomized-adaptive design (ERADE) proposed by Hu, Zhang, and He. The original ERADE uses a discrete allocation probability function and leverages the stopping time theory of stochastic processes to establish asymptotic results. It has been proven that the design can reach the Cramér-Rao lower bound for any target allocation proportion. This study further expands the original framework by replacing the traditional case-by-case sequential enrollment with group recruitment over fixed time intervals(weekly, biweekly, or monthly), and dynamically updates the allocation probabilities based on the cumulative response information within each group. Meanwhile, to better fit practical application scenarios, we further explicitly consider the situations of randomly missing data and response delay. Theoretical analysis shows that the new design retains all the main asymptotic properties of the original ERADE, and still performs well under the conditions of response delay and missing data. Finally, through simulation studies and the redesign of a real-world clinical trial, the effectiveness and practicality of the proposed method are verified.

</details>


### [161] [Optimal score function estimation via derivatives constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19084)
*Thomas Bonis,Thanh Mai Pham Ngoc,Viet Chi Tran*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究通过经验风险最小化进行得分函数估计的问题。首先在平坦环面上证明将假设空间约束在Sobolev球内足以防止过拟合并获得极小极大估计率。随后在得分生成模型背景下，基于一个关于得分估计率与生成模型输出质量之间关系的猜想，得到了使用Sobolev球约束的得分函数估计器所实现的极小极大率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究得分函数估计中如何防止过拟合并获得最优估计率，以及该估计在得分生成模型中的理论保证。

Method: 采用经验风险最小化框架，将假设空间约束在Sobolev球内进行正则化，分别在平坦环面上的密度得分估计和得分生成模型两个场景中分析极小极大估计率。

Result: 证明了Sobolev球约束下的假设空间能够防止过拟合并获得极小极大最优估计率；在生成模型场景下，基于猜想得到了对应的极小极大率。

Conclusion: Sobolev球约束是得分函数估计中实现最优泛化性能的有效正则化策略，并为得分生成模型提供了理论速率保证。

Abstract: We consider the problem of score function estimation via empirical risk minimization. We first start with the question of inferring the score function of a probability measure $μ$ with density on the flat torus from a sample of distribution $μ$. We show that constraining the hypothesis space to a Sobolev ball is sufficient to prevent overfitting and obtaining minimax estimation rates. We then consider the problem of score function estimation in the context of score-based generative modeling. Again, under a conjecture tying the score estimation rates to the quality of the output of a score-based generative model, we obtain minimax rates for such an approach using score function estimators obtained by constraining the hypothesis class to a Sobolev ball.

</details>


### [162] [Patnaik-Pearson intrinsic dimension for internal representations of neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19268)
*Tom Hadfield*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的数据流形本征维度度量——Patnaik-Pearson维度，将其应用于神经网络（特别是Transformer）的内部表示分析。该方法结合了HTSR/SETOL理论与TwoNN估计器，证明了该维度的数学性质，并建立了与Pareto分布谱密度下权重矩阵的关联。通过对BERT-base和DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1模型的分析，研究了token嵌入的初始本征维度及其在层间传播中的演化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有本征维度估计方法（如TwoNN）虽能刻画数据流形的几何结构，但缺乏与神经网络权重矩阵谱分布理论的直接联系。Martin等人的HTSR/SETOL工作揭示了权重矩阵谱密度与模型泛化性能的关系，然而尚未有一种统一的维度度量能够桥接流形本征维度和谱尾指数。因此，本文旨在提出一种新的维度定义，将这两条研究线索整合，从而更深入地理解神经网络内部表示的几何特性。

Method: 1. 定义Patnaik-Pearson维度，基于TwoNN估计器的思想并融合Patnaik-Pearson分布理论；2. 证明该维度的数学性质（如可加性、变换下的行为等）；3. 对于经验谱密度服从Pareto（幂律）分布的权重矩阵，建立Patnaik-Pearson维度与HTSR/SETOL分析中尾指数临界值的对应关系；4. 结合理论与数值方法，研究数据流形在典型神经网络变换（如线性变换、非线性激活、残差连接等）下Patnaik-Pearson维度的变化规律；5. 在BERT-base和DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1两个实际模型上进行实验，分析token嵌入初始流形的维度以及维度随层深度的演化。

Result: 1. 严格证明了Patnaik-Pearson维度的若干数学性质；2. 建立了该维度与HTSR/SETOL框架中尾指数临界值的一致性关系；3. 揭示了神经网络各层变换对Patnaik-Pearson维度的定量影响规律；4. 在BERT-base和DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1模型上，观察到token嵌入的初始流形具有特定维度值，且该维度随层数增加呈现规律性演化，反映了模型内部表示几何结构的层次化变化。

Conclusion: Patnaik-Pearson维度为分析神经网络内部表示的几何结构提供了一种新的有效工具，它统一了流形本征维度与权重矩阵谱尾指数两个理论视角，能够刻画数据流形经过神经网络各层变换后的几何演化规律，为理解深度模型的表示能力与泛化机制提供了新的理论支撑。

Abstract: We define a new measure of intrinsic dimension of a data manifold, which we call the Patnaik-Pearson dimension, and apply this to internal representations of neural networks, in particular transformers. The inspiration for this comes from the HTSR and SETOL work of Martin, Mahoney and Hinrichs, combined with the TwoNN intrinsic dimension estimator of Facco et al. We prove various properties of this intrinsic dimension estimator. Treating weight matrices of neural networks as data manifolds, for weight matrices whose Empirical Spectral Density follows a Pareto (Power Law) distribution, we relate the Patnaik-Pearson dimension to the HTSR and SETOL analysis, and show that critical values of the tail exponent coincide for the two approaches. Using a combination of theoretical and numerical techniques, we study the behaviour of the Patnaik-Pearson dimension of a data manifold under the transformations typical to neural networks. We apply this machinery to the BERT-base and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1 models, to investigate first the Patnaik-Pearson dimension of the initial data manifold of token embeddings, and second the evolution of the Patnaik-Pearson dimension as token embeddings pass through the layers of the model. Code and notebooks used for the numerical results presented here is available at https://github.com/tdhadfield/PatnaikPearson

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [The Implicit Bias of Steepest Descent with Mini-batch Stochastic Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11557)
*Jichu Li,Xuan Tang,Difan Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多类分类中mini-batch随机最速下降法的隐式偏差，揭示了批大小、动量和方差缩减如何影响最大间隔行为和收敛速率。主要发现包括：无动量时仅全批量梯度能保证最坏情况收敛；动量通过批-动量权衡实现小批量收敛到近似最大间隔解；方差缩减可恢复全批量的隐式偏差但收敛更慢；单样本最速下降无动量时收敛到完全不同的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作中广泛使用的优化方法（如SignSGD和Muon）可被解释为不同范数诱导几何下的最速下降，但随机最速下降在随机优化中的隐式偏差尚未被充分理解，尤其是在批大小、动量和方差缩减等因素如何影响最大间隔行为和收敛性方面缺乏统一分析。

Method: 采用统一的框架分析mini-batch随机最速下降法在多类分类中的行为，研究不同entry-wise和Schatten-p范数下的隐式偏差。分别考察了无动量、有动量、方差缩减以及无动量单样本情形下的收敛特性和间隔行为，并提供了显式的、维度无关的收敛速率。

Result: 1）无动量时仅全批量梯度能保证最坏情况收敛到最大间隔解；2）动量使小批量能收敛到近似最大间隔解，但存在批-动量权衡且收敛变慢；3）方差缩减可恢复任意批大小下的全批量隐式偏差，但收敛速率更慢；4）单样本无动量最速下降收敛到完全不同的偏差，揭示了纯随机更新的关键局限性。

Conclusion: 该统一分析澄清了随机优化何时与全批量行为一致，为深入探索随机梯度最速下降算法的训练行为铺平了道路，并为实际优化方法的选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: A variety of widely used optimization methods like SignSGD and Muon can be interpreted as instances of steepest descent under different norm-induced geometries. In this work, we study the implicit bias of mini-batch stochastic steepest descent in multi-class classification, characterizing how batch size, momentum, and variance reduction shape the limiting max-margin behavior and convergence rates under general entry-wise and Schatten-$p$ norms. We show that, without momentum, worst-case convergence and successful classification can only be guaranteed with full-batch gradient. In contrast, momentum enables small-batch convergence to an approximate max-margin solution through a batch-momentum trade-off, though it slows convergence. This approach provides fully explicit, dimension-free rates that improve upon prior results. Moreover, we prove that variance reduction can recover the exact full-batch implicit bias for any batch size, albeit at a slower convergence rate. Finally, we further investigate the batch-size-one steepest descent without momentum, and reveal its convergence to a fundamentally different bias via a concrete data example, which reveals a key limitation of purely stochastic updates. Overall, our unified analysis clarifies when stochastic optimization aligns with full-batch behavior, and paves the way for perform deeper explorations of the training behavior of stochastic gradient steepest descent algorithms.

</details>


### [164] [Fisher Width: A Geometric Measure of Complexity on Statistical Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18306)
*Vu Khac Ky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fisher宽度，作为统计流形上高斯宽度的黎曼几何类比，用于度量统计模型在Fisher信息度量下的几何复杂性，并证明其在高维概率、压缩感知和统计学习理论中的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯宽度是衡量集合有效维度的核心几何复杂度指标，但它本质上是欧几里得的。统计模型具有由Fisher信息度量诱导的自然黎曼几何，其中方向根据统计可区分性而非欧氏长度进行缩放。现有缺乏一个能够捕捉统计流形局部曲率且具有重参数化不变性的几何复杂度度量。

Method: 在参数点θ处，用局部度量张量G(θ)^{1/2}替换欧几里得单位矩阵，对Fisher重缩放后的集合计算高斯宽度，从而得到Fisher宽度。该量对局部统计曲率敏感且在光滑重参数化下保持不变。作者建立了Fisher宽度的基本理论，包括浓度性质、度量扰动稳定性以及与欧几里得基线的谱比较界。

Result: Fisher宽度保留了高斯宽度的关键结构特征（浓度、度量扰动稳定性、谱比较界），同时能捕捉欧几里得度量无法感知的各向异性几何效应。作为应用，论文证明了Fisher-Lipschitz假设类的泛化界，并提出了可计算估计器，在MNIST上对三类模型进行了实证评估。

Conclusion: Fisher宽度之于统计流形，正如高斯宽度之于欧几里得凸体。该工作为在弯曲统计流形上研究复杂性和学习奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Gaussian width is a central geometric complexity measure in high-dimensional probability, compressed sensing, convex optimization, and learning theory. It quantifies the average extent of a set along random directions, thereby capturing the effective dimension of constraint sets, hypothesis classes, and descent cones. However, this notion is intrinsically Euclidean. Statistical models instead carry a natural Riemannian geometry induced by the Fisher information metric, where directions are scaled according to statistical distinguishability rather than ambient Euclidean length.
  We introduce Fisher width, a Fisher-geometric analogue of Gaussian width for statistical manifolds. At a parameter point $θ$, Fisher width replaces the Euclidean identity by the local metric tensor $G(θ)^{1/2}$, measuring the Gaussian width of the Fisher-rescaled set. This makes the resulting quantity sensitive to local statistical curvature and invariant under smooth reparameterizations.
  We develop the basic theory of Fisher width, showing that it retains key structural features of Gaussian width, including concentration, metric perturbation stability, and spectral comparison bounds with the Euclidean baseline, while also capturing anisotropic geometric effects invisible to Euclidean measures. As an application, we prove a generalization bound for Fisher-Lipschitz hypothesis classes and propose computable estimators, which we evaluate empirically on MNIST across three model classes.
  Fisher width is to statistical manifolds what Gaussian width is to Euclidean convex bodies. This work lays the foundation for studying complexity and learning on curved statistical manifolds.

</details>


### [165] [Measurement noise limits the advantage of nonlinear models over linear models in biomedical prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18420)
*Marc-Andre Schulz,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在生物医学表格数据中，由于测量噪声的存在，非线性模型（如深度网络、梯度提升树）相比于线性模型的优势会消失。测量噪声以特征可靠性的k次方衰减k阶交互作用，而线性部分只衰减一次。在典型生物医学测量可靠性下，即使底层生物学机制高度非线性，非线性优势也会消失，且无法通过更大的样本量或更灵活的模型恢复，只能通过改进测量手段解决。作者通过精确的超额风险恒等式，指出测量可靠性、样本量和特征表示三者必须同时满足条件，灵活模型才能发挥作用，而大多数生物医学任务不满足这一条件。UK Biobank的140个任务验证了这一结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为什么在生物医学表格数据中，灵活模型（深度网络、梯度提升树等）反复被线性/逻辑回归匹配甚至击败的现象，指出根本原因在于测量噪声而非模型能力不足。

Method: 整合测量误差统计、心理测量学和高斯分析中的经典理论，构建精确的超额风险恒等式；通过UK Biobank 140个任务进行实证验证。

Result: 测量噪声以特征可靠性的k次方衰减k阶交互作用；在典型生物医学测量可靠性下，非线性优势消失；更大样本量和更灵活模型无法恢复被噪声抹除的非线性结构；三个条件（测量可靠性、样本量、特征表示）必须同时满足灵活模型才能胜出。

Conclusion: 生物医学中线性与灵活模型之间的平局不能作为判断底层生物学是否非线性的依据；非线性被噪声隐藏而非不存在；改进测量手段是恢复非线性优势的唯一途径。

Abstract: On biomedical tabular data, flexible models such as deep networks, gradient-boosted trees, and kernel methods are repeatedly matched or beaten by linear and logistic regression given the same features. The usual reaction is to treat this as a model-side shortfall, to be fixed with more data, a better architecture, or tuning, on the assumption that the nonlinear structure is there and the model has failed to capture it. We argue that these fixes cannot help when the binding limit is the measurement rather than the model, as it frequently is in biomedicine. Additive noise blurs the population-optimal predictor, and because blurring removes a function's fine, rapidly varying detail before its broad shape, it erases nonlinear structure faster than linear structure. A degree-$k$ interaction is attenuated by the $k$-th power of feature reliability, while the linear part is attenuated only once. At the reliabilities typical of biomedical measurement, the nonlinear advantage can vanish even when the underlying biology is strongly nonlinear, and what the noise removes cannot be recovered by a larger cohort or a more flexible model, only by better measurement. The nonlinearity is hidden, not absent, and a tie between linear and flexible models is not by itself a verdict on the biology. These pieces are classical, drawn from measurement-error statistics, psychometrics, and Gaussian analysis, and we assemble them into an exact excess-risk identity. Measurement reliability is one of three conditions, alongside sample size and feature representation, that must align for a flexible model to help, and together they leave only a narrow window that most biomedical tasks fall outside. Across 140 UK Biobank tasks, the gap between flexible and linear models, where it exists, carries the predicted noise signature, and the three conditions can be separated by intervention but not by a benchmark alone.

</details>


### [166] [Quantum Annealing Enhanced Reinforcement Learning for Accurate Remaining Useful Lifetime Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18503)
*Manoranjan Gandhudi,Arunkumar V.,G. R. Anil,Gangadharan G. R*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子退火增强Q学习（QAQL）框架，将量子退火的采样行为与Q学习的序列决策相结合，通过将Q值更新编码为小规模QUBO问题并在D-Wave Advantage系统上求解，利用量子退火器返回的近优动作分布实现随机动作选择，从而避免非线性退化轨迹上的过早收敛。在NASA C-MAPSS和工业设备预测维护数据集上，QAQL在多项误差指标上显著优于经典和量子基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计退化模型无法捕捉真实系统的强非线性，数据驱动模型在高维非凸搜索空间中容易收敛到次优解，亟需一种能有效处理非线性退化轨迹且避免过早收敛的RUL估计方法。

Method: 将每个Q值更新编码为小规模QUBO问题，其基态对应贪心动作；利用量子退火器多次读取返回近优动作的分布，实现随机动作选择以提供探索；量子退火器嵌入在强化学习循环内部，而非事后附加。

Result: 在NASA C-MAPSS涡扇发动机数据集和设备群预测维护数据集上，QAQL在六项误差指标上均优于经典和量子基线方法，具有统计学显著性。

Conclusion: 量子退火不仅是理论上的优化器，而是可以在工业预测维护的强化学习回路中实际使用的优化工具。

Abstract: Remaining useful life (RUL) estimation is central to predictive maintenance, where an unplanned failure can cost far more than the asset itself. Statistical degradation models miss the strong nonlinearity of real systems, and data-driven models often converge to suboptimal solutions in high-dimensional, non-convex search spaces. We propose a Quantum Annealing enhanced Q-Learning (QAQL) framework that couples the sampling behaviour of quantum annealing with the sequential decision making of Q-learning. Each Q-value update is encoded as a small quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) whose ground state is the greedy action; rather than acting as a deterministic optimizer, the annealer returns a distribution over near-optimal actions across many reads, and this stochastic action selection supplies the exploration that curbs premature convergence on nonlinear degradation trajectories. The QUBO is solved on the D-Wave Advantage system using minor embedding, with the annealer woven into the reinforcement-learning loop rather than bolted on after training. We validate QAQL on two public benchmarks: the NASA C-MAPSS turbofan engine datasets and a device-fleet predictive maintenance dataset. Averaged over many independent runs and across six error metrics, QAQL outperforms the classical and quantum baselines considered in this study, with statistically significant improvements. The results indicate that quantum annealing is a usable, not merely theoretical, optimizer inside a reinforcement-learning loop for industrial predictive-maintenance applications.

</details>


### [167] [Concept Modulation Models: A Unified Framework for Identifiability and Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18509)
*Soheun Yi,Yizhou Lu,Chandler Squires,Pradeep Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出概念调制模型（CMMs），通过属性势（attribute potentials）统一了条件潜变量模型中的可辨识性与外推保证，将基于转移的可辨识性推广到条件设置，并建立了代数化的外推准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件潜变量模型的可辨识性和外推保证大多是模型特定的，缺乏统一的理论框架。非线性ICA、因果表示学习、扰动建模等领域虽有各自的分析，但相互独立，缺少跨模型的通用工具。

Method: 提出概念调制模型（CMMs），其结构为A→Λ→C→X，属性A选择调制器Λ，调制器诱导潜在概念法则C，概念生成观测特征X。通过属性势（log-density比值）将特征一致性转化为受CMM类约束的潜在概念转移，分离了通用的提升步骤与模型特定的刚性论证。

Result: 建立了基于属性势的代数化外推准则：当转移的属性势恒等式扩展到未见属性时，外推成立。该方法恢复了多个现有模型特定结论，并揭示了它们共享的基于势的证明对象。

Conclusion: CMMs提供了一个统一框架，将可辨识性与外推问题转化为属性势的代数条件，为条件潜变量模型的理论分析提供了通用工具。

Abstract: Reliable generalization in conditional latent variable models requires understanding both identifiability and extrapolation: how observed variation across attributes determines latent structure, and how that structure determines distributions at unseen attributes. However, existing identifiability and extrapolation guarantees are largely model-specific, with separate analyses in nonlinear ICA, causal representation learning, perturbation modeling, and related conditional latent variable models. We introduce concept modulation models (CMMs), an attribute-indexed class of conditional generative models with structure $A\to Λ\to C\to X$, where attributes select modulators, modulators induce latent concept laws, and concepts generate observed features. CMMs lift transition-based identifiability to conditional settings by showing that feature agreement on observed attributes induces a latent concept transition constrained by the CMM class. We express these constraints through attribute potentials, log-density ratios between attribute-conditioned concept laws, separating the generic lifting step from model-specific rigidity arguments. The same potentials control extrapolation: agreement at unseen attributes holds exactly when the transported attribute-potential identities extend to those attributes. This yields algebraic extrapolation criteria, identifies the common potential-based proof objects behind several existing identifiability and extrapolation results, and, when combined with the model-specific rigidity arguments in those works, recovers their stated conclusions.

</details>


### [168] [Effects of sparsity and superposition on loss in simple autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18538)
*Mriganka Basu Roy Chowdhury,Eric McLaughlin Weiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对神经网络中叠加现象（superposition）的数学基础进行了严格分析，针对幂激活函数在稀疏输入条件下给出了L2重建损失的上下界，验证了Elhage等人(2022)的部分实验发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络的 mechanistic interpretability（机制可解释性）面临多语义性（polysemanticity）难题，即单个神经元通常负责多个不同任务，妨碍了对网络功能的清晰解释。Elhage等人(2022)指出这源于叠加现象（superposition），但缺乏严格的数学证明。本文旨在为叠加现象的发生和最优性提供数学基础。

Method: 针对具有幂激活函数的神经网络，在稀疏输入条件下，分析L2重建损失的上界和下界，并在极稀疏条件下证明这些界是紧的（tight），从而严格验证Elhage等人(2022)的部分经验发现。

Result: 在极稀疏条件下，针对幂激活函数给出了L2重建损失的紧上下界，为解释叠加现象的最优性提供了严格数学依据。

Conclusion: 本文从数学上严格证明了神经网络中叠加现象的某些最优性特性，为机械可解释性中多语义性问题的理论理解提供了支撑，并提出了若干开放问题供后续研究。

Abstract: One of the major difficulties in the mechanistic interpretability of neural networks is the occurrence of polysemanticity, which suggests that each neuron is typically responsible for multiple different tasks, impeding a clean interpretation of their function. The seminal paper of Elhage et al. (2022) argues that this occurs due to superposition, a phenomenon where the neural network represents distinct features as non-orthogonal directions in a lower-dimensional space, a strategy that allows much greater compression of the data without sacrificing fidelity due to the feature sparsity of input vectors. Elhage et al. (2022) empirically validates these hypotheses in a rather natural and simple autoencoder with sparse inputs. The contribution of the present work is to analyze the mathematical basis for the occurrence and optimality of superposition, while rigorously corroborating some of their findings. In particular, we provide upper and lower bounds for the L2 reconstruction loss, tight in the very sparse regime, for power activation functions. A short list of interesting open problems are also included at the end.

</details>


### [169] [TS-Fault: Benchmarking Time Series Forecasters Against Structural Faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18539)
*Yuyang Zhao,Lian Xu,Hao Miao,Chenxi Liu,Hao Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TS-Fault基准，通过四类参数化故障场景评估时间序列预测模型的鲁棒性，发现干净数据准确率与鲁棒性负相关，机制级故障会彻底改变模型排名，且基础模型虽然干净数据表现最好但最脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSF模型评价仅依赖干净数据上的单一指标（如平均误差），隐含假设其能预测部署可靠性。但真实故障并非独立同分布噪声，而是具有时间形状、变量依赖断裂、状态转移伴随缺失、以及跨传感管道的因果传播等结构性事件。需要系统性的鲁棒性评估方法。

Method: 提出TS-Fault基准，沿两个正交轴（观测级vs机制级、单变量vs多变量）组织四类故障模式，通过统一重要性分数将故障注入最关键的预测窗口。采用配对干净/损坏协议，在6个数据集、21个模型、4种模式、5个难度级别上进行系统评估。

Result: (1)干净数据准确率与鲁棒性呈负相关；(2)观测级故障下排名保持，机制级故障下排名彻底重排；(3)所有灾难性故障均发生在机制级故障下，基础模型在干净数据上准确率最高但最脆弱。

Conclusion: TSF模型的鲁棒性不能从干净数据准确率推断，必须针对结构化故障模式进行专门测试；机制级故障对比观测级故障更具破坏性和区分力；基础模型的高准确率是以牺牲鲁棒性为代价的。

Abstract: Time series forecasting (TSF) underpins consequential decisions in energy, transportation, finance, and healthcare, yet TSF models are almost universally ranked by a single number (e.g., average error) on clean held-out data, under the implicit assumption that it predicts deployed reliability. However, real faults are not i.i.d noise but structured events with temporal shape, broken cross-variable dependencies, regime change coupled with missingness, and causal propagation across a sensing pipeline. Treating TSF robustness as a data-quality problem, we present TS-Fault, a benchmark that evaluates forecasting models under explicit, parameterized fault scenarios with controllable semantic difficulty. TS-Fault organizes recurring failures into four modes along two orthogonal axes (observation- vs mechanism-level; univariate vs multivariate) and injects each fault into the most prediction-critical window via a unified importance score. This design enables robustness to be tested against the structures models actually rely on, rather than reduced to generic noise sensitivity. We evaluate 21 models across 6 datasets, 4 modes, and 5 difficulty levels under a paired clean/corrupt protocol. The results reveal three findings that contradict common leaderboard intuition: (i) clean-data accuracy anti-correlates with robustness; (ii) clean rankings are preserved under observation-level faults but reshuffled under mechanism-level faults; and (iii) all catastrophic failures occur under mechanism-level faults, with foundation models achieving the highest clean-data accuracy yet exhibiting the greatest fragility. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Ray-zyy/TS-Fault.

</details>


### [170] [Breaking the Solver Bottleneck: Training Task Generators at the Learnable Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18284)
*Lorenz Wolf,Connor Watts,Roger Creus Castanyer,Geoffrey Bradway,Maxwill Lin,Augustine N. Mavor-Parker,Matthew Daborn-Sargent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PROPEL提出了一种求解器摊销框架，通过轻量级激活探针预测目标求解器的通过率，从而在不执行求解器rollout的情况下高效训练任务生成器，使其生成位于可学习前沿的任务。在数学、代码和软件工程任务中，该方法显著提升了处于目标求解率水平的任务比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习训练智能体的瓶颈已从计算资源转向前沿任务供给——即有效、可解且难度恰好适应当前模型的任务。固定任务分布会随模型提升而饱和，而朴素合成生成的任务往往琐碎、不可解或病态。使用RL训练任务生成器以优化任务的有效性和可学习性虽可解决该瓶颈，但直接优化需要每个候选任务执行求解器rollout，对于软件工程任务一次rollout需数十分钟，求解器在环的生成器训练难以实现。

Method: PROPEL框架包含三个阶段：(1) 使用冻结的生成器参考模型生成任务，并对每个任务执行一次求解器rollout以构建标注语料库；(2) 在该语料库上训练一个轻量级激活探针（lightweight activation probe），该探针从生成器参考模型的隐层激活中预测目标求解器的通过率；(3) 在生成器优化阶段，将探针作为求解率的代理，将生成器评估简化为单次前向传播，从而避免昂贵的求解器rollout。

Result: 在编码任务中，处于可学习前沿（目标求解率水平）的生成任务比例：Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct求解器从10.1%提升至20.0%，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct求解器从5.3%提升至12.6%。在软件工程任务中，对于Qwen3.5-27B求解器，在训练探针和生成器时未见过的仓库上，目标求解率任务比例从9.8%提升至19.6%。

Conclusion: PROPEL通过求解器摊销策略，有效解决了任务生成器训练中求解器在环的计算瓶颈，能够将生成分布向目标求解率偏移，在数学、代码和软件工程任务上均取得了显著提升，且具备跨模型规模和跨领域的泛化能力。

Abstract: The limiting resource for training agents via reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly frontier task supply: valid, solvable tasks just difficult enough to train the current model. As reasoning and agentic models improve, fixed task distributions saturate, while naive synthetic generation yields tasks that are trivial, impossible, or ill-posed. Training a task generator with RL to optimize validity and learnability can address this bottleneck, but direct optimization requires repeated solver rollouts per candidate. For software-engineering (SWE) tasks, a single rollout can take tens of minutes; solver-in-the-loop generator training is intractable. We introduce PROPEL, a solver-amortized framework for training task generators at the targeted solve rate. PROPEL trains a lightweight activation probe on a one-time labeled corpus of generated tasks and solver outcomes. The probe predicts target-solver pass rate from a frozen generator reference model and serves as a proxy for solve rate during generator optimization, reducing generator evaluation to a single forward pass. Across math, code, and software-engineering at multiple model scales, PROPEL shifts generation toward the targeted solve rate: for coding, tasks generated at the learnable frontier increase from $10.1\% \rightarrow 20.0\%$ for a Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct solver and from $5.3\% \rightarrow 12.6\%$ for a Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct solver. For SWE, PROPEL increases the share of generations at the targeted solve rate from $9.8\% \rightarrow 19.6\%$ for Qwen3.5-27B on repositories not seen during training of probe and generator.

</details>


### [171] [Online Distributional Prediction via Latent Cluster Geometry Under Drift and Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18778)
*Navyansh Mahla,Prateek Chanda,Ganesh Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在线分布预测方法，通过潜聚类几何（变长中心配置）表示候选分布，利用Gibbs拟后验和可逆跳跃MCMC进行在线预测，并在非平稳流（含漂移和对抗性破坏）下实现了关于Wasserstein-1遗憾的理论保证。通过重启变体处理漂移导致的记忆过期，遗憾上界分解为PAC-Bayesian复杂度项、破坏敏感的后验扰动项和动态最优传输项，在若干合理条件下达到亚线性累积遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多非平稳流在线学习任务需要预测完整的数据生成分布而非仅跟踪点估计，但现有方法缺乏对分布级预测的理论保证，尤其是在同时存在漂移和对抗性破坏的场景下。

Method: 使用潜聚类几何（变长中心配置）表示候选分布，通过Gibbs拟后验进行后验平均得到在线预测器，利用可逆跳跃MCMC采样变维后验。针对漂移问题提出重启变体（时间局部化拟贝叶斯更新），理论分析通过分解遗憾为三项进行高概率界。

Result: 在有限支撑、稳定潜几何、预测映射正则性、预言可实现性、局部化重启窗口、亚线性传输作用和亚线性破坏预算等条件下，重启预测器实现亚线性累积Wasserstein遗憾。无需对数据流、漂移机制或破坏过程指定参数模型。

Conclusion: 该方法在非结构化非平稳流中实现了分布级在线预测的理论可保证性，潜聚类几何提供了结构化的不确定性与正则化空间，重启策略有效缓解了漂移带来的记忆过时问题。

Abstract: Online learning in non-stationary streams is often formulated as tracking a point estimate, but many applications require predicting the full data-generating distribution. We study online distributional prediction under drift and adversarial corruption. Our approach represents each candidate law through a latent cluster geometry: a variable-size configuration of centers that organizes probability mass and induces a predictive distribution. A Gibbs quasi-posterior over these configurations yields an online predictor by posterior averaging, and the resulting variable-dimensional posterior can be sampled with reversible-jump MCMC. The method therefore avoids specifying a parametric streaming law while retaining a structured latent space for uncertainty, regularization, and comparison.
  We evaluate performance by cumulative Wasserstein-1 regret against the time-varying true law. The analysis separates two effects: corruption perturbs the loss-based posterior update, whereas drift makes long-horizon posterior memory stale. We address the latter with a restarted variant that temporally localizes the same quasi-Bayesian update. The resulting high-probability bounds decompose into a PAC-Bayesian complexity term, a corruption-sensitive posterior perturbation term, and a dynamic optimal-transport term driven by \(A_T^{\mathrm{OT}}=\sum_{t=2}^T W_2^2(p_{t-1}^*,p_t^*)\). Under bounded support, stable latent geometry, predictive-map regularity, oracle realizability, localized restart windows, sublinear transport action, and sublinear corruption budget, the restarted predictor achieves sublinear cumulative Wasserstein regret. These guarantees require no parametric model for the stream, drift mechanism, or corruption process.

</details>


### [172] [A Link between Shock-wave Theory and Symmetry-reduced Stochastic Gradient Descent for Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18303)
*Taiki Miyagawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了冲击波理论与随机梯度下降对称商化学习动力学之间的数学显式联系，通过微分几何、李群理论和流体力学工具，证明在商流形上有效动力学满足粘性Hamilton-Jacobi方程，且损失函数梯度服从Burgers型方程，可严格证明冲击波形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习理论难以解释训练过程中的相变现象，且参数对称性导致的标准观测指标（如参数范数）具有误导性，亟需建立一种几何上自洽的理论框架来揭示对称商化学习动力学的本质行为。

Method: 结合微分几何、李群理论和流体力学，通过对参数对称性进行商化处理并施加局部熵粗粒化，推导出商流形上的有效动力学方程；利用梯度场假设将原始参数动力学投影到商空间，得到Burgers型方程。

Result: 证明了多层感知机、卷积神经网络、Transformer和平均场网络均服从Hamilton-Jacobi或Burgers型方程；指出对称校正的商可观测量为监测、预测和控制训练阶段相变提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 该框架为深度学习训练动力学提供了严格的数学刻画，对称商化视角能够消除参数冗余的误导效应，有望发展出实用的训练诊断工具。

Abstract: We develop a mathematically explicit link between shock-wave theory and the symmetry-quotiented learning dynamics of stochastic gradient descent, drawing on differential geometry, Lie group theory, and fluid mechanics. Specifically, after quotienting parameter symmetries and applying local-entropy coarse-graining, the effective dynamics satisfy a viscous Hamilton--Jacobi equation on the quotient manifold. Moreover, under the assumption that the raw parameter dynamics can be summarized by a gradient field on the quotiented space, the gradient of the coarse-grained loss function obeys a Burgers-type equation, and shock formation can be established rigorously. We apply our theory to multilayer perceptrons, convolutional neural networks, Transformers, and mean-field networks, and show that they obey the Hamilton--Jacobi or Burgers-type equations. We conjecture that this framework also yields practical diagnostics for deep learning. In architectures such as Transformers, raw parameter norms are often distorted by symmetry redundancy and may therefore be misleading, whereas symmetry-corrected quotient observables provide a principled basis for monitoring, forecasting, and controlling training-phase transitions.

</details>


### [173] [Strategic Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18867)
*Jivat Neet Kaur,Pratik Patil,Divya Shanmugam,Emma Pierson,Michael I. Jordan,Nika Haghtalab,Meena Jagadeesan,Ahmed Alaa,Serena Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在算法预测影响资源分配时，如何通过特征选择和岭正则化来应对策略性操纵。主要发现是仅基于可操纵性排除特征通常次优，并提出了联合选择特征集和正则化水平的实用算法，在医疗支付基准案例中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险领域（如医疗保健）中，算法预测结果会影响资源分配，而输入特征可能被策略性操纵。现有方法通常要求重新设计预测器以显式考虑策略交互，但在实际中决策者只能调整现有预测流程中的粗粒度杠杆（如选择特征子集和正则化）。因此需要对此类约束下的策略分类问题建立形式化理论指导。

Method: 从理论上刻画了在最优正则化条件下，不同特征子集的性能特征；提出了联合优化特征选择与岭正则化水平的实用算法；在医疗支付基准数据集上进行了案例研究验证。

Result: 单独基于特征可操纵性排除特征通常不是最优策略；提供了特征子集在最优正则化下性能的精细刻画；提出的联合选择算法在实际案例中有效指导了粗粒度策略杠杆的设计。

Conclusion: 该工作为缓解算法决策系统中策略性行为的影响提供了原则性且实用的框架，特别适用于决策者只能调整现有预测管道中粗粒度杠杆的现实场景。

Abstract: When algorithmic predictors inform resource allocation in high-stakes domains such as healthcare, these predictors must account for strategic manipulation of input features. The typical solution is to redesign the predictor itself to explicitly account for strategic interactions. In practice, however, decision makers are often constrained to adjusting coarser levers within existing prediction pipelines. For example, healthcare organizations often select which features to exclude based on perceived manipulability, while using standard regularization procedures to shrink the coefficients of retained features. In this work, we initiate a formal study of strategic classification through feature selection and its interaction with ridge regularization. Our main finding is that excluding individual features based on their manipulability alone is generally suboptimal. We provide a fine-grained characterization of the performance of a feature subset under optimal regularization, yielding new insights for policy design. Motivated by this characterization, we develop a practical algorithm for jointly choosing the feature set and the level of ridge regularization. Through a real-world case study on a healthcare payments benchmark, we illustrate how our algorithm can guide the design of coarse policy levers in practice. Our results provide a principled, practical framework for mitigating the effects of strategic behavior in algorithmic decision-making systems.

</details>


### [174] [Attribution-Guided and Coverage-Maximized Pruning for Structural MoE Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18304)
*Yifu Ding,Jiacheng Wang,Ge Yang,Yongcheng Jing,Jinyang Guo,Xianglong Liu,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对MoE模型的结构化剪枝框架，通过将剪枝比率分配建模为通道分数覆盖率最大化问题，并基于归因近似高效求解，在50%或25%结构化剪枝结合4-bit量化下保持模型精度，在Qwen3-30B-A3B上实现5.27倍内存压缩且超越SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE压缩方法仅在专家层面进行粗粒度剪枝（移除整个专家或基于粗粒度重要性分数排序），无法捕捉专家内部的细粒度冗余，导致剪枝预算分配不当和压缩效果有限。

Method: 1) 观察到MoE专家内部信息高度集中于少量通道，存在大量冗余；2) 将剪枝比率分配问题形式化为通道分数覆盖率最大化问题；3) 使用基于归因的近似方法高效求解该问题。

Result: 在DeepSeek和Qwen MoE模型上，50%或25%结构化剪枝结合4-bit量化下保持模型精度；在Qwen3-30B-A3B上内存减少5.27倍，在多个基准上一致优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 提出的通道级结构化剪枝框架能有效利用MoE专家内部的细粒度冗余，实现更高效的模型压缩，优于粗粒度的专家级剪枝方法。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale compute efficiently, yet remain expensive to deploy due to their substantial memory footprint and inference overhead. Prior compression methods mainly operate at the expert level, either removing entire experts or ranking experts by coarse-grained importance scores. However, such expert-wise decisions are often too coarse to capture fine-grained redundancy, leading to misallocated pruning budgets and limited compression. To address this problem, we observe that information within MoE experts is highly concentrated in a small subset of channels, leaving substantial redundancy even in experts deemed important. Based on this observation, we propose a structural pruning framework tailored for MoE models. Our method reformulates prune-ratio allocation as a channel-score coverage maximization problem and solves it efficiently using an attribution-based approximation. Experiments on DeepSeek and Qwen MoE models show that our method preserves model accuracy under 50% or 25% structured pruning when combined with 4-bit quantization. On Qwen3-30B-A3B, our approach reduces memory footprint by 5.27$\times$ and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [175] [DRIFT: Refining Instruction Data via On-Policy Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18307)
*Zefan Wang,Lincheng Li,Tianyu Yu,Yuan Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRIFT利用基于同策略影响函数的数据归因方法，通过解决标准影响函数存在的近端差距和梯度范数偏差问题，有效提升大语言模型监督微调的性能上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据筛选方法在受限预算下加速训练表现优异，但难以提升模型能力上限。关键在于如何优化数据分布以选择最能提升最终模型性能的样本，而非仅保留性能保持的子集。

Method: 提出DRIFT框架：1) 使用模型同策略（on-policy）展开作为验证目标，以最小化参数近端差距；2) 基于轨迹正确性进行符号加权；3) 针对梯度范数偏差（梯度黑客问题）去偏影响分数，使少量验证查询作为可靠锚点来归因整个数据集。

Result: 在7B参数规模的指令微调和推理模型上，DRIFT持续提升了两种模型的能力上限，性能优于现有的数据筛选基线方法。

Conclusion: DRIFT通过解决标准影响函数的两个结构性问题（近端差距和梯度范数偏差），有效改进了数据归因质量，从而在监督微调中提升模型性能上限。

Abstract: Optimizing the training data distribution for Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) dictates the capability of Large Language Models (LLMs). While existing data curation methods excel at accelerating training under constrained budgets, they are less suited to elevating the capability upper bound. The challenge here is no longer to identify a smaller subset that preserves performance, but to refine the data distribution toward instances most capable of improving the final model. To address this problem, we explore instance-level data attribution using Influence Functions (IF). We identify that standard IF formulations struggle in this setting due to two structural limitations: a proximity gap caused by off-policy validation targets, and a severe bias towards gradient norm. We propose DRIFT (Data Refinement via On-Policy Influence Functions for Supervised Fine-Tuning). Instead of relying on external reference data, DRIFT utilizes the model's on-policy rollouts as validation targets, which empirically minimizes the parameter proximity gap and better aligns with the local neighborhood assumption of IF. It further applies signed weighting based on trajectory correctness and debiases influence scores against the gradient hacking issue, allowing a small set of validation queries to act as reliable anchors for attributing the full dataset. Experiments on 7B-parameter instruction and reasoning models show that DRIFT consistently raises the performance ceiling on both, outperforming existing data curation baselines.

</details>


### [176] [Smoothness-Based Derandomization of PAC-Bayes Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19105)
*Alexandre Lemire Paquin,Brahim Chaib-Draa,Philippe Giguère*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究平滑损失函数的PAC-Bayes去随机化方法，通过利用损失和预测器类的平滑性，获得确定性预测器的高概率泛化界。核心贡献是将从Gibbs预测器到后验均值确定性预测器的转换成本量化为Jensen间隙类的泛化差距，并通过Rademacher复杂度控制该类，得到涉及参数Jacobian和Hessian平坦度量的确定性预测器泛化界。该框架适用于有界和无界平滑损失函数，并在线性预测器和平滑神经网络上进行了特化。理论中出现的Jacobian和Hessian量启发了实用正则化器，针对BatchNorm网络通过折叠BatchNorm变换计算有效权重的正则化项。CIFAR-10上的实验展示了该正则化器在不同批量大小下的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PAC-Bayes方法通常针对随机预测器（Gibbs预测器）给出泛化界，但实际中更常用的是确定性预测器（如后验均值）。如何将随机预测器的泛化保证转移到确定性预测器上，并利用平滑性获得更紧的界，是本文要解决的核心问题。

Method: 1) 提出Jensen间隙类概念，将Gibbs预测器到后验均值确定性预测器的转换成本量化为该类的泛化差距。2) 通过Rademacher复杂度控制Jensen间隙类，得到涉及参数Jacobian和Hessian的平坦度量的泛化界。3) 针对BatchNorm网络，通过折叠BatchNorm变换到相邻仿射权重来计算有效权重的正则化项。

Result: 建立了确定性预测器的高概率泛化界，界中显式包含参数Jacobian和Hessian范数项（平坦度量）。在CIFAR-10上的实验表明，所提正则化器在不同批量大小下表现出不同的正则化效果。

Conclusion: 平滑性假设使得从随机预测器到确定性预测器的PAC-Bayes去随机化成为可能，所导出的平坦度正则化器在实践中有效，且与BatchNorm的兼容性通过折叠操作得到保证。

Abstract: We study PAC-Bayes derandomization for smooth loss functions. Our goal is to obtain generalization bounds that hold with high probability for deterministic predictors by exploiting smoothness properties of both the loss and the predictor class. We show that passing from the Gibbs predictor to the deterministic predictor at the posterior mean has a precise cost, given by the generalization gap of the Jensen gap class. We control this class through its Rademacher complexity, leading to bounds for deterministic predictors that involve flatness quantities expressed in terms of parameter Jacobians and Hessians of the score map.
